### [1] [$H\mathbb{Z}/4$ is not an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom Spectrum over the $2$-Complete Sphere Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19446)
*Mattie Ji*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Kitchloo遗留的例外情形：证明$H\mathbb{Z}/4$不是$2$-完备（进而$2$-局部）球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hopkins-Mahowald定理断言$H\mathbb{Z}/p$是$p$-完备球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱；Kitchloo将其推广到$H\mathbb{Z}/p^k$，但在$(p,k)=(2,2)$这一情形未能解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过反证/障碍论证，证明$H\mathbb{Z}/4$不可能是$2$-完备球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱；论证由GPT-5.6 Sol和GPT-6 Astra提供大量协助，初稿随后用Claude Fable 5.1审阅。

Result: $H\mathbb{Z}/4$不是$2$-完备球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱，因此也不是$2$-局部球谱上的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱，从而解决了Kitchloo定理的遗留情形。

Conclusion: Hopkins-Mahowald-Kitchloo关于$H\mathbb{Z}/p^k$的$\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom谱刻画在$(p,k)=(2,2)$处不成立，该情形被证明为真正例外。

Abstract: For any prime number $p$, the classic Hopkins-Mahowald theorem asserts that $H\mathbb{Z}/p$ is an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $p$-complete sphere spectrum. Kitchloo extended this result to show that $H\mathbb{Z}/p^k$ is an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $p$-local sphere spectrum, except for the case when $(p, k) = (2, 2)$. We solve the remaining case by showing that $H\mathbb{Z}/4$ is not an $\mathbb{E}_2$-Thom spectrum over the $2$-complete sphere spectrum, and consequently the $2$-local sphere spectrum. This work was developed with substantial assistance from GPT-5.6 Sol and GPT-6 Astra, and an early draft was subsequently reviewed using Claude Fable 5.1.

</details>


### [2] [New families of moment-angle manifolds diffeomorphic to connected sums of products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19945)
*Victoria Kovyrshina,Taras Panov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 证明了在特定条件下（3维星形单纯球、恰有两个互不相邻的缺失边；奇维邻接星形单纯球），moment-angle流形可微同胚于若干球面乘积的连通和，并给出非多胞Barnette与Brückner球对应流形的显式描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索moment-angle流形 $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}$ 的拓扑/微分结构，特别是其在单纯球为星形（含多胞）情形下的分解形式，以揭示流形与组合结构之间的关系，并刻画非多胞单纯球（如Barnette与Brückner球）对应的momemtn-angle流形。

Method: 对3维星形单纯球 $\mathcal K$（恰有两个互不相邻缺失边）证明其moment-angle流形 $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}$ 微分同胚于球面乘积的连通和，且其中一个连通和项为三个球面的乘积；对奇维邻接星形单纯球证明 $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}\cong M_1\#\cdots\# M_k$，其中每个 $M_i$ 是两个球面的乘积；利用上述结果对非多胞Barnette与Brückner球给出moment-angle流形的显式描述。

Result: 当 $\mathcal K$ 为3维星形（特别地多胞）单纯球且恰有两个互不相邻缺失边时，$\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}$ 微分同胚于球面乘积的连通和，其中一个连通和项是三个球面的乘积；对奇维邻接星形单纯球，$\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}\cong M_1\#\cdots\# M_k$，每个 $M_i$ 是两个球面的乘积；并显式描述了非多胞Barnette与Brückner球对应的moment-angle流形。

Conclusion: 在星形（含多胞）单纯球的特定组合条件下，moment-angle流形具有球面乘积连通和的微分同胚结构，并可推广到奇维邻接星形单纯球；该框架还可用于刻画非多胞Barnette与Brückner球对应的moment-angle流形。

Abstract: We prove that the moment-angle manifold $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K}$ is diffeomorphic to a connected sum of products of spheres when $\mathcal K$ is a starshaped (in particular, polytopal) 3-dimensional simplicial sphere with exactly two missing edges that are not adjacent to each other. One of the summands of the connected sum is a product of three spheres.
  For neighbourly starshaped simplicial spheres $\mathcal K$ of odd dimension, we prove the diffeomorphism $\mathcal Z_{\mathcal K} \cong M_1\#\cdots\# M_k$, where each $M_i$ is a product of two spheres. We give an explicit description of the moment-angle manifolds corresponding to non-polytopal Barnette and Brückner spheres.

</details>


### [3] [Representation stability of string links and manifold links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20470)
*Filipp Buryak*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了随着链环数量的增加，字符串链与流形链空间的有理上同调满足表示稳定性；两类上同调群在每一度上构成有限生成的FI^#-模，并给出了显式的生成度上界，同时将其推广到有理同伦群的生成界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究嵌入空间（字符串链、流形链）在同调/上同调层面随分量数增加的稳定现象，将FI-模等函子式稳定性框架从有限型构型空间推广到链环嵌入空间，从而统一刻画随链数增长的有理稳定性并给出定量生成界。

Method: 结合几何结构映射的构造、模浸入嵌入的毛图复形模型（hairy graph complex models），以及一个函子框架：该框架允许在遗忘对称群作用后建立生成界。

Result: 对字符串链（标准嵌入的不交并R^m→R^d，m≥1，d≥m+3）与流形链（固定嵌入闭m-流形到R^{d-2}的不交并，d≥3）两情形，有理上同调群在每一度构成有限生成FI^#-模；第n度的生成元性（arity）至多为 n(d-2)/(d-m-2)。此外还得到有理同伦群显式的生成界。

Conclusion: 字符串链与流形链空间的有理上同调与有理同伦均具有表示稳定性，可通过FI^#-模的有限生成性及显式生成界刻画；所建立的几何-图复形-函子化方法为处理嵌入空间随分量数的稳定性提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We prove representation stability for rational cohomology of spaces of string links and manifold links in Euclidean space as the number of links grows. For string links we consider the component of the standard embedding of disjoint copies of $\mathbb{R}^{m}$ into $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, where $m \geq 1$ and $d \geq m+3$. For manifold links we consider the component determined by disjoint copies of a fixed embedding of a closed smooth $m$-manifold into $\mathbb{R}^{d-2}$, where $d \geq 3$. In both cases, the rational cohomology groups form finitely generated $\mathrm{FI}^{\#}$-modules in each degree with generation arity at most $n\frac{d-2}{d-m-2}$ in degree $n$. We also obtain explicit generation bounds for the rational homotopy groups. The proofs combine geometric constructions of structure maps, hairy graph complex models for embeddings modulo immersions and a functorial framework that allows generation bounds to be established after forgetting the symmetric group actions.

</details>

### [4] [Deep Learning for Singular PDEs: A Weighted Neural Network Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19335)
*Badr Oulgiht*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度学习框架，用于数值求解带齐次Dirichlet边界条件与正性约束的弱奇异半线性椭圆方程，通过硬约束嵌入边界条件、Softplus变换保持正性，并采用奇异性感知加权残差损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱奇异半线性椭圆方程在求解时面临困难：当正解在边界附近趋近于零时，非线性源项会变为无界，传统残差方法难以稳定训练与准确逼近。

Method: 方法包含三个组成部分：一是通过硬约束形式将Dirichlet边界条件直接嵌入神经网络表示；二是使用Softplus变换在计算域内部保持正性，确保奇异非线性项在训练过程中始终良定义；三是引入奇异性感知加权残差损失，对预测解较小、奇异行为更显著的区域进行加权强调。

Result: 在弱奇异情形下，通过数值参考解与受控验证测试对方法进行评估，并与标准残差形式进行比较，以考察逼近精度与收敛行为。数值实验展示了硬边界约束、正性保持与奇异性感知残差加权相结合用于弱奇异椭圆问题的潜力。

Conclusion: 将硬边界强制、正性保持和奇异性感知残差加权相结合，是处理弱奇异椭圆问题的一种有前景的深度学习途径，可提升近似精度与训练稳定性。

Abstract: We propose a deep learning framework for the numerical approximation of weakly singular semilinear elliptic equations subject to homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions and positivity constraints. These problems are challenging because the nonlinear source term becomes unbounded as the positive solution approaches zero near the boundary.
  The proposed approach combines three components. First, the Dirichlet boundary conditions are embedded directly into the neural representation through a hard-constraint formulation. Second, a Softplus transformation preserves positivity in the interior of the computational domain, ensuring that the singular nonlinear term remains well-defined during training. Third, a singularity-aware weighted residual loss emphasizes regions where the predicted solution becomes small and the singular behavior is more pronounced.
  The method is evaluated in the weak singularity regime through numerical reference solutions and controlled verification tests. Comparisons with the standard residual formulation are used to assess approximation accuracy and convergence behavior. The numerical experiments illustrate the potential of combining hard boundary enforcement, positivity preservation, and singularity-aware residual weighting for weakly singular elliptic problems.

</details>


### [5] [Wideband Directional $\mathcal{H}^2$-Matrix Compression for the Electric Field Integral Equation with Geometry-Adaptive Cluster Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19431)
*Joshua M. Tetzner,Simon B. Adrian*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种支持几何自适应聚类树的高效宽带方向性H²矩阵构造方法，用于电场积分方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方向性H²矩阵构造仅支持盒树（octree/box tree），难以适应几何或离散化与八叉树聚类不匹配的情形，因此需要扩展以支持几何自适应聚类树。

Method: 根据每个簇的电尺寸（而非盒树层级）确定方向数；使用球面Fibonacci点构造方向；通过角度最近邻映射建立父簇与子簇方向集之间的层次关系；采用非完全自适应交叉近似（incomplete adaptive cross approximation）构造嵌套方向性表示，并引入稳健化的树模仿主元选取策略（robustified tree-mimicry pivoting）以避免特定几何与网格导致的块结构矩阵过早收敛。

Result: 数值结果验证达到目标精度；存储量不超过基于八叉树的构造，且在几何或离散化与八叉树聚类不匹配时显著减少；对高频问题表现出预期的O(N log N)复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提方法将宽带方向性H²矩阵构造推广至几何自适应聚类树，在保持精度与复杂度的同时改善了存储效率。

Abstract: We present an efficient wideband construction of directional $\mathcal{H}^2$-matrices for the electrical field integral equation that, in contrast to existing constructions, supports not only box trees but also geometry-adaptive cluster trees. To accommodate geometry-adaptive cluster trees, we determine the number of directions from the electrical size of each cluster (instead of the level of a box tree), construct the directions using Spherical-Fibonacci points, and establish a hierarchy between the direction sets of a cluster and its children through an angular nearest-neighbor mapping. We construct the nested directional representation using the incomplete adaptive cross approximation, for which we introduce a robustified tree-mimicry pivoting strategy that prevents premature convergence for block-structured matrices arising from certain geometries and meshes. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves the desired accuracy, requires no more storage than the octree-based construction and substantially less when the geometry or discretization is poorly matched to octree clustering, and exhibits the expected $\mathcal{O}(N\log N)$ scaling for high-frequency problems.

</details>

*Xuehai Huang,Zheqian Tang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了应变梯度弹性(SGE)模型的内罚非协调有限元方法，采用由无散度面泡函数加性的向量值二次多项式构造的H¹非协调位移元，并在任意维数下建立最优且对Lamé系数λ和尺寸参数ι稳健的误差估计，进而导出二维和三维非协调Stokes复形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应变梯度弹性模型需在位移的一阶和二阶导数上引入H¹正则性及额外连续性约束，传统协调元构造复杂且自由度代价高；同时，该模型的误差估计还需对Lamé系数λ和尺寸参数ι稳健，避免锁定或病态现象。

Method: 采用内罚非协调有限元离散SGE模型：使用由向量值二次多项式加性无散度面泡函数构造的H¹非协调位移元，在任意维数下进行格式设计；借助底层散度交换(divergence-commuting)结构，构造二维和三维非协调有限元Stokes复形。

Result: 建立了关于Lamé系数λ和尺寸参数ι一致稳健的最优误差估计；由散度交换结构推导出二维、三维非协调有限元Stokes复形；数值实验验证了理论收敛阶与参数稳健性，并通过单轴拉伸圆孔基准算例展示了方法在多连通域上的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提内罚非协调有限元方法为SGE模型提供了一种有效、稳健且适用于任意维数与多连通域的离散方案，并揭示了与Stokes复形之间的离散结构联系。

Abstract: An interior penalty nonconforming finite element method is developed for a strain gradient elasticity (SGE) model in arbitrary dimensions, using an $H^1$-nonconforming displacement element built from vector-valued quadratic polynomials enriched by divergence-free face bubbles. Rigorous analysis establishes optimal and robust error estimates with respect to both the Lamé coefficient $λ$ and the size parameter $ι$. The underlying divergence-commuting structure further leads to nonconforming finite element Stokes complexes in two and three dimensions. Numerical experiments support the predicted convergence behavior and parameter robustness, while a circular-hole benchmark under uniaxial tension illustrates the applicability of the method to a multiply connected domain.

</details>


### [7] [A Fictitious Domain Formulation with Bubble Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19836)
*Stefano Berrone,Lorenzo Neva,Stefano Scialò,Fabio Vicini*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文放宽了虚拟区域法中Lagrange乘子施加边界条件时对两套独立均匀网格及固定网格尺寸比的依赖，直接由背景三角剖分的迹构造边界网格，并通过在解空间中加入泡函数恢复一致离散inf-sup条件，从而建立稳定性并推出最优先验误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于Lagrange乘子的虚拟区域法需要区域与边界两套独立均匀网格，且网格尺寸须满足预设比例，限制了方法的灵活性。作者希望放宽这一网格假设。

Method: 将复杂区域嵌入非匹配背景网格，用Lagrange乘子弱施加边界条件；直接由背景三角剖分的迹构造边界网格；在离散解空间中加入泡函数以恢复一致离散inf-sup条件；构造合适的算子证明该条件。

Result: 构造出可用于证明一致离散inf-sup条件的算子，建立了相应格式的稳定性，并据此导出最优先验误差估计，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 通过由背景剖分迹构造边界网格并引入泡函数富集，可在更弱的网格假设下保证虚拟区域格式的稳定性与最优收敛性。

Abstract: The fictitious domain method offers a powerful alternative to circumvent complex mesh generation process by embedding the complex physical domain into a simpler, non-matching background mesh. Among the various strategies to enforce the boundary condition on such a mesh, one possibility is to impose it weakly through a Lagrange multiplier. Existing approaches following this strategy typically require two independent uniform meshes for the domain and the boundary, whose mesh sizes are related by a prescribed ratio. In this work, we relax this requirement by constructing the boundary mesh directly from the trace of the background triangulation. To recover the uniform discrete inf-sup condition under this weaker mesh assumption, we enrich the discrete space of the solution with bubble functions. Our main result is the construction of a suitable operator that allows us to prove this uniform discrete inf-sup condition, thereby establishing the stability of the resulting scheme. As a consequence, we derive optimal a priori error estimates and provide a numerical experiment to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [8] [Entropy-Based Local Characteristic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19838)
*Shaoshuai Chu,Michael Herty,Alexander Kurganov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于熵的局部特征分解（ELCD），通过在相空间中选取局部熵最小的候选状态作为界面平均态来线性化通量雅可比矩阵，并将其嵌入二阶有限体积与五阶有限差分格式；二维欧拉方程数值实验表明，相比算术平均，ELCD 能更锐利地分辨复杂波结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶数值方法求解双曲守恒律时会出现伪振荡，局部特征分解（LCD）是常用抑制手段，但其效果依赖于界面平均态的代表性。传统做法采用相邻网格值的算术平均或 Roe 型平均，这类平均未必能准确刻画界面处的局部波结构，从而影响振荡抑制与间断分辨能力。作者希望找到一种更具物理意义的界面平均态选取方式，以改善复杂波结构的解析质量。

Method: 提出熵基局部特征分解（ELCD）：在构造界面平均态时，不仅考虑界面两侧相邻单元的状态，还引入相空间中若干邻近状态作为候选；在这些候选状态中选择使局部熵最小者，作为该界面的平均态，用于对通量雅可比矩阵进行线性化，进而完成特征分解。作者将 ELCD 集成到多种二阶有限体积格式和五阶有限差分格式中，并以二维气体动力学欧拉方程为测试对象开展数值实验，与使用算术平均的对应格式进行对比。

Result: 二维欧拉方程的数值实验显示，采用 ELCD 的二阶有限体积与五阶有限差分格式在分辨复杂波结构方面总体优于使用算术平均的对应格式，表现为波结构更锐利、伪振荡得到更有效抑制。也就是说，熵最小化准则所选取的界面平均态比传统算术平均更能反映局部流动特征。

Conclusion: ELCD 为 LCD 提供了一种基于熵准则的界面平均态选取策略，可作为一种通用模块嵌入不同精度的有限体积与有限差分格式中。数值结果验证了其在复杂波结构分辨上的优势，表明以局部熵最小为准则构造界面平均态是抑制伪振荡、提高高阶格式分辨率的有效途径。

Abstract: Local characteristic decomposition (LCD) is widely used in high-order numerical methods for hyperbolic systems of conservation laws to reduce spurious oscillations appearing in the computed solutions. The LCD implementation requires a representative average interface state, typically obtained using arithmetic or Roe-type averages of the nearly grid values. We propose an entropy-based LCD (ELCD), in which the states in the two cells adjacent to an interface are considered together with several nearby states in phase space. Among these candidates, we select the state that locally minimizes the entropy and use it as an average interface state for linearizing the flux Jacobian. We incorporate the ELCD into several second-order finite-volume and fifth-order finite-difference schemes. Numerical experiments for the two-dimensional Euler equations of gas dynamics show that the schemes, which utilize the ELCD procedure generally resolve complex wave structures more sharply than their counterparts, which use the arithmetic averages.

</details>


### [9] [Validating Hexahedra through their Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19926)
*Paul Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了三线性六面体网格元的雅可比行列式（jacdet）正值性可由边界上的正值性保证（Knupp猜想），并给出有限候选点上的判定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析中三线性六面体单元需保持正体积，即三线性映射保持正jacdet。通常需在单元整个体积内验证jacdet正值性，计算代价高；若只需在边界上验证将大幅简化。因此作者希望证明Knupp猜想并给出可行的判定方法。

Method: 理论证明方法：证明三线性六面体的jacdet正值性在边界成立即可保证内部成立；证明任意有效六面体的全局最小jacdet必位于边界；证明边界上全局最小jacdet（无论单元是否有效）必落在有限个候选点中，这些点可通过四次方程求根确定。最后结合上述结果构造算法，在有限边界点集上评估jacdet来验证六面体。

Abstract: Trilinear hexahedral mesh elements are used in finite element analysis to simulate volumetric phenomena. Realism of the simulation mandates that mesh elements through the trilinear map maintain positive volume, or mathematically, that the trilinear map maintains positive Jacobian determinant (jacdet). While jacdet positivity generally needs to be verified in the full element volume, we prove Knupp's conjecture which posits that for trilinear hexahedra, jacdet positivity on the boundary of the element is sufficient to guarantee positivity in the entire element. We further prove that for any valid hexahedron, its globally minimal jacdet must reside on the boundary. Lastly, we prove that the globally minimal jacdet on the boundary of a hexahedron, valid or not, must be achieved within a finite set of candidate points that can be determined by quartic root finding. Abstract: Topology optimization (TopOpt) is a standard tool for structural conceptual design, providing optimal structures with great design freedom. We employ the Empirical Interscale Finite Element Method (EIFEM) coupled with the Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (DEIM) to construct localized, parameter-dependent reduced operators. From an optimization perspective, this approach can be interpreted as a preconditioning strategy in which inexact gradients are used to accelerate convergence. Furthermore, parameterizing the design space in terms of explicit geometric features, such as inclusion radii or lattice widths, significantly improves the manu facturability of the resulting optimized designs. We assess the performance of the method in terms of computational time, design topology and structural performance with TopOpt as baseline for three differ ent unit cell geometries and three different benchmarks. Abstract: We derive exact butterfly-style factorizations for the Karhunen-Loeve transform (KLT) of a first-order autoregressive (AR(1)) source with known correlation coefficient rho. For even order N, the KLT matrix factors into a butterfly stage B_N, the fixed half-size DCT-II and DCT-IV cores CII_{N/2} and CIV_{N/2}, and two orthogonal correction factors Qs_{N/2} and Qa_{N/2}. These corrections are the eigenvector matrices of explicit diagonal plus rank-one matrices. In the limiting case rho -> 1, the corrections become identities, and the factorization reduces to the classic Chen-Smith-Fralick factorization of the DCT-II. For N = 4, it reduces to one butterfly and two plane rotations with closed-form angles tan(2 psi_s) = -2/(1 - rho) and tan(2 psi_a) = 2/(1 + rho). In the general case, the correction factors are instances of the central rank-one-update eigenproblem in divide-and-conquer eigensolvers; applying fast-multipole and hierarchical methods to them, we derive a factorization with overall complexity O(N log N), giving accuracy-controlled application of the exact KLT at large N. Signal-flow graphs of the resulting algorithms are provided.

</details>

Abstract: In this article, we propose two other DLRA-type dynamics for stochastic differential equations (SDEs) than the one studied in arXiv:2308.11581, derived from a minimization of functionals and (informally) from a Stratonovich formulation, respectively. Indeed, its derivation exploits the Stratonovich formulation to write stochastic processes on manifold, and, hence, possesses a term that depends on the geometry of the manifold itself. In the context of SDEs, the Dynamical Low-Rank Approximation (DLRA) already showed remarkable results, in terms of approximation and computational efficiency of computational time because of being completely computed ``on-the-fly".

Abstract: In this second article (Part II), we analyze the numerical algorithms for the Dynamical Low-Rank Approximation (DLRA) of Stochastic Differential Equations (SDEs) introduced in Part I arXiv:2601.21428 under the perspective of the stochastic discretization. Specifically, we employ a Monte Carlo method with $M$ samples to approximate the stochastic space and all the related quantities of interest. Consequently, these algorithms produce noisy interacting particle systems whose error analysis is not standard. Assuming high moments and subgaussian tails of the initial condition, in the case of elliptic diffusion, we provide convergence results for the DLR Projector Splitting for SDEs presented in Part I. When the fully discretized Gramian is of full rank for all the time evolution, then one observes a convergence rate close to the usual Monte Carlo one, i.e. $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{M}})$, for large $M$. If a regularization of this matrix is employed at each time-step, the rate is close to $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt[3]{M}})$ for large $M$. On the other hand, in case the regularization occurs only when the smallest singular value of the Gramian is smaller than a prescribed positive threshold, the stochastic convergence rate is located between the aforementioned results. Furthermore, in the case of general diffusion, we provide an easy-to-implement convergent algorithm. Concerning the DLR Euler-Maruyama and the DLR Projector Splitting for Euler-Maruyama (EM), these results are not straightforward to derive and a brief discussion on why this is a challenging task is provided, too. Numerical simulations will complete this analysis.

</details>

Abstract: This paper develops and analyzes an optimal-order fractional backward collocation method for adjoint Volterra integro-differential equations with weakly singular kernels. The backward Volterra structure, together with the weakly singular kernel, induces fractional-power singularities at the terminal endpoint, thereby reducing the classical regularity of the exact solution and causing order deterioration in standard polynomial collocation methods. We first establish a regularity result that characterizes the terminal singular behavior of the solution, showing that the solution is continuously differentiable, whereas its second derivative may exhibit a weak singularity at the terminal endpoint. Motivated by this regularity structure, we introduce a terminally graded mesh and construct a fractional backward collocation scheme whose local approximation space is adapted to the endpoint singularity. Rigorous convergence and superconvergence estimates are derived for both the solution and its derivative. With suitable choices of the fractional parameter and the mesh-grading exponent, the proposed method attains the optimal convergence orders dictated by the local approximation degree. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical predictions and demonstrate the accuracy and effectiveness of the method for adjoint weakly singular Volterra integro-differential equations.

Abstract: This work studies adaptive sparse-grid discontinuous Galerkin (DG) discretizations for the Bhatnagar--Gross--Krook (BGK) model, a kinetic equation posed in four- and six-dimensional phase-space. Standard DG methods are rendered impractical for the BGK model by the curse of dimensionality, motivating compressed representations that adapt to the solution in time. Using the adaptive sparse-grid DG method, we quantify accuracy and compression by comparing the adaptive degrees of freedom to full-grid DG methods and by assessing the resulting kinetic and fluid quantities in both fluid and rarefied regimes. Test cases include a relaxation problem, a multidimensional Sod shock tube, and shear/expansion flows used in prior low-rank BGK studies. To build an efficient Maxwellian evaluation without violating conservation, a central obstacle for structure-perserving BGK simulations, we introduce a hybrid interpolation strategy that exploits velocity separability to recover the correct discrete collision invariants and prove conservation of the resulting discrete collision operator on adaptive sparse grids. Our results show that the adaptive sparse-grid strategy can recover accurate and physically relevant solutions with sharp gradients, and the method reduces the active degrees of freedom by factors ranging from several-fold to several orders of magnitude, with the largest reductions occurring in the six-dimensional examples. All computations are performed with the open-source ASGarD adaptive sparse-grid DG library.

Various numerical examples and comparisons are provided to confirm the theoretical results.

Consequently, classical difference quotients of the solution operator generally fail to converge in $L^1$, even in one space dimension. Although generalized tangent-vector techniques have been developed for one-dimensional problems, extending these ideas to multiple space dimensions is considerably more challenging because it requires a geometric description of hypersurfaces of discontinuity.

From a handful of seeded founders the system grows into a spatially organized ecosystem of coexisting, competing and dominating species. Using Kustaanheimo--Stiefel regularization, we map negative-energy Kepler orbits to four-dimensional isotropic harmonic oscillators. The group composition law of the symplectic flow determines an integral kernel built from the classical two-point action and Van Vleck amplitude, whose infinitesimal generator satisfies an exact linear evolution equation---a property we term \textit{quadratic closure}. Imposing three geometric constraints---decay at infinity, regularity at the origin, and fiber invariance under the Hopf fibration---we recover the exact $E_n/E_1=1/n^2$ scaling and $g_n=n^2$ degeneracy. The phase scale $α$ sets the action unit; all spectral structural features are independent of its value. This framework offers a symmetry-transparent route to spectral data: for quadratic Hamiltonians, the classical kernel incurs zero semiclassical error even at ground-state energies, and the geometric constraints directly identify symmetry-protected degeneracies without diagonalization. We apply the method to shallow impurity states, moiré superlattices, and semiclassical transport, demonstrating how geometric screening rules simplify spectral analysis in condensed-matter environments.

Abstract: In our previous work we introduced the Reconstruction Dimension, an upper bound on the reconstructable degrees of freedom determined solely by the symmetry. In actual physical systems, however, the state quantities are generated through physical processes $P_o$ induced by the cause $O$. The state space is therefore often degenerate, and the upper bound determined by the representation-theoretic structure is not reached. This calls for a distinction between the upper bound determined solely by symmetry and the one realized after the physical process. We therefore introduce a three-level structure of upper bounds: the Structural, Effective, and Physical Reconstruction Dimensions (SRD, ERD, PRD), determined respectively by the representation-theoretic structure of the intermediate space, by the concrete design of the reconstruction map, and by the degeneracy of the state space under the physical process $P_o$. For each irreducible component they form the hierarchy $\mathrm{SRD}\ge\mathrm{ERD}\ge\mathrm{PRD}$, so that the two gaps identify at which level reconstructability is lost. The framework is illustrated through two contrasting systems: the orientation dynamics of flake-like particles, where the cause is a velocity gradinet, and the two-body problem, where the cause itself is defined through the symmetry. This paper provides a concrete framework for reconstruction in physical inverse problems.

They are governed under common physical boundary and initial conditions and are coupled through one cumulative energy and the associated field equations. They are not separate boundary-value problems and need not appear in a prescribed temporal sequence. The cumulative character of the framework concerns the response of the combined state to variation of externally prescribed conditions. For the special case of an initially perfect body, loss of stability may produce successive branches with defect and then source participation. Such a sequence is a bifurcation property of the selected initial state and constitutive energy, rather than a defining hierarchy of the framework. A one-dimensional specialization illustrates structural bifurcation, residual deformation and residual stress produced by retained structure, dependence on the initial combined state, directional memory under reverse loading, parametric cumulative hysteresis, and changes of observable tangent stiffness associated with structural stabilization. A spatial extension introduces defect and source gradient terms and yields a size-dependent structural stability operator. For the stable quartic energy considered, the gradient terms regularize nonuniform perturbations but do not by themselves generate a finite-wavelength localization instability on an already stable three-component branch. The examples thereby distinguish consequences of the cumulative structure and energy from phenomena requiring additional constitutive evolution laws.

Conclusion: 三种互补方法相互印证，表明对四次型(0, 1)与(1, 0) Dirac系综非对称相的刻画是自洽且可靠的，尤其在大N极限下具有良好的一致性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the asymmetric phases of the quartic type (0, 1) and (1, 0) Dirac en- sembles via three approaches: the Riemann-Hilbert approach, bootstrapping with positivity, and Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) simulations. The focus of this work is on asymmetric solutions to the Schwinger-Dyson and saddle point equations of these models, whose solution spaces prove deeply intricate. Via the Riemann-Hilbert approach, we are able to give explicit formulae for the eigenvalue distributions and free energy of various solutions. Using Hamiltonian Monte Carlo simulations, we are able to reconstruct the phase structure. Lastly, using bootstrapping with positivity, we are able to reconstruct the eigenvalue distribution of these models from their bootstrapped moments. All three methods show excellent agreement for a large matrix size.

</details>

Abstract: Ambient convergence does not determine a nonlocal energy on a cut body: a cut has zero volume, but a microscopic bond retains which labelled bank its path crosses. We give a direct measure-theoretic resolution of this information and prove the corresponding sharp-interface Gamma-limit. The cut may be a finite or countable family of Borel, countably rectifiable hypersurfaces with finite total surface mass, measurable approximate normals, and measurable binary bank data. No closedness, positive reach, finite atlas, curvature bound, angle condition, or regularity of the branch set is assumed. For heterogeneous Kac interactions generated by a uniformly spanning family of possibly noncommuting flows, the limit is the resolved anisotropic variation plus the outer boundary contact and one coefficient-one contact on each bank. The Gamma-functional contains no compulsory lower-dimensional term where a crack approaches the boundary; constant recovery creates no concentration there. The proof combines an exact spacetime area formula for flow crossings, a one-dimensional split-extension lower bound, and ordered-flow compactness. We also remove compact radial support. A finite first moment yields the same local Griffith functional; at the power-law threshold the limits separate into local, logarithmically renormalized local, and genuinely fractional regimes. Smooth slit and junction detectors are recovered as uniformly equivalent realizations of the direct crossing rule.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对固定宽度的矩形条带，结合均匀几乎可加性估计与乘积测度集中不等式，证明期望的free-to-fixed边界修正除以切向长度收敛，并给出沿切向区间的自平均。推导精确的有限体积Gaussian插值恒等式，证明一致指数边界混合条件蕴含具有显式指数速率的边界局域化。将切向压强定理推广到任意维数与有限一阶矩的对称耦合律，并在乘积集中假设下得到沿切向立方体的自平均。提出次临界开键判据，对稀疏符号耦合显式验证，得到低温局域化。

Result: 证明对每个有限逆温及零温，期望边界修正除切向长度收敛；由乘积测度集中得到切向区间自平均。指数边界混合条件=>边界局域化并给出显式指数速率。切向压强定理推广至所有维数与有限一阶矩对称耦合律，且在乘积集中假设下沿切向立方体自平均。次临界开键判据对稀疏符号耦合成立，可在不假设无限体积Gibbs态性质的情况下获得低温局域化。

Conclusion: 本文建立了无序Edwards–Anderson Ising模型边界自由能的收敛、自平均与局域化的一般性理论框架，将结果推广到高维、稀疏符号耦合与零温情形，局域化不依赖无限体积Gibbs态的假设。

Abstract: We study boundary free energies in disordered Edwards--Anderson Ising models. For rectangular strips of fixed width, we prove that the expected free-to-fixed boundary correction, divided by the tangential length, converges for every finite inverse temperature and at zero temperature. The proof combines a uniform almost-additivity estimate with product-measure concentration, yielding self-averaging along tangential intervals. We derive an exact finite-volume Gaussian interpolation identity and prove that a uniform exponential boundary-mixing condition implies boundary localization with an explicit exponential rate. The tangential pressure theorem extends to all dimensions and symmetric coupling laws with finite first moment; under a product-concentration hypothesis, self-averaging is obtained along tangential cubes. Finally, a subcritical open-bond criterion, verified explicitly for sparse signed couplings, gives low-temperature localization without assuming an infinite-volume Gibbs-state property.

</details>

Conclusion: Toda方程与电磁单极子之间存在结构性联系，Maxwell方程在一般分支上起分类作用，而Liouville分支与球面解分别给出纯磁场与单位电荷单极子，且结论不依赖于Einstein或自对偶假设。

Abstract: Every solution of the hyperbolic Toda equation determines a classical Abelian monopole on a (2+1)-dimensional Einstein--Weyl space. We determine when its connection is also a source-free electromagnetic potential on a curved Lorentzian 3+1 spacetime with a spacelike Killing field. On the generic branch, Maxwell's equation classifies the admissible Killing-fibre scale and horizontal twist through one characteristic of the Toda field. A separated Liouville branch gives purely magnetic fields with considerably freer fibre geometry. The round spherical solution yields a monopole of unit charge with conserved flux on a contracting screen. The background metric here is neither assumed to be Einstein nor self-dual.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对双调和亥姆霍兹方程中夹持障碍物的逆形状问题，近场算子无法直接满足因子分解法所需的对称因子分解条件，因此需要一种方法来恢复可分解结构以实现严格的障碍物表征。

Method: 引入一个与障碍物无关的远场变换，将近场算子扩展为满足对称因子分解的远场算子；基于该变换算子谱数据，结合因子分解法理论给出障碍物的完整表征，并设计实用重建算法；数值实验采用基本解法生成合成近场数据。

Result: 因子分解法理论实现了对障碍物的严格完整表征，所提重建算法有效；数值实验验证了方法的有效性；仅使用点源产生的散射场测量也能进行重建，展示出减少测量数据量的潜力。

Conclusion: 远场变换与因子分解法相结合可有效解决双调和亥姆霍兹方程中夹持障碍物的逆形状问题，且具备减少测量数据量的潜力。

Abstract: This paper considers an inverse shape problem for recovering an unknown impenetrable clamped obstacle in two dimensions from near-field point source and dipole measurements for the biharmonic Helmholtz equation in the frequency domain. The measured data consist of the scattered field and its normal derivative on a closed measurement curve surrounding the obstacle. Since the associated near-field operator does not directly admit the symmetric factorization required by the factorization method, we introduce a far-field transformation. This transformation is defined independently of the obstacle and augments the near-field operator into the associated far-field operator which does admit a symmetric factorization. This yields a rigorous complete characterization of the obstacle by the factorization method theory and leads to a practical reconstruction algorithm based on the spectral data of the transformed operator. Numerical experiments are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, with synthetic near-field data generated using the method of fundamental solutions. We also consider reconstructions using only scattered-field measurements generated by point sources, demonstrating the potential for reduced the amount of measured data.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用数学物理的分析风格，研究带约束球面积分在固定两向量范数及相互标量积条件下的大N渐近。利用非厄米变换R1、R2刻画离域区渐近行为；通过鞍点分析讨论向局域区的转变，该转变由移位矩阵的最大奇异值及相应左右奇异向量的重叠所控制。并结合厄米球面积分机制与库仑气体图像构造猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作将球面积分机制从厄米情形推广至非厄米随机矩阵，揭示了由R1、R2及最大奇异值—奇异向量重叠控制的离域—局域相变，并为单特征值大偏差与边界涨落提出可进一步验证的猜想。

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ asymptotics of a constrained spherical integral for non-Hermitian random matrices, in which the norms and mutual scalar product of two vectors are fixed. In the delocalized regime, the asymptotics are governed by the non-Hermitian transforms $\mathcal R_1$ and $\mathcal R_2$. At saddle-point level, the constrained integral exhibits a transition to a localized regime controlled by the largest singular value of a shifted matrix and by the overlap of its associated left and right singular vectors. Motivated by the Hermitian spherical-integral mechanism and by the Coulomb-gas picture, we formulate conjectures for one-eigenvalue large deviations and boundary fluctuations. Throughout, our analysis is carried out in the spirit of mathematical physics.

</details>

Abstract: We study the Anderson model on hyperbolic lattices. The graph is the Cartesian product of the Euclidean lattice with the regular hyperbolic square lattice having five squares at each vertex. We prove the existence of absolutely continuous spectrum and the absence of singular spectrum on a deterministic set of positive measure at weak disorder, and for $0<λ\le2$ when the single-site density is bounded below on $[-1,1]$. Truncated Cauchy distributions and controlled perturbations give purely absolutely continuous spectrum on intervals.

</details>

Estrada,Faris Hamdi,Samuel Kendig,Taeyoon Lee,Jose Oscar Mur-Miranda,Emma Sommers,Paul Titchener,Margaret Wang,Achu Wilson,Mark Yeatman,Osman Dogan Yirmibesoglu,Alfred A. Rizzi,Annan Mozeika,Nicolas Rojas,Lael Odhner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AthenaZero是一款双臂操作机器人，通过准直驱驱动与传动远程化设计降低惯量，实现接近人类的有效末端质量，并在投掷、接球、击球三类棒球任务中展示高速动态操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作器惯量大，难以在毫秒级时间尺度上实现高加速的动态操作；本文旨在最小化惯量同时不牺牲控制权限，使机器人具备接近人类的动态操作能力。

Method: 采用准直驱驱动（quasi-direct drive actuation）与传动远程化（transmission remotization）技术设计双臂操作器，降低有效末端质量并提升力矩透明性；随后在棒球启发的三类任务（投掷、接球、击球）中验证系统性能。

Conclusion: AthenaZero凭借低惯量、高力矩透明性与高加速能力，适用于需要人类级时间尺度的动态操作任务，并在多主体交互场景中表现出有效性与适应性。

Abstract: AthenaZero is a bimanual manipulator designed to minimize inertia without compromising control authority. By utilizing quasi-direct drive actuation and transmission remotization techniques, the system achieves an effective endpoint mass comparable to that of a human---about an order of magnitude less than conventional robot manipulators. This characteristic, combined with its inherent torque transparency, makes AthenaZero exceptionally well-suited for dynamic manipulation. We describe the methodology} that led to this design and demonstrate the robot's capabilities on three baseball-inspired tasks: throwing, catching, and batting, which showcase complex interactions on human-comparable timescales where milliseconds matter. AthenaZero was capable of throwing at speeds in excess of 30 m/s, with catching and batting at speeds in excess of 14 m/s over a short 7.3 m distance. Batting practice and a game of catch were subsequently performed in robot-to-robot and human-to-robot variations, showcasing the efficacy and adaptability of our system in tasks that require high acceleration.

</details>


### [41] [DITTO: Dexterous Interface for Transparent TeleOperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19196)
*Joaquin Palacios,Katelyn Lee,Cheng Zhang,Zhanpeng He,Matei Ciocarlie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DITTO 通过机械手与同构外骨骼的协同设计，将野外数据采集与带关节力反馈的双边遥操作统一到单一平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自由度手部操作数据采集困难：界面需同时捕捉丰富手部运动并呈现精确操作所需的接触交互。现有方法存在权衡——遥操作保证部署一致性但缺乏力反馈；手持（野外）系统提供自然的力透明性，却在部署时引入视觉具身差距。

Method: 提出 DITTO（Dexterous Interface for Transparent TeleOperation），基于解剖学信息对 7-DOF 灵巧机械手与运动学等价的电动外骨骼进行协同设计；外骨骼与机械手之间采用 1 对 1 执行器映射，从而在单一平台上统一支持手持（野外）数据采集与带关节级力反馈的双边遥操作。

Result: 验证了 DITTO 外骨骼可覆盖操作者食指到拇指的自然工作空间，并通过在接触丰富任务上学习到的策略展示了 DITTO 的灵巧操作能力。

Conclusion: DITTO 以同构硬件协同设计弥合数据采集与部署之间的差距，实现力透明且具身一致的高自由度手部操作数据采集与遥操作。

Abstract: Collecting data for manipulation with high-DOF hands is challenging, as interfaces must capture rich hand motion while rendering the contact interactions essential for precise manipulation. Existing data collection approaches face a trade-off: teleoperation ensures deployment consistency but lacks force feedback, while handheld (in-the-wild) systems provide natural force transparency but introduce a visual embodiment gap at deployment. We present DITTO, a Dexterous Interface for Transparent TeleOperation, which resolves this through the anatomically informed co-design of a dexterous 7-DOF robotic hand and a kinematically equivalent motorized exoskeleton. A 1-to-1 actuator mapping between the exoskeleton and robotic hand enables handheld (in-the-wild) data collection and bilateral teleoperation with joint-level force feedback unified in a single platform. We demonstrate that the DITTO exoskeleton spans the operator's natural index-to-thumb workspace, and showcase DITTO's dexterous capabilities via learned policies on contact-rich tasks.

</details>


### [42] [ULOHA: An Underwater Bimanual Robot System for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19200)
*Masato Kobayashi,Takeru Tsunoori*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ULOHA 是一个水下双臂机器人学习平台，结合定制主从硬件与 LeRobot 软件扩展，支持遥操作、多视角感知、示教采集、策略训练与自主部署；实验评估 ACT、Diffusion Policy 与 SmolVLA，并研究水下的气泡扰动、浮力驱动运动、动作执行时域与实时分块，另开展空-水跨介质策略迁移的单臂研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下视觉运动策略学习多集中于单机械臂，而双臂模仿学习主要在空气中研究，缺乏统一的水下双臂学习实验平台，也缺少对空气中学习方法与执行策略在水下适用性的系统考察。

Method: 构建 ULOHA 平台：定制-designed 主从（leader--follower）硬件，并在 LeRobot 上做软件扩展，集成遥操作、多视角传感、示教采集、策略训练与自主部署。在真机上演示双臂协调行为（臂间迁移、共享物体操作、浮力驱动拦截），评估 ACT、Diffusion Policy 与视觉-语言-动作模型 SmolVLA，并分析气泡扰动、浮力驱动物体运动、动作执行时域与实时 chunking；另设单臂实验考察空-水双向策略迁移。

Result: 真机实验展示了多种水下双臂协调行为，包括臂间迁移、共享物体操作与浮力驱动拦截；对 ACT、Diffusion Policy、SmolVLA 的评测揭示了空气中学习方法与执行策略在水下受气泡扰动、浮力运动、执行时域与实时分块影响的表现。单臂研究表明，在测试条件下，跨越空气与水的示教可支持两种介质中的执行。

Conclusion: ULOHA 为研究水下环境中感知与物理效应耦合下的双臂机器人学习提供了统一实验平台，并表明跨介质示教在特定条件下可实现空-水双向策略执行。

Abstract: Underwater visuomotor policy learning has focused primarily on single manipulators, while bimanual imitation learning has been studied largely in air. We present ULOHA, an underwater bimanual robot learning platform that combines custom-designed leader--follower hardware with software extensions to LeRobot, integrating teleoperation, multi-view sensing, demonstration collection, policy training, and autonomous deployment. Real-robot experiments demonstrate a range of coordinated underwater bimanual behaviors, including inter-arm transfer, shared-object manipulation, and buoyancy-driven interception. We evaluate ACT, Diffusion Policy, and the vision--language--action model SmolVLA on the platform. We investigate how learning methods and execution strategies developed for manipulation in air perform underwater, examining bubble disturbances, buoyancy-driven object motion, action-execution horizons, and real-time chunking. A separate single-arm study examines policy transfer between air and water and shows that demonstrations spanning both media support execution in both under the tested conditions. ULOHA provides a unified experimental platform for studying underwater bimanual robot learning under the coupled perceptual and physical effects of underwater environments. Additional material: https://mertcookimg.github.io/uloha/

</details>


### [43] [REACT: A Fully Spiking State-Space Model for Real-Time Event-Driven Temporal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19204)
*Geoffroy Keime,Nicolas Cuperlier,Benoit R. Cottereau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出REACT，一种全脉冲状态空间模型，逐事件处理原始事件流而不做时间累积，用于低延迟事件驱动时间感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的机器人视觉感知需随传感流持续演化。事件相机具备微秒级时间分辨率与异步感知能力，但多数学习方法将事件累积为帧或时间箱，引入积分延迟，限制快速反应能力。

Method: 提出REACT全脉冲状态空间模型，逐事件处理原始事件，不做时间累积。核心为复值脉冲神经元C-SiLIF，其连续时间动力学由事件间物理间隔驱动，内部状态以单个事件的时间分辨率演化。在手势识别与全视场事件流TTC估计任务上评估，无需目标边界框或定位输入。

Result: 在EvTTC上，REACT实现9.59%相对TTC误差，端到端推理延迟4.6 ms，距最佳学习方法仅0.15个百分点且无需目标先验。该延迟在数据集平均接近速度下对应车辆仅移动4 cm，而最快竞争学习方法为1 m。模型支持任意时刻TTC预测、零样本迁移至不同驾驶序列及INT8量化，将每32,768个事件的估计能耗从18.5 mJ降至2.8 mJ。

Conclusion: 事件驱动脉冲状态空间动力学可为反应式机器人系统提供低延迟、持续更新的时间感知能力。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in dynamic environments require visual perception that evolves continuously with the incoming sensory stream. Event cameras provide microsecond temporal resolution and asynchronous sensing, but most learning-based methods accumulate events into frames or temporal bins, introducing an integration delay that can limit fast reaction. Here we propose REACT, a fully spiking state-space model for event-driven temporal perception that processes raw events one by one, without temporal accumulation. REACT uses a complex-valued spiking neuron, C-SiLIF, whose continuous-time dynamics are driven by the physical inter-event interval, allowing its internal state to evolve at the temporal resolution of individual events. We evaluate REACT on gesture recognition and time-to-collision (TTC) estimation from full-field event streams, without a target bounding box or localization input. On EvTTC, REACT achieves a 9.59% relative TTC error with 4.6 ms end-to-end inference latency, within 0.15 percentage points of the best learned method while requiring no target prior. At the dataset's mean approach speed, this latency corresponds to only 4 cm of vehicle motion, compared with 1 m for the fastest competing learned method. REACT further supports anytime TTC prediction, zero-shot transfer to a different driving sequence, and INT8 quantization, reducing the estimated energy consumption from 18.5 to 2.8 mJ per 32,768 events. These results show that event-driven spiking state-space dynamics can provide low-latency, continuously updated temporal perception for reactive robotic systems.

</details>


### [44] [4D Radar Perception Algorithms for Autonomous Driving: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19216)
*Xumin Wu,Jun Zhou,Jilin Mei,Chen Min,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于4D毫米波雷达感知算法的综述，按感知任务与算法的演进脉络组织内容，从雷达基础、目标级感知延伸至动态场景理解，并总结了数据集与评测、挑战及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D毫米波雷达感知算法近年快速发展，研究范围从信号处理、目标检测扩展到语义分割、运动估计、占据预测与动态场景重建，领域内亟需按感知任务演进进行系统梳理，以厘清从稀疏目标感知到动态空间理解的研究脉络与实证支撑。

Method: 综述性研究，无单一方法。以感知任务与算法的演进为主线组织：先介绍雷达基础、数据表示与质量增强方法，再依次综述目标级感知、运动与定位、局部与稠密空间感知、动态场景理解；在各方向对比纯雷达学习、多模态融合、跨模态监督与知识蒸馏三类范式；重点关注高度（俯仰）、多普勒测量与雷达物理先验在各任务中的利用方式；并梳理现有数据集的覆盖任务、输入数据、标注与评测协议。

Result: 作为综述，其"结果"体现为系统的归纳与对比：给出按任务划分的研究版图，明确纯雷达学习、多模态融合、跨模态监督与知识蒸馏三类技术路线的差异，阐明高度、多普勒与物理先验的跨任务利用方式，并汇总数据集在任务覆盖、输入、标注与评测协议上的现状，从而澄清各研究方向的实证支撑。

Conclusion: 该综述提供了以任务为导向的视角，刻画了4D雷达感知从稀疏目标感知向动态空间理解的转变，并讨论了自动驾驶中4D雷达感知的共同挑战与未来方向。

Abstract: Research on 4D millimeter-wave radar perception algorithms has flourished in recent years, extending from signal processing and object detection to semantic segmentation, motion estimation, occupancy prediction, and dynamic scene reconstruction. This review organizes the field according to the evolution of perception tasks and algorithms. It first introduces radar fundamentals, data representations, and quality-enhancement methods, and then reviews object-level perception, motion and localization, local and dense spatial perception, and dynamic scene understanding. Across these directions, we compare radar-only learning, multimodal fusion, and cross-modal supervision and knowledge distillation. Particular attention is paid to how elevation, Doppler measurements, and radar physical priors are exploited across tasks. We further summarize the task coverage, input data, annotations, and evaluation protocols of existing datasets, clarifying the empirical support for different research directions. Finally, we discuss the common challenges and future directions of 4D radar perception for autonomous driving. This review provides a task-oriented perspective on the transition from sparse object perception to dynamic spatial understanding.

</details>


### [45] [Grasping by interconnection: robust closing motions from coarse object templates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19228)
*Julien Vanderheyden,Guillaume Drion,Fulvio Forni,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 设计基于四个原则的抓取运动规划器（虚拟模型控制实现），在无反馈条件下可容忍约1cm尺寸误差和数厘米/数十度位姿误差，鲁棒性可内建于闭合运动本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手抓取时物体形状、尺寸和位姿往往仅有近似已知；现有抓取规划器通常依赖精确物体模型或用反馈纠正误差，但闭合运动自身能容忍多大不准确性尚不明确。

Method: 基于四项原则设计运动规划器：物体的粗略模板、人类抓取类型、以物体为中心的交互、以及采用顺从滑动接触而非预设接触点；通过虚拟模型控制实现，并在Shadow Dexterous Hand上评估。

Result: 无反馈条件下，规划闭合运动可容忍约1cm尺寸误差及数厘米、数十度的位姿误差，在27种测试条件中的25种优于最先进的数据驱动规划器；成功抓取80个日常物体中的82.5%，并在自主流程中取得成功。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性可被设计进闭合运动本身而非仅依赖反馈；该规划器为不确定环境下的可靠操作提供了路径，后续将结合物理手上的自适应反馈控制进一步推进。

Abstract: Dexterous robot hands must often grasp objects whose shape, size, and pose are known only approximately. Grasp planners typically require accurate object models or correct errors with feedback, but how much inaccuracy a closing motion can tolerate on its own remains unclear. To address this question, we designed a motion planner based on four principles: a coarse template of the object, human grasp types, an object-centric interaction, and compliant, sliding contacts instead of prescribed contact points. This paper presents the planner, implemented through virtual model control, and its evaluation on a Shadow Dexterous Hand. Without feedback, the planned closing motions tolerated size errors of about 1cm and pose errors of several centimeters and tens of degrees, a wider range than a state-of-the-art data-driven planner in 25 of 27 tested conditions. They also grasped 82.5% of 80 everyday objects and succeeded within an autonomous pipeline. Robustness can thus be designed into the closing motion itself, rather than left only to feedback. This planner opens a path toward reliable manipulation in uncertain settings, which we will pursue by combining it with adaptive feedback control on the physical hand.

</details>


### [46] [Learning Safe Humanoid Navigation from Reduced Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19272)
*William D. Compton,Zachary Olkin,Ryan Bena,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出RoM-Nav，一种两阶段强化学习导航框架，先在降阶动力学上用完整3D LiDAR观察训练导航策略，再迁移到全阶人形动力学并冻结运动策略，配合Poisson安全滤波器，在Unitree G1上实现无地图多层导航（垂直位移超10m、路径超100m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单阶段强化学习导航流水线难以扩展到多层、多楼层地形，瓶颈在于楼梯等复杂人形地形交互。作者希望突破这一可扩展性限制。

Method: 将导航问题分解为两部分：首先在降阶动力学模型上，以完整3D LiDAR观测训练复杂多层地形导航策略；然后利用该导航知识初始化一个运行于全阶人形动力学、并冻结运动策略在环的策略。此外，对导航策略输出应用Poisson安全滤波器，以在分布外障碍下恢复安全性。

Result: Poisson安全滤波器能在不降低导航成功率的前提下，对分布外障碍恢复安全性。最终策略在Unitree G1上完成无地图多层导航，测试涵盖垂直位移超10m、路径长度超100m的场景。

Conclusion: 两阶段分解加安全滤波的RL导航框架可有效扩展到复杂多层人形地形，验证了方法在真实人形机器人上的可行性。

Abstract: Research in humanoid robotics has achieved rapid progress in locomotion, and recent results have pushed the boundary on autonomous navigation. We demonstrate that a standard single-stage RL navigation pipeline struggles to scale to multi-level and multi-story terrain, limited by the difficulty of complex humanoid terrain interactions such as stairs. To overcome this challenge, we decompose the navigation problem into two pieces. First, we train a policy operating on the reduced order dynamics but with full 3D LiDAR observations to navigate complex, multi-story terrain. We then utilize this navigation knowledge to kickstart a policy operating on the full-order humanoid dynamics, with a frozen locomotion policy in the loop. Additionally, we demonstrate that applying a Poisson safety filter to the navigation policy output recovers safety in the presence of out-of-distribution obstacles, without dropping navigation success rate. We demonstrate the resulting RoM-Nav policy on a Unitree G1, accomplishing mapless multi-floor navigation covering trials with over 10m of vertical displacement and over 100m of path length. Project page with videos https://wdc3iii.github.io/rom-nav/ .

</details>


### [47] [OHRID-Retail: An Open Multimodal Dataset of Human Activity in Retail Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19302)
*Xiangrui Wang,Yuetong Wu,Jalen Beeman,Robert Cook,Yu Gu,Nathanial Pearson,Trevor Smith,Read Hayes,Boyi Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发布 OHRID Retail 数据集：16 名健康成人在模拟货架拣选任务中，于无机器人、低速引导、高速引导三种条件下采集全身运动学（17 个 Xsens Awinda 惯性传感器）与 10 处表面肌电（Delsys Trigno）数据，开放原始记录与分析资源，支持人机交互、人体活动识别与职业生物力学等研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 描述人类与移动机器人在共享环境中的开放数据集仍然有限，尤其是融合移动、伸取、物体操作与机器人引导的零售场景数据缺失。该数据集旨在填补这一空白，为多模态人体行为建模与人机协作研究提供公开数据基础。

Method: 采用被试内设计，16 名健康成人执行模拟货架拣选任务，设置三种条件（无机器人、低速机器人引导、高速机器人引导），每条件每人完成 2 次试验。使用 17 个 Xsens Awinda 惯性传感器采集全身运动学，使用 Delsys Trigno 表面肌电在 10 个部位测量肌肉活动，并配套处理指标、文档与可复现分析资源。

Result: 描述性分析显示，全身运动强度与肌肉激活在不同机器人交互条件及不同身体部位之间存在差异；数据集提供公开可获取的原始记录、处理后指标与可复现分析资源。

Conclusion: OHRID Retail 可支持人体活动识别、多模态传感器融合、职业生物力学、工效学、人类感知机器人导航及零售等共享环境中的人机交互研究。

Abstract: Open datasets describing human behavior in environments shared with mobile robots remain limited, particularly for retail activities that combine locomotion, reaching, object handling, and robot guided movement. This paper introduces OHRID Retail, an open, human centered multimodal dataset collected from 16 healthy adults performing a simulated shelf picking task under three within participant conditions: no robot, low speed robot guidance, and high speed robot guidance. Each participant completed two trials per condition. Whole body kinematics were recorded using 17 Xsens Awinda inertial sensors and muscle activity was measured at 10 locations using Delsys Trigno surface electromyography sensors. Descriptive analyses demonstrate variation in whole body movement intensity and muscle activation across robot interaction conditions and body locations. OHRID Retail provides openly available raw recordings, processed measures, documentation, and reproducible analysis resources. The dataset can support research in human activity recognition, multimodal sensor fusion, occupational biomechanics, ergonomics, human aware robot navigation, and human robot interaction in retail and related shared environments.

</details>


### [48] [GAVEL: Graph World Models for Verified and Efficient Long-Horizon LLM Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19315)
*Ruiyang Wang,Hao-Lun Hsu,Swarajh Mehta,Jiwoo Kim,Zhihao Dou,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAVEL：基于显式图世界模型的长时程LLM规划验证与修复框架，在BEHAVIOR-1K上将单任务成功率从41.2%提升至91.8%，多任务成功率从19.9%提升至92.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽为长时程机器人规划提供灵活接口，但其生成的规划常无法遵守具身约束、难以从规划错误中恢复，且在部分可观测条件下推理能力不足。

Method: 构建显式图世界模型，编码物体关系、动作前置条件与效果、以及对未观测物体位置的概率信念；在执行前预测LLM动作后果、检测违规并直接依据世界模型修复可推导的错误；仅将需语义推理的错误交由LLM重新规划；对多任务指令，基于物体位置分布重排剩余子任务以最小化期望搜索成本。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K的100个单任务与500条多任务指令上评估：使用Qwen3-8B时，单任务成功率由41.2%提升至91.8%，多任务成功率由19.9%提升至92.6%；分布信念推理相较静态变体减少约5.4%的移动距离。

Conclusion: 显式图世界模型外壳能显著提升长时程具身规划的可靠性与效率，且对紧凑型与前沿托管LLM能力均适用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) provide a flexible interface for long-horizon robot planning, but generated plans often fail to respect embodiment constraints, recover from planning errors, or reason effectively under partial observability. We present GAVEL, a framework for verifying and repairing long-horizon LLM planning built around an explicit graph world model. The graph represents relevant object-relations, action pre-conditions and effects, and probabilistic beliefs over unobserved object locations. This model can predict the consequences of LLM-generated actions before execution, detect violations, and repair those whose corrections follow directly from the world model. This method also reserves LLM replanning solely for errors requiring semantic reasoning. For multi-task instructions, GAVEL reasons over distributions of possible object locations to reorder remaining subtasks and minimize expected search cost. We evaluate GAVEL on BEHAVIOR-1K across 100 single long-horizon tasks and 500 multi-task instructions. With Qwen3-8B, GAVEL improves single-task success from 41.2% to 91.8% and multi-task success from 19.9% to 92.6%. Distributional belief reasoning also reduces travel distance by approximately 5.4% compared with a static variant. These improvements show that an explicit graph world model harness can substantially improve the reliability and efficiency of long-horizon embodied planning across compact and frontier hosted LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [49] [A Morphing Aerial Robot With Thruster-Integrated Flexible Continuum Links for Shape Adaptive Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19328)
*Eri Sawada,Kazuki Sugihara,Ayano Miyamichi,Kunio Kojima,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带有柔性连续体连杆的变形转子分布式空中机器人，兼具高形状适应性与扩展的力旋量空间，并首次实现了集成推进器的柔性连杆空中操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操作是扩展空中机器人应用的关键。实现通用空中操作的两大方向为：(i) 利用软体机械臂获得高环境适应性；(ii) 通过将推进器沿机械臂分布来扩展可行力旋量空间。然而，此前没有空中机器人能同时满足这两项要求。

Method: 提出变形转子分布式空中机器人，采用柔性连续体连杆：连杆作为软体机械臂被动顺应环境形状，沿连杆分布的推进器扩展可行推力旋量空间，使末端执行器能施加较大交互力。为抑制轻质连续体连杆的振动，设计了兼具高扭转与垂向刚度的复合板簧结构，并开发了抑振控制方法。

Result: 通过上述实现，演示了稳定飞行以及多种空中操作任务。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个使用集成推进器的柔性连杆实现空中操作的工作，同时满足了高形状适应性与扩展力旋量空间两项需求。

Abstract: In recent years, aerial manipulation has attracted increasing attention as a key to expand the application of aerial robots. In this work, we focus on two major research directions for achieving versatile aerial manipulation: (i) acquiring high environmental adaptability using soft manipulators, and (ii) expanding the feasible wrench space by distributing thrusters along the manipulator. However, no aerial robot has simultaneously satisfied these two requirements. Therefore, in this paper, we propose a morphing rotor-distributed aerial robot with flexible continuum links that achieves both high shape adaptability and an expanded wrench space. The flexible continuum links function as soft manipulators, passively conforming to the shape of the environment, while the thrusters distributed along the continuum links expand the feasible thrust wrench space and enable the end-effector to exert large interaction forces. To realize the proposed robot, it is essential to suppress vibrations of the lightweight continuum links. Thus, we develop a composite leaf-spring structure that provides both high torsional and vertical stiffness, and vibration-suppressing control methods. Using these implementations, we demonstrate stable flight and a variety of aerial manipulation tasks. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to realize aerial manipulations using flexible links with an integrated thruster.

</details>


### [50] [SemSafe-3DGS: Semantic Risk-Aware Active Navigation in Uncertain 3D Gaussian Splatting Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19330)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语义风险感知的安全主动感知框架，在带属性3D高斯地图中导航，通过类别相关风险权重调制AVaR碰撞裕度并聚合为控制障碍函数，与主动感知障碍统一于CBF-QP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全规划主要基于几何推理，语义不同但几何相似的场景元素会产生相近控制响应，忽略语义后果差异；且在部分可观测环境中需兼顾安全与信息获取。

Method: 在带属性3D高斯地图中，用语义属性通过类别相关风险权重调制Average Value-at-Risk碰撞裕度模型，使安全关键高斯基元在复合障碍中权重更大；加权裕度聚合为控制障碍函数，并引入轨迹相关主动感知障碍以降低机器人预期运动方向上的地图不确定性；二者集成于统一CBF-QP，将语义风险感知避碰作为硬约束，安全或任务进度冲突时松弛信息获取。

Result: 实验表明：安全约束高效、主动感知提升导航性能、轨迹随语义自适应调整，并在Ackermann动力学实机上完成执行。

Conclusion: 语义风险可有效融入安全控制障碍框架，实现语义依赖的轨迹适应与安全-感知权衡，并具备实机可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in partially observed environments must navigate safely while acquiring observations that improve future planning. Existing safety formulations generally reason primarily about geometry. Consequently, geometrically similar scene elements may induce comparable control responses despite having different semantic consequences. We present a semantic risk aware safe-active perception framework for navigation in attributed 3D Gaussian maps. Semantic attributes modulate an Average Value-at-Risk collision clearance model through class dependent risk weights, allowing safety-critical Gaussian primitives to receive greater influence in the composite barrier. The resulting weighted clearances are aggregated into a control barrier function, while a trajectory-relevant active perception barrier promotes observations that reduce geometric map uncertainty along the robot's anticipated motion. Both objectives are integrated in a unified CBF-QP that enforces semantic risk-aware collision avoidance as a hard constraint while relaxing information acquisition when it conflicts with safety or task progress. Experiments demonstrate efficient safety constraint, improved navigation through active perception, semantic dependent trajectory adaptation, and real-robot execution under Ackermann dynamics.

</details>


### [51] [Dynamic-LIVO: A Dynamic-Aware LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry System Using Spatio-Temporal Normals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19336)
*Zhixin Zhang,Samuel Ahiwe,Matthew Hale,Liang Zhao,Pawel Ladosz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dynamic-LIVO，一种面向动态环境的动态感知LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计系统，实现鲁棒状态估计与静态彩色建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的运动物体会污染LiDAR与视觉观测，导致状态估计漂移和地图中出现动态残留（"鬼影"），影响建图质量与定位精度。

Method: 采用时空（S-T）法线分析识别动态LiDAR点，并将分类结果传播至LiDAR-惯性与视觉-惯性更新，阻断动态测量对状态估计与建图的影响；针对新观测与空间稀疏区域S-T法线估计不可靠的问题，引入时间延迟S-T法线估计策略，推迟对约束不足点的分类，待后续观测增加后再重新评估。

Result: 在公开数据集与自采数据集（多种传感器配置）上的大量实验表明，Dynamic-LIVO在复杂动态环境中提升了定位精度，并生成更干净的静态彩色地图。

Conclusion: Dynamic-LIVO通过时空法线分析与时间延迟估计策略，有效实现动态环境下的鲁棒状态估计与静态彩色建图，源代码与自采数据集将在录用后公开。

Abstract: This paper proposes Dynamic-LIVO, a dynamic-aware LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) system for robust state estimation and static colored mapping in dynamic environments. Dynamic-LIVO employs Spatio-Temporal (S-T) normal analysis to identify dynamic LiDAR points and propagates the resulting classification to both LiDAR-inertial and visual-inertial updates, preventing dynamic LiDAR measurements and their associated visual observations from affecting state estimation and mapping. However, S-T normal estimation can be unreliable in newly observed and spatially sparse regions due to insufficient spatio-temporal observations. To address this issue, we introduce a time-delayed S-T normal estimation strategy that defers the classification of insufficiently constrained points and re-evaluates them as additional observations become available. This strategy improves dynamic classification reliability while preserving valid static points for map construction. Extensive experiments on public and self-collected datasets with diverse sensor configurations demonstrate that Dynamic-LIVO improves localization accuracy and produces cleaner static colored maps in challenging dynamic environments. The source code and self-collected dataset will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [52] [ViLoMan: Learning Visual-Proprioceptive Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation Skills for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19340)
*Zejie Tian,Ruibing Hou,Bingpeng Ma,Börje F. Karlsson,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViLoMan是一个可扩展的自主人形全身移动操作框架，将部分人体交互演示转化为物理可行的机器人轨迹，并通过师生蒸馏学习直接从机载深度与本体感知映射到关节级全身动作的统一策略，在关门任务上实现仿真到现实的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形移动操作需要自适应的全身协调以融合移动与物理交互，但自主移动操作学习面临两大困难：一是缺乏多样且物理可执行的机器人-物体交互数据；二是难以直接从机载观测学习统一的全身控制策略。

Method: ViLoMan首先将人-物交互的部分运动学演示转化为完整、物理可执行的机器人轨迹；随后在师生蒸馏框架中利用这些轨迹，学习一个将第一人称深度观测与本体感知直接映射为关节级全身动作的统一策略。部署阶段无需参考运动或中间指令。

Result: 在多种门配置与机器人初始条件下的仿真和真实世界中评估关门任务。结果表明，单一策略使Unitree G1人形机器人仅依靠机载深度感知与本体感知完成完整任务，并在任务变化下稳健泛化，且有效实现从仿真到现实的迁移。

Conclusion: ViLoMan通过演示数据转化与师生蒸馏，实现了无需参考运动与中间指令的自主人形全身移动操作，并在仿真与真实场景中验证了其泛化性与sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires adaptive whole-body coordination to seamlessly integrate locomotion and physical interaction. Despite recent advances, learning autonomous loco-manipulation remains challenging due to the scarcity of diverse, physically executable robot-object interaction data and the difficulty of learning unified whole-body control directly from onboard observations. We present ViLoMan, a scalable framework for autonomous humanoid loco-manipulation. ViLoMan first transforms partial kinematic demonstrations of human-object interactions into complete, physically executable robot trajectories. It then leverages these trajectories within a teacher-student distillation framework to learn a unified policy that maps egocentric depth observations and proprioceptive measurements directly to joint-level whole-body actions. During deployment, the policy requires neither reference motions nor intermediate commands. We evaluate ViLoMan on door-closing tasks across diverse door configurations and robot initial conditions in both simulation and the real world. Experimental results demonstrate that a single policy enables a Unitree G1 humanoid to complete the full task using only onboard depth sensing and proprioception, while generalizing robustly across task variations and transferring effectively from simulation to reality. Project page: viloman-anonymous.pages.dev.

</details>


### [53] [Kinematics-Grounded Agentic AI for Robotic Additive Manufacturing Process Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19347)
*Jingzhan Ge,Ruimin Chen,Azadeh Haghighi,Jiong Tang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出A-RAM——一个"智能体-专家-工具"框架，将用户意图与零件文件转化为可追溯、可直接执行的机器人增材制造规划方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人增材制造使路径规划依赖于机器人本体特性：切片决策与零件朝向决定生成路径，而零件朝向与工作空间布置又影响其运动学可实现性。现有AM工具、基于大模型的决策支持方法与数字孪生系统均无法对上述耦合决策进行集成的执行前评估。

Method: LLM负责解读制造目标与约束、识别给定及可搜索的规划变量，并将推理编码为模式受限的请求；确定性规划智能体据此实例化对应搜索工作流；领域工具对切片、布置、逆运动学、轨迹时序、Joint-6加加速度与挤出等指标计算定量证据。在六轴机械臂AM单元上通过三类案例研究评估。

Result: 在评估的候选集中，所选方案相比最不利的有效候选，最大Joint-6加加速度降低达53.5%，平均绝对Joint-6加加速度降低达48.3%；面向目标的填充筛选使运动规划完成时间缩短达40.1%，挤出路径缩短达12.7%。

Conclusion: A-RAM实现了切片—布置—运动学耦合决策的执行前集成评估，可生成可追溯且可直接执行的规划方案，并显著改善运动平顺性与效率。

Abstract: Robotic additive manufacturing (AM) extends material-extrusion printing beyond gantry kinematics but makes process planning robot-dependent. A slicer-generated plan that appears favorable in part coordinates can become infeasible or robotically unfavorable on a manipulator because slicer-process decisions and part orientation determine the generated path, while part orientation and workspace placement affect its kinematic realization. Existing AM tools, large language model (LLM)-based decision-support methods, and digital-shadow systems do not provide integrated pre-execution evaluation of these coupled decisions. This paper presents agentic robotic additive manufacturing (A-RAM), an agent-specialist-tool framework that converts user intent and a part file into traceable, execution-ready plans. The LLM interprets manufacturing objectives and constraints, identifies prescribed and searchable planning variables, and encodes this reasoning in a schema-constrained request; a deterministic Planning Agent instantiates the corresponding search workflow, while domain tools compute quantitative evidence for slicing, placement, inverse kinematics, trajectory timing, Joint-6 jerk, and extrusion. The framework is evaluated on a six-axis robotic-arm AM cell through three case studies covering expert-specified planning, goal-only planning, objective-dependent infill screening, and geometry-dependent orientation-placement selection. Across the evaluated candidate sets, selected plans achieve up to 53.5% lower maximum Joint-6 jerk and 48.3% lower mean absolute Joint-6 jerk than the least favorable valid candidates, while objective-specific infill screening yields motion-plan completion times up to 40.1% shorter and extrusion paths up to 12.7% shorter than the corresponding least favorable screened patterns.

</details>


### [54] [Design and Experimental Validation of a 3D Printed Torsional Series Elastic Actuator for Safe Human Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19367)
*Joel Hidalgo Pisco,Melissa Cobos Condo,Luigi Miranda,Dennys Paillacho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并验证了一种用于串联弹性驱动器（SEA）的3D打印热塑性聚氨酯（TPU）低刚度扭转弹簧，通过有限元分析与实验验证其高度线性的扭矩-形变响应（Ks=0.066 Nm/degree），并配合带扭矩阈值切换的混合位置控制器，实现了安全的物理人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机交互（pHRI）中内在安全至关重要，尤其对社交与服务机器人而言。串联弹性驱动器虽能提供基于硬件的柔性，但传统金属设计通常需要复杂的多部件装配，成本高、制造难度大，因此需要一种低成本、易制造且安全的柔性元件方案。

Method: 采用3D打印TPU制造低刚度扭转弹簧作为SEA的柔性元件；进行有限元分析（FEA）预测其扭矩-形变特性；并设计带扭矩阈值切换的混合位置控制器，以适配标准位置受限伺服电机，实现轨迹跟踪与外力顺服的切换控制。

Result: 弹簧表现出高度线性的扭矩-形变响应（Ks=0.066 Nm/degree），与数值预测偏差小于3%（偏差归因于FDM结构各向异性）；系统能准确跟踪非稳态轨迹并安全顺服外部扰动；TPU的固有材料阻尼充当被动低通滤波器，抑制控制模式切换时的高频振荡。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构为安全pHRI提供了一种成本低、可靠且易于制造的解决方案，验证了3D打印TPU扭转弹簧在SEA设计中的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Ensuring intrinsic safety in physical human robot interaction (pHRI) is a critical requirement for social and service robots. While Series Elastic Actuators (SEAs) offer hardware based compliance, traditional metallic designs often require complex, multi part assemblies. This paper presents the design, finite element analysis (FEA), and experimental validation of a low stiffness, torsional spring for SEAs, manufactured via 3D printed thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU). The compliant element exhibits a highly linear torque deformation response (Ks = 0.066 Nm/degree), matching numerical predictions with under 3% deviation, a variance attributed to FDM structural anisotropy. To accommodate external interactions using standard position limited servomotors, a hybrid position controller with torque threshold switching was implemented. Experimental evaluations demonstrate the system ability to accurately track non stationary trajectories and safely yield to external disturbances. Furthermore, the inherent material damping of the TPU acts as a passive low pass filter, preventing high frequency oscillations during control mode transitions. The proposed architecture offers a cost effective, reliable, and easily manufacturable solution for safe pHRI.

</details>


### [55] [Enhancing the Perception of Safety and Comfort during Physical Human-Robot Handshake Interactions by Integrating Flexible Elements into a Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19375)
*Joel Hidalgo,Dennys Paillacho,Melissa Cobos,Luigi Miranda*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验对比刚性关节与串联弹性驱动器（SEA）机械臂在握手交互中的表现，发现SEA能提升用户感知的安全性与舒适度，支持其用于更安全、更易被社会接受的人机交互设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机物理交互中的安全与舒适是社交技术发展的关键问题，而握手等自然手势因涉及直接物理接触而成为挑战。作者希望验证在机械臂中引入串联弹性驱动器（SEA）吸收冲击这一设计策略，是否能改善感知安全性与交互体验，并明确其在提升物理安全与社会接受度方面的优势。

Method: 设计实验以直接对比关节刚性与引入弹性元件的效果。10名志愿者（6男4女）分别与两种机械臂各完成一次握手交互：一种为刚性关节，另一种为采用SEA的柔性关节。测试中采集末端执行器轨迹等客观数据，并通过聚焦安全性、自然性与自信心的感知问卷收集主观信息。

Result: 问卷结果显示，配备SEA的机械臂在安全性与舒适度方面获得更高的用户感知评价，支持其在促进更安全、更具社会接受性的人机交互方面的潜力。同时采集了末端执行器轨迹的客观数据用于对比分析。

Conclusion: 在机械臂中引入串联弹性驱动器（SEA）能够提升握手交互中用户感知的安全性与舒适度，表明SEA有助于改善人机物理交互的物理安全与社会接受度，适合应用于日常场景中的社交机器人系统。

Abstract: Safety and comfort in human-robot physical in-teractions are essential aspects in the development of social technologies, where natural gestures, such as handshakes, represent a challenge due to their direct physical contact. The implementation of series elastic actuators (SEA) to absorb impacts is proposed as a design strategy that favors safer interactions. This paper presents an experimental study aimed at evaluating how the incorporation of SEAs in robotic arms influences perceived safety and the interaction experience dur-ing handshaking. The design allows a direct comparison of the effect of rigidity versus the incorporation of elastic elements, in order to identify the advantages of SEAs in improving the physical safety and social acceptance of robotic systems in everyday contexts. The experiment was carried out with 10 volunteers (6 men and 4 women), who performed two interactions with each robotic arm: one with rigid joints and the other with flexible joints using SEA. During testing, objective data on end-effector trajectories were collected, as well as subjective information through a perception survey focused on safety, naturalness, and confidence during the handshake. The survey results show increased perceptions of safety and comfort with the SEA-equipped arm, supporting its potential to facilitate safer and more socially accepted human-robot interactions.

</details>


### [56] [Task-Oriented Active Learning of Residual Dynamics for Model Predictive Path Integral Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19378)
*Nobuaki Aoki,Hojin Lee,Stefan Sosnowski,Sandra Hirche*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ToIA主动学习准则，用于MPPI在线GP残差学习，按任务相关性加权早期观测对后续预测不确定性的削减，提升越野导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线残差学习可减小预测控制中的模型失配，但被动数据采集难以覆盖任务后期才变得重要的状态；而任务无关主动学习虽聚焦不确定或信息量大的区域，其获取的信息未必提升任务性能。

Method: 针对带在线高斯过程残差学习的模型预测路径积分控制（MPPI），提出Task-Oriented Information Acquisition（ToIA）准则。对每条采样控制序列，估计rollout早期获得的观测对同一rollout后续状态预测不确定性的削减量，并以该rollout与任务的相关性加权。评分直接在现有MPPI rollout批次上计算，无需采样未来观测或在假设后验更新下重新优化控制。

Result: 在异构地形的留出地图越野导航仿真中，ToIA相比被动GP学习将到达目标成功率分别提升19.3和27.4个百分点，并在密集与稀疏在线学习间隔下均优于任务无关主动学习基线。消融实验表明任务相关性在稀疏模型更新下尤为重要。实现可在NVIDIA RTX 2080 Ti上支持20 Hz在线控制。

Conclusion: ToIA通过将信息增益与任务相关性耦合，在计算开销可控的前提下实现高效在线主动残差学习，显著提升MPPI在异构地形下的导航性能，且任务相关性在低更新频率场景中收益更突出。

Abstract: Online residual learning can reduce model mismatch in predictive control, but passive data collection may fail to adequately cover states that become important later in the task. Task-agnostic active learning targets uncertain or informative regions, but information acquired in such regions does not necessarily improve task performance. This paper introduces Task-Oriented Information Acquisition (ToIA), an active-learning criterion for model predictive path integral control (MPPI) with online Gaussian process (GP) residual learning. For each sampled control sequence, ToIA estimates how much an observation obtained early in the rollout would reduce predictive uncertainty at later states on the same rollout, and weights this reduction by the rollout's relevance to the task. The score is evaluated over the existing MPPI rollout batch without sampling future observations or re-optimizing control under hypothetical posterior updates. In simulated off-road navigation across held-out maps with heterogeneous terrain, ToIA improved the goal-reaching success rate over passive GP learning by 19.3 and 27.4 percentage points and outperformed task-agnostic active-learning baselines across dense and sparse online-learning intervals. An ablation study indicates that task relevance is particularly important under sparse model updates. The implementation supports online control at 20 Hz on an NVIDIA RTX 2080 Ti.

</details>


### [57] [From Rollout to Reset: A Graph-Based Harness for Autonomous Long-Horizon Manipulation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19413)
*Jing Jiang,Yue Yang,Xinkai Jiang,Gedas Bertasius,Daniel J. Szafir,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALTER 通过在学习到的原子重置技能库上进行规划来恢复场景，将演示成本与技能库规模而非终结状态数量挂钩，在四个长时程任务上优于 AutoEval 与运动规划重置，并大幅降低人工时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人评估依赖人工重置场景，耗费操作者时间且初始状态分布未指定，导致结果难以复现。AutoEval 虽能自动化重置与评分，但仅适用于单步任务：长时程 rollout 的终结状态组合爆炸，单一学习到的重置策略无法覆盖。

Method: HALTER（用于自主长时程任务评估与重置的框架）在线从点云与视觉基础模型构建空间场景图，由 LLM 在该图上推理，完成 rollout 评分、重置规划与重置成功性验证；重置本身是在一组学习到的原子重置技能库上进行规划，无需为任何任务采集带标注的成功图像。

Result: 在 Franka 机械臂的四个长时程任务上，HALTER 在 76% 的 episode 中成功恢复场景（AutoEval 52%，运动规划重置 65%）；完成技能比例的估计正确率为 90%（对照 76%）；重置验证判定正确率为 91%（AutoEval 78%）；相对人工重置将评估活动的操作者时间减少 72%。在三个留出任务上考察组合泛化，HALTER 重置成功率为 74.7%，而逐任务重置策略仅 1.3%；并对场景表示与图更新频率进行了消融。

Conclusion: 以原子技能库规划替代单一重置策略，可使演示成本随技能库规模而非终结状态数量扩展，从而把自动化重置与评分扩展到长时程任务，并提升复现性、降低人工负担。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies are improving quickly, and real-robot evaluation remains the standard evidence for that progress. It still relies on a human to reset the scene between rollouts, which consumes operator time and leaves the initial state distribution unspecified, so results reproduce poorly. A recent system, AutoEval, automates both reset and scoring, but only for single-step tasks, because a long-horizon rollout can terminate in combinatorially many configurations that no single learned reset policy covers. We present HALTER, a Harness for Autonomous Long-horizon Task Evaluation and Reset, which restores the scene by planning over a library of learned atomic reset skills, so demonstration cost scales with the size of that library rather than with the number of terminal states. HALTER builds a spatial scene graph online from point clouds and vision foundation models, and an LLM reasons over this graph to score the rollout, plan the reset, and verify that the reset succeeded, without collecting labeled success images for any task. On four long-horizon tasks on a Franka arm, HALTER restores the scene in 76% of episodes, against 52% for AutoEval and 65% for a motion-planning reset, and it estimates the completed-skill fraction correctly in 90% of episodes, against 76%. Its reset-verification verdict is correct in 91% of episodes, compared with 78% for AutoEval. It also cuts the operator time of an evaluation campaign by 72% relative to manual reset. We further measure compositional generalization on three held-out tasks, where HALTER resets 74.7% of episodes against 1.3% for a per-task reset policy, and we ablate the scene representation and the graph update rate.

</details>


### [58] [Predict Before You Deploy: Offline Prediction of Quantization-Induced Task Degradation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19441)
*Jiuyi Xu,Jinjia Guo,Meida Chen,Jing Du,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PreDE 框架，通过离线动作偏差预测量化导致的 WAM 任务性能退化，减少闭环评估成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）依赖视频生成骨干，部署时内存与计算开销大；训练后量化虽可降内存、加速推理，但位宽、分组、量化器构成巨大配置空间，穷举闭环评估代价高昂。

Method: PreDE（Predict Before You Deploy）为策略校准框架：基于离线动作偏差预测量化引起的任务退化；用小型开发集的闭环结果校准两个阈值，在固定观测日志下对配置做出接受/拒绝/推迟决策；在"设置内标签排序假设"下，规则仅在所有与开发标签一致的阈值都同意时给出决策。

Result: 跨 5 个 WAM 与 4 个基准设置，量化损失依赖配置，无法仅由位宽或统一偏差阈值解释。28 个留出配置中，PreDE 在观测闭环结果前给出 21 个决策（75% 覆盖），全部匹配观测标签；推迟项含可接受结果及 33 个百分点的损失。450 次 Franka Research 3 试验中，高偏差组均显著退化，低偏差组无显著退化；实机 W4A4 获得 1.37× 动作查询加速与约 44% 峰值内存下降。

Conclusion: 结果支持采用策略特定的行为校准进行量化配置选择，同时识别出仍需闭环评估的候选配置。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) rely on video-generation backbones, requiring substantial memory and compute for deployment. Post-training quantization reduces memory and can accelerate inference, but bit width, grouping, and quantizer choice define a large configuration space. Identifying configurations that preserve task performance through exhaustive closed-loop evaluation is costly. We propose PreDE (Predict Before You Deploy), a policy-calibrated framework for predicting quantization-induced task degradation from offline action deviations. Using closed-loop outcomes from a small development set, PreDE calibrates two thresholds and accepts, rejects, or defers new configurations using a fixed observation log. Under a within-setting label-ordering hypothesis, the rule issues decisions where all thresholds consistent with the development labels agree. Across five WAMs and four benchmark settings, quantization produces configuration-dependent task losses that cannot be explained by bit width alone or a shared deviation threshold. Across 28 held-out configurations from two policies, PreDE issued 21 decisions before observing closed-loop outcomes (75% coverage), all matching the observed acceptable or degraded labels. Deferred candidates included both acceptable outcomes and a 33-percentage-point loss. In 450 Franka Research 3 trials across two independently fine-tuned policies, all configurations assigned to high-deviation groups before testing showed significant degradation, while low-deviation comparisons showed no statistically significant degradation. On the real robot, W4A4 achieved a 1.37x action-query speedup and approximately 44% lower peak memory. These results support policy-specific behavioral calibration for quantization configuration selection while identifying candidates that require closed-loop evaluation. The code is available at https://github.com/jiuyixu25/PreDE.

</details>


### [59] [From Wizard-of-Oz Human-Robot Dialogue Collection to a Taxonomy of Robot Response Decisions: A Retrospective Analysis of Assistive Pilot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19447)
*Guangping Liu,Nicholas Hawkins,Tipu Sultan,Flavio Esposito,Madi Dian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文回顾性分析一项室内机器人Wizard-of-Oz先导研究，从40个任务片段中提炼出六种响应模式与四类歧义的分层分类法，并验证其标注一致性与用于训练视觉语言模型的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常室内环境中遵循自然语言指令的机器人必须应对不完整的人类话语：指令常省略关键信息（视野外物体的身份、意图目的地或用户目标）。现有数据集缺乏真实情境对话，也缺乏基于实践的标准来判断机器人何时应行动、确认、澄清或拒绝。原研究中向导缺乏正式交流政策，导致机器人侧决策不一致，因而需要显式的决策方案。

Method: 回顾性分析一项先导Wizard-of-Oz研究：5名参与者使用轮椅式移动操作臂执行开门、开抽屉、喂食、饮水、清洁等日常室内任务，向导在不遵循正式交流政策的情况下作答。基于40个片段，归纳出包含六种响应模式（ANSWER、REPORT_DONE、REFUSE、CONFIRM、CLARIFY、ACT）与四类歧义（意图、指称、空间、可理解性）的分层分类法。由两名人类标注者和一名AI标注者将该方案应用于先导数据，并进行一致性评估；同时以分类法衍生的标签微调LLaVA-1.6-7B（针对ACT与CLARIFY）。

Result: 干净标签率分别为91%和89%；在决策点、响应模式与歧义层级上，Cohen's κ在0.72至0.95之间（涵盖人-人与人-AI比较）。以分类法衍生标签微调LLaVA-1.6-7B用于ACT和CLARIFY，表明利用该分类法标注训练视觉语言模型可行。决策点识别与REPORT_DONE仍存在边界案例。

Conclusion: 该分层分类法可支持一致的不完整指令处理决策与标注，并可支撑视觉语言模型的微调；剩余边界问题促使提出约束性协议，以实现更一致的对话采集。

Abstract: Robots that follow natural-language instructions in everyday indoor environments must act on incomplete human utterances. Instructions often omit essential information, such as the identity of an out-of-view object, an intended destination, or the user's goal. Existing datasets contain little real-world situated dialogue and provide few practice-grounded criteria for deciding when a robot should act, confirm, clarify, or refuse.
