Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对贫氢超亮超新星SN 2018bsz的多波段测光再分析发现，在扣除平滑衰减后，u至Ks共十个波段存在相位一致的约31.6天准周期残余调制，持续约三个周期，其物理起源尚不能唯一确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星光变曲线中的周期性结构可能揭示中心引擎动力学、双星轨道运动或与结构化星周介质相互作用的物理时钟，因此有必要对具有多波段长期监测数据的SLSN-I进行系统的周期性搜寻。

Method: 使用Swift/UVOT与GROND观测数据，覆盖极大光后约110天静止系时间；对各波段平滑衰减趋势独立建模并扣除，然后采用广义Lomb-Scargle周期图分析残差；开展联合多波段拟合，并利用比较星检验与月周期检验排除观测系统误差。

Result: 在u至Ks十个波段中发现相位大致一致的周期性残余变化；g、r、i、z、J单波段最强探测给出31.5–31.8天周期，联合多波段分析得到静止系周期P=31.61±0.03天；信号可持续追踪约三个周期，呈准周期性，对去趋势方法稳健；调制在光学至近红外基本相位相干，振幅随波长变短而增大，低显著性波段还显示最佳周期可能随波长漂移。

Conclusion: 该调制是SLSN-I中相干、月尺度测光调制最清晰的实例之一；但现有观测无法唯一确定其物理起源，Lense-Thirring进动、与爆炸前已结构化的星周介质相互作用，以及与幸存伴星的后超新星相互作用均为可能解释。

Abstract: Recurrent structure in supernova (SN) light curves may reveal physical clocks associated with central-engine dynamics, binary orbital motion, or interaction with structured circumstellar material (CSM). We present a multiband photometric reanalysis of the nearby hydrogen-poor superluminous SN (SLSN-I) 2018bsz (z=0.0267), using Swift/UVOT and GROND observations spanning approximately 110 rest-frame days after maximum light. After independently modelling and subtracting the smooth decline in each band, Generalized Lomb-Scargle periodograms reveal recurrent residual variations at broadly consistent phases across ten bands from u to Ks. The strongest individual-band detections, in the g, r, i, z, and J bands, yield periods of 31.5-31.8 days, while the joint multiband analysis gives a rest-frame period of P=31.61 (+/-0.03) days. The modulation is traced over approximately three cycles and is therefore described as quasi-periodic. The signal is robust to the adopted detrending procedure, while comparison-star and lunar-cycle tests disfavour an observational systematic. The modulation is broadly phase-coherent from the optical to the near-infrared, but its amplitude increases towards shorter wavelengths; lower-significance bands also show possible wavelength-dependent shifts in the best-fitting period. The present observations do not uniquely determine the physical origin. Lense-Thirring precession, interaction with CSM structured before the explosion, and post-SN interaction with a surviving companion remain possible. SN 2018bsz provides one of the clearest examples of coherent, month-scale photometric modulation in an SLSN-I.

</details>


### [163] [The 2025.6 X-ray Minimum of Eta Carinae](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20046)
*Amit Kashi,Noam Soker*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用NICER、Swift、XRISM和Chandra对η Carinae 2025.6年X射线极小期及恢复期进行观测，发现极小期比前五个周期提前约10天，光谱软化且光度短时标变化，支持X射线衰减由双星风相互作用而非视线吸收（如食）主导，其主因是主星物质吸积到次星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清η Carinae近星点X射线极小期的物理成因：是视线方向吸收增强（如食效应）还是双星风碰撞区本身的变化，并借此理解双星吸积在亮蓝变星大爆发及中等光度光学瞬变中的作用。

Method: 使用NICER/XTI、Swift/XRT、XRISM/Xtend与Chandra/HRC-I对2025.6年X射线极小期及恢复期开展多仪器观测；构建软（0.5–2 keV）与硬（2–10 keV）波段光变曲线，并计算硬度比，与以往五个周期的行为进行对比。

Result: X射线极小期比前五个周期提前约10天到达；与以往事件一致，光谱在衰减过程中变软，同时X射线光度存在短时标变化。

Conclusion: 增强的视线吸收（如食所预期）对产生该光谱事件不起主要作用；X射线衰减源于双星风的随机变化与不稳定性及其相互作用、碰撞风区瓦解，以及主星风物质吸积到次星。近星点深X射线极小期的主要过程是主星物质被次星吸积；这意味着19世纪大爆发与小爆发期间（主星质量损失率更高）吸积率更高，吸积可能驱动喷流，并可推广解释其他亮蓝变星巨爆发与中等光度光学瞬变。

Abstract: We present observations of the recent 2025.6 X-ray minimum and recovery of $η$~Carinae, obtained with NICER/XTI, Swift/XRT, XRISM/Xtend, and Chandra/HRC-I. We construct light curves in the soft (0.5--2 keV) and hard (2--10 keV) bands and derive hardness ratios. The X-ray emission reached its minimum about 10 days earlier than in the previous five cycles. As in previous events, the spectrum softened during the decline, while the X-ray luminosity varied on short timescales. These findings strengthen the conclusion that enhanced line-of-sight absorption, such as that expected during an eclipse, does not have a significant role in producing the spectroscopic event. Instead, they indicate that the X-ray decline is caused by stochastic variations and instabilities in the two stellar winds and their interaction, destruction of the colliding winds region, and accretion of primary wind onto the secondary. The main process behind the deep X-ray minimum near periastron is the accretion of mass lost by the primary star onto the secondary star. Accretion at periastron passages in $η$ Carinae implies that accretion occurred at much higher rates during the nineteenth-century Great and Lesser Eruptions, when the primary star lost mass at much higher rates. Such accretion likely launches jets. The broader implication is that accretion events in binary systems, possibly with jet launching, power other luminous blue variable giant eruptions and intermediate-luminosity optical transients.

</details>


### [164] [Evolution and mergers of eccentric white dwarf binaries encountering intermediate-mass black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20338)
*Adarsh Pandey,Aryabrat Mahapatra,Tapobrata Sarkar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用三维SPH模拟研究了偏心白矮星双星近距离掠过中等质量黑洞(IMBH)后的演化，发现IMBH遭遇显著加速并合，且并合结果与遭遇强度非单调相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究近距离经过中等质量黑洞(IMBH)如何改变在内部近心点发生名义洛希瓣溢出的偏心白矮星(WD)双星的演化。

Method: 采用三维光滑粒子流体动力学(SPH)，对偏心率e_in=0.6、质量比q=0.2–0.7的双星，在施瓦茨希尔德IMBH周围以抛物线、逆行轨道、两种遭遇强度β^b=0.5和1.5进行演化，并与孤立演化情形对比。

Result: IMBH极大加速并合：所有受扰双星若并合均在第二次内部近心点通过期间或之后不久发生，快于孤立并合时间。并合结果与遭遇强度非单调：q=0.2双星在β^b=1.5时保留两个束缚核心，而在β^b=0.5时并合。更深遭遇使其进入偏心率更低的轨道，表明遭遇时机相对内轨道很重要。深遭遇还导致q=0.2–0.4的内部轨道角动量瞬时反转，吸积体束缚物质比施主体获得更多自旋角动量。双星质心绕IMBH的比角动量仅变化初始值的几×10^-4。识别出可在动力学时标上燃烧氦的氦源物质，q=0.7时约占氦源质量的15–17%。

Conclusion: IMBH遭遇能够在定量上改变偏心白矮星双星的演化。

Abstract: We study how a close passage past an intermediate-mass black hole (IMBH) changes the evolution of an eccentric white dwarf (WD) binary that has nominal Roche lobe overflow at internal pericenter. Using three-dimensional smoothed particle hydrodynamics, we follow binaries with eccentricity $e_{\rm in}=0.6$ and mass ratios $q=0.2$ -- $0.7$ on parabolic, retrograde orbits around a Schwarzschild IMBH at two encounter strengths, $β^b=0.5$ and $1.5$. We compare them with the same binaries evolved in isolation. The IMBH greatly speeds up the merger. Every IMBH-perturbed binary that merges does so during or shortly after its second internal pericenter passage, which is always less than the time taken to merge in isolation. The merger outcome is not monotonic with encounter strength. The $q=0.2$ binary retains two bound cores for $β^b=1.5$ but merges at $β^b=0.5$. In the deeper encounter it settles onto a less eccentric post-encounter orbit, showing that the timing of the encounter relative to the inner orbit plays an important role. Deep encounters also produce transient reversals of the internal orbital angular momentum for $q=0.2$ -- $0.4$, while the material bound to the accretor gains more spin angular momentum than that bound to the donor. The specific orbital angular momentum of the binary CM around the IMBH changes by only a few $\times10^{-4}$ of its initial value. We also identify He-origin material that can burn helium on a dynamical timescale, amounting to $\sim15$ -- $17\%$ of the He-origin mass for $q=0.7$. IMBH encounters can therefore quantitatively change the evolution of eccentric WD binaries.

</details>


### [165] [Low Frequency Radio Imaging Study of PSR J1818-1607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20745)
*A. Yadav,M. P. Surnis,B. C. Joshi,M. Bagchi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用uGMRT在300–500 MHz与550–750 MHz对磁星PSR J1818−1607进行低频射电观测，探测到其连续谱源，发现流量密度与谱指数变化及两次可能的射电耀发，并讨论谱指数变化对射电辐射机制的启示；多份存档射电图像中未探测到该源及弥漫辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星射电辐射机制尚不明确，尤其射电响亮磁星的谱指数演化与耀发行为缺乏低频高灵敏度观测约束，因此需要对PSR J1818−1607开展低频射电监测。

Method: 使用升级后的巨型米波射电望远镜（uGMRT）在band 3（300–500 MHz）和band 4（550–750 MHz）对磁星PSR J1818−1607进行低频射电观测，识别其连续谱源，测量流量密度时间序列与谱指数，并检索多份存档射电成像巡天数据。

Result: 识别出与磁星相关的连续谱源；观测到流量密度及谱指数的变化；流量密度时间序列显示两次潜在的射电耀发事件，且其谱性质不同；存档射电巡天中未探测到该连续谱源，也无相关弥漫辐射的迹象。

Conclusion: PSR J1818−1607的射电辐射存在时变流量与谱指数，提示其耀发期间可能存在不同的辐射机制；谱指数变化为射电响亮磁星的辐射机制模型提供了观测约束，但源的稳定性与弥漫辐射关联仍待更多观测验证。

Abstract: We report on the low-frequency radio observations of magnetar PSR J1818$-$1607 carried out with the upgraded Giant Metrewave Radio Telescope at band 3 (300$-$500 MHz) and band 4 (550-750 MHz). We have identified the continuum source associated with the magnetar and report variations in flux density and its spectral index. The flux density timeseries for the magnetar reveals the presence of two potential radio flaring episodes with varying spectral properties. We discuss the implications of the spectral index changes on the potential emission mechanisms for radio-loud magnetars. We also report non-detections of the continuum source as well as no direct indication for an associated diffuse emission from multiple archival radio imaging surveys.

</details>


### [166] [A systematic search for long GRBs without supernovae: global properties and rate of events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20787)
*Yu-Han Yang,Eleonora Troja,Nicolas Monsalves,Massine El Kabir,Narjes Shahamat Dehsorkh,Hira Waseem*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用Swift二十年观测系统搜寻近距（z≲0.35）无超新星关联的长伽马暴，将候选样本数量翻倍，并给出体积率约0.5 Gpc⁻³ yr⁻¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分长伽马暴（LGRB）不与明亮超新星（SN）成协，说明其爆发机制可能不同于传统坍缩星（collapsar）通道。此类无SN长伽马暴稀少，尚不清楚它们只是偶发离群事件，还是GRB族群中的重要组成部分，因此需要系统性搜寻与统计研究。

Method: 使用Swift卫星二十年观测数据，对近距（z≲0.35）且无确认SN成协的LGRB进行系统搜寻；分析其高能特性、余辉和宿主环境，筛选出具有可靠SN上限和距离尺度的样本。

Result: 识别出一个异质族群，部分与恒星形成或尘埃环境相关，部分位于宁静星系。所得样本使候选无SN事件数量增加一倍以上。由基准样本导出未经束流修正的体积率R=(0.5±0.2) Gpc⁻³ yr⁻¹，与短伽马暴（SGRB）以及光度L≳10⁵⁰ erg s⁻¹的标准含SN长伽马暴的表观发生率相当。

Conclusion: 对合理的束流因子，无SN长伽马暴的推断率与多种致密双星并合通道相符，但若将整个族群归因于单一前身星通道，允许参数空间已很窄。受候选事件数少及距离尺度不确定限制，样本无法约束低光度尾端；需要更大样本判断它们是已知前身星通道的暗弱尾端，还是通往相对论性爆发的独立路径。

Abstract: Some long duration gamma-ray bursts (LGRBs) are not associated with bright supernovae (SNe), indicating that their explosion mechanism differs from the traditional collapsar channel. SN-less LGRBs are rare, thus it remains unclear whether they are sporadic outliers or a substantial component of the GRB population. In this work, we use two decades of Swift observations to conduct a systematic search for nearby ($z\lesssim$0.35) LGRBs without a confirmed SN association. Our analysis reveals a heterogeneous population linked, in part, to star-forming or dusty environments and, in part, to quiescent galaxies. Through the analysis of their high-energy properties, afterglows, and environments, we identify a sample of LGRBs with robust SN limits and distance scales, more than doubling the number of candidate SN-less events. From our fiducial sample, we derive a volumetric rate, not corrected for beaming, of $R = (0.5 \pm 0.2) {\rm~Gpc^{-3}\ yr^{-1}}$, comparable to the apparent rate of short duration GRBs (SGRBs) and standard LGRBs with SNe with luminosities $L \gtrsim\,10^{50}\,{\rm erg\,s^{-1}}$. For plausible beaming factors, the inferred rate of SN-less LGRBs is consistent with several compact binary merger channels, although the allowed parameter space is already narrow if the entire population is attributed to a single progenitor channel. Owing to the small number of candidate events and the uncertainties in their distance scale, our sample leaves the low-luminosity tail unconstrained. A larger sample will be needed to determine whether they are the faint tail of known progenitor channels or evidence of a distinct route to relativistic explosions.

</details>


### [167] [Systematic Effects of Hydrogen and Helium Atmosphere Mismatch on Radius Inference in PSR~J0740+6620-like Synthetic NICER Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20795)
*Isiah M. Holt,M. Coleman Miller,Alexander J. Dittmann,Frederick K. Lamb*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用基于PSR J0740+6620的合成NICER脉冲波形数据，研究错误假设大气成分（氢/氦）对中子星半径推断的影响，发现当前观测的信噪比下偏差很小，但高信噪比下会产生显著偏差且残差仍可接受，贝叶斯证据能可靠识别正确大气模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星半径约束可揭示其内部冷致密物质的性质。已有研究证明NICER脉冲波形数据的半径推断对多类建模系统误差稳健，但大气成分假设错误（氢或氦）这一系统误差尚未被系统考察，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于约2.1 M⊙脉冲星PSR J0740+6620构建合成NICER脉冲波形数据，交叉假设大气成分为氢或氦（即合成数据与推断假设不一致），在真实观测的斑点到本底比条件下评估半径偏差；进一步将斑点-本底比提高约20倍、保持总计数约5.5×10^5不变，检验高信噪比下的偏差及残差表现，并采用贝叶斯证据进行模型比较。

Result: 在PSR J0740+6620实际数据的斑点-本底比下，氢/氦大气成分错配导致的半径推断偏差很小。将斑点-本底比提高约20倍后，成分错配可产生显著偏置的半径估计，同时该错误模型仍能给出统计上可接受的残差拟合；但即便在这些情况下，贝叶斯证据仍能一致地识别出正确的大气模型。

Conclusion: 错误的氦/氢大气成分假设在高信噪比下可能导致显著偏置的中子星半径推断，且仅凭拟合残差无法察觉。因此应重视并采用贝叶斯证据进行模型比较与拟合优度检验，以确保大气模型选择的可靠性。

Abstract: Constraints on neutron star radii provide insight into the properties of the cold, dense matter in their interiors. Previous studies using synthetic Neutron star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER) pulse waveform data have demonstrated that radius inferences derived therefrom are robust against several classes of modeling systematics. Here we explore the consequences of assuming the wrong atmospheric composition, using synthetic data based on the $\sim 2.1~M_\odot$ pulsar PSR~J0740$+$6620. We find that the assumption of a hydrogen atmosphere when the synthetic data assumed a helium atmosphere, or vice versa, produces little bias in the inferred radius at the spot-to-background ratio of the actual PSR~J0740$+$6620 data. However, when we increase the spot-to-background ratio by a factor of $\sim20$ while keeping the total number of counts fixed at the observed $\sim 5.5\times 10^5$, we find that composition mismatch can produce significantly biased radius estimates while remaining hidden inside a fit with statistically acceptable residuals. Even in these cases, the Bayesian evidence consistently identifies the correct atmospheric model. Our findings reinforce the importance of using the Bayesian evidence for model comparison and goodness-of-fit tests.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Tachikawa's second conjecture implies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19172)
*Haruhisa Enomoto*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明Tachikawa第二猜想蕴含Artin代数的Auslander-Reiten猜想，并由此推出若干同调猜想彼此等价、且Tachikawa第二猜想蕴含第一猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Auslander-Reiten猜想、广义Nakayama猜想、Auslander-Gorenstein猜想、Nakayama猜想、Gorenstein-投射猜想与Tachikawa第二猜想之间的联系一直是同调代数与表示论关注的核心问题。已知的蕴含关系尚不足以确定它们是否等价，缺少Tachikawa第二猜想对Auslander-Reiten猜想的蕴含这一关键环节。

Method: 在交换Artin环上的Artin代数框架中，利用代数的二重平凡扩张（two-fold trivial extension）构造论证，证明Tachikawa第二猜想蕴含Auslander-Reiten猜想。

Result: Tachikawa第二猜想 ⇒ Auslander-Reiten猜想；结合已知蕴含关系，上述六个猜想（Auslander-Reiten、广义Nakayama、Auslander-Gorenstein、Nakayama、Gorenstein-投射、Tachikawa第二）全部等价，并且Tachikawa第二猜想蕴含Tachikawa第一猜想。

Conclusion: 同调猜想的等价网络被补全为一个完整的等价类，Tachikawa第二猜想成为该等价体系中的核心，同时确立其对第一猜想的蕴含。

Abstract: We prove that Tachikawa's second conjecture implies the Auslander-Reiten conjecture for artin algebras over a commutative artinian ring. The proof uses the two-fold trivial extension of an algebra. Together with known implications, it follows that the Auslander-Reiten conjecture, the generalized Nakayama conjecture, the Auslander-Gorenstein conjecture, the Nakayama conjecture, the Gorenstein-projective conjecture, and Tachikawa's second conjecture are all equivalent, and that Tachikawa's second conjecture implies his first.

</details>


### [169] [The Arthur-Packet Support Equality for Real Reductive Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19197)
*Jiawei Yang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文在实Arthur参数与其Jordan分解中单幂参数之间，证明了microlocal packet包含于两步Jordan诱导像支集内的反向包含，从而在AIMV猜想方向上补全等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIMV已证明microlocal packet含于ψ₂ packet两步Jordan诱导的支集中并猜想相等；本文旨在证明反向包含以确立等式。

Method: 先平移至充分正的平移，使相关ABV空间连通分支共享旗簇模型；用perverse devissage将问题化为奇异支集蕴含；借助AIMV microstalk比较中的incidence计算；再对packet成员用coherent continuation回归原参数。

Result: 证明了反向包含成立，即microlocal packet等于ψ₂ packet两步Jordan诱导像的支集。

Conclusion: 反向包含得证，确立了AIMV所猜想的两步Jordan诱导等式。

Abstract: Let $ψ$ be a real Arthur parameter and let $ψ_2$ be the unipotent parameter in a fixed Jordan decomposition. Adams, Ionov, Mason-Brown, and Vogan proved that the microlocal packet of $ψ$ is contained in the support of the two-step Jordan induction of the packet of $ψ_2$, and conjectured equality. We prove the reverse inclusion. The argument first passes to a sufficiently positive translate, where the relevant connected components of the ABV spaces have a common flag-variety model. Perverse devissage reduces the problem to a singular-support implication, which follows from the incidence calculation underlying the AIMV microstalk comparison. Coherent continuation for individual packet members then returns the result to the original parameter.

</details>


### [170] [On the Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda conjecture on formal degrees for $\mathrm{G_2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19430)
*Yugo Takanashi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 证明$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$的triality与内窥群$\mathrm{G}_2$之间的twisted endoscopic character identities蕴含$\mathrm{G}_2$的Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda形式度猜想，并在此过程中建立了$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$带triality的表示论基本结果，关键工具是$E_6$型伴随群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究$\mathrm{G}_2$的形式度（formal degree）问题，即Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda猜想，并寻找证明该猜想的有效路径。作者希望通过$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$与$\mathrm{G}_2$之间的内窥关系来建立这一结论，同时弥补$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$带triality的表示论中缺失的基础结果。

Method: 利用$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$的triality自同构与内窥群$\mathrm{G}_2$之间的twisted endoscopic character identities（扭曲内窥特征恒等式）；建立$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$带triality作用下的表示论基础结果；关键地应用$E_6$型伴随群（the adjoint group of type $E_6$）作为分析工具。

Result: 证明了$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$与$\mathrm{G}_2$之间的twisted endoscopic character identities蕴含$\mathrm{G}_2$的Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda形式度猜想；同时获得了$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$带triality表示论的一系列基本结论。

Conclusion: Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda猜想对$\mathrm{G}_2$成立，可由$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$的三重性与$\mathrm{G}_2$之间的扭曲内窥特征恒等式导出；$\mathrm{PGSO}_8$带triality的表示论可由$E_6$型伴随群的方法系统地处理。

Abstract: We prove that the twisted endoscopic character identities between triality $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ and the endoscopic group $\mathrm{G}_2$ imply the Hiraga-Ichino-Ikeda conjecture on formal degrees for $\mathrm{G}_2$. In the course of proving the main result, we also establish some fundamental results on representation theory of $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ with triality. A key feature of our approach is an application of the adjoint group of type $E_6$.

</details>


### [171] [A Comparison Theorem for Parahoric Character Sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19860)
*Zhihang Yu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为仿射抛物子群上特征层的特征函数建立了Jordan分解公式，在$q$充分大时将其等同于深层次Deligne--Lusztig特征，并证明广义深层次Green函数与特征层函数的正交性，最终给出$G^{F^2}$特征限制到$G^{F}$的重数的层论表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lusztig (1990) 的结果给出了深度零情形下特征层特征函数与Deligne--Lusztig特征的关系，但正深度情形尚缺乏相应的分解与正交性理论，本文旨在将这一框架推广到正深度。

Method: 对INY25定义的仿射抛物子群上的特征层，建立其特征函数的Jordan分解公式；在$q$充分大时将该函数与深层次Deligne--Lusztig特征比较；进而证明广义深层次Green函数与特征层函数的正交性。

Result: 得到Jordan分解公式；证明特征函数等于$(-1)^{\dim G}$乘以对应的深层次Deligne--Lusztig特征（推广Lusztig 1990至正深度）；证明广义深层次Green函数与特征层函数的正交性；并给出$G^{F^2}$特征限制到$G^{F}$的重数的层论表达。

Conclusion: 该工作将深度为零的经典理论系统推广至正深度，为正深度Deligne--Lusztig理论与特征层理论的联系提供了统一框架，并给出了特征限制重数的层论解释。

Abstract: We establish a Jordan decomposition formula for the characteristic functions of the character sheaves on parahoric subgroups defined in \cite{INY25}. For a sufficiently large $q$, these functions are $(-1)^{\dim G}$ times the corresponding deep level Deligne--Lusztig characters, extending \cite{Lu90} to positive depth. We also prove the orthogonality for the generalized deep level Green functions and the character sheaves functions. As an application, we present a sheaf-theoretic expression to the multiplicity of the $G^{F^2}$ character restricted to $G^{F}$.

</details>


### [172] [A refined multiplication formula in 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius extriangulated categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20049)
*Ming Ding,Fan Xu,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文在具有稳定范畴关于诱导簇倾斜对象可构造锥的 Hom-finite 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius extriangulated 范畴上，证明了 Wang-Wei-Zhang 簇特征函数的乘法公式，推广了已有结果，并给出冻结项的极小余核中项特征表示及无环箭图主系数下的显式 mesh 关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang-Wei-Zhang 的乘法公式与 Keller-Plamondon-Qin 的结果分别覆盖一维情形与（稳定）2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius 或三角范畴情形，尚缺乏在更一般的 extriangulated 范畴框架下的统一乘法公式。作者希望在此一般框架中建立该公式，并进一步理解冻结项与 Auslander-Reiten mesh 的关系。

Method: 在 Hom-finite 2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius extriangulated 范畴 C 上取簇倾斜对象，并假设其稳定范畴关于诱导的簇倾斜对象具有可构造锥；在此条件下证明 Wang-Wei-Zhang 簇特征函数的乘法公式。

Result: 证明了该乘法公式，推广了 Wang-Wei-Zhang 的一维情形结果与 Keller-Plamondon-Qin 的（稳定）2-Calabi-Yau Frobenius 或三角范畴结果；作为推论，用极小余核中项的特征函数表达了 Auslander-Reiten mesh 中的冻结项；对带主系数的无环箭图，在 Higgs 范畴中计算了冻结重数并得到显式主系数 mesh 关系。

Conclusion: 该乘法公式在 extriangulated 范畴层面统一并推广了若干已有簇特征函数乘法结果，同时通过极小余核中项刻画冻结项、并在 Higgs 范畴中给出主系数 mesh 关系，为后续研究提供了工具。

Abstract: We prove a multiplication formula for the Wang-Wei-Zhang cluster character on a Hom-finite $2$-Calabi--Yau Frobenius extriangulated category $\mathcal C$ with a cluster-tilting object under the assumption that its stable category has constructible cones with respect to the induced cluster-tilting object. This formula generalizes those obtained by Wang-Wei-Zhang for $\mathcal C$ in the one-dimensional case and by Keller-Plamondon-Qin for (stably) $2$-Calabi-Yau Frobenius or triangulated categories. As a consequence, we express the frozen term in an Auslander-Reiten mesh with the character of a minimal cosyzygy middle term. For acyclic quivers with principal coefficients, we compute these frozen multiplicities in Higgs categories and obtain explicit principal coefficient mesh relations.

</details>


### [173] [Unitary Branching for $\mathfrak{sl}(m\vert n), \mathfrak{osp}(m\vert 2n)$ and $F(4)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20332)
*Steffen Schmidt*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 利用Dirac不等式与Freudenthal递推的super类比，确定基本经典Lie超代数在偶子代数限制下的分支规律与重数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定不可约酉表示在偶子代数限制下的分支（既约成分及其重数）是Lie超代数表示论的核心问题，以往方法对非适应正系统及非典型度缺乏统一处理。

Method: 结合Huang-Pandžić-Dirac算子D_{g,g_0}的Dirac不等式与Dirac形式导出的Freudenthal递推super类比；通过odd反射传输Dirac不等式以推广到不适配于相应实形式的正系统，并确定修正项，得到有限递推过程。

Result: 给出确定偶成分及其分支重数的有限递推方法，并得到重数的封闭公式，统一适用于任意非典型度；对sl(m|n)，修正Dirac不等式给出与酉表示相关的所有正系统的酉性刻画。

Conclusion: 建立了sl(m|n)、osp(m|2n)、F(4)在偶子代数限制下分支的统一Dirac方法，并推广了酉性判据。

Abstract: We determine the branching of irreducible unitary representations of the basic classical Lie superalgebras $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, $\mathfrak{osp}(m|2n)$, and $F(4)$ under restriction to their even subalgebras. The proof combines Dirac inequalities for the Huang-Pandžić-Dirac operator $\operatorname{D}_{\mathfrak{g},\mathfrak{g}_{\bar{0}}}$ with a super analogue of Freudenthal's recursion derived from the Dirac formalism. We extend the Dirac method to positive systems that are not adapted to the relevant real form by transporting the Dirac inequality through odd reflections and determining the resulting correction terms. This yields finite recursive procedures that determine the occurring even constituents and their branching multiplicities, together with closed formulas for the latter, uniformly in the degree of atypicality. For $\mathfrak{sl}(m|n)$, the corrected Dirac inequalities also give a characterization of unitarity for all positive systems relevant to unitary representations.

</details>


### [174] [Representations of formal Lie groups and Lie pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20547)
*Fulin Chen,Binyong Sun,Chuyun Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文发展形式Lie群与Lie对的表示论基础，证明形式Lie群的表示范畴同构于对应Lie对的模范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式Lie群是微分几何中Lie群的形式化推广，类比于代数几何中的形式群概形。前作已建立形式Lie群基本理论及形式Lie理论定理（识别形式Lie群与Lie对），但表示论尚未建立，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在形式Lie群与Lie对框架下发展表示论基础；在形式流形上的函数空间上构造Lie对的模；通过形式Lie理论定理建立形式Lie群表示与对应Lie对模之间的对应关系。

Result: 在形式流形上的函数空间上构造了Lie对的模，并证明形式Lie群的表示范畴与对应Lie对的模范畴同构。

Conclusion: 形式Lie群的表示论可完全归结为对应Lie对的模论，从而将形式Lie群的表示范畴与Lie对的模范畴等同起来。

Abstract: Formal Lie groups, which generalize Lie groups in differential geometry, are analogous to formal group schemes in algebraic geometry. In a previous paper, we established the basic theory of formal Lie groups, including a formal Lie theory theorem that identifies formal Lie groups with Lie pairs. In this paper, we develop the foundations of the representation theory of formal Lie groups and Lie pairs. In particular, we construct modules of Lie pairs on function spaces over formal manifolds and prove that the category of representations of a formal Lie group is isomorphic to the category of modules of the corresponding Lie pair.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Closest univariate convex linear-quadratic function approximation with minimal number of Pieces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.18164)
*Namrata Kundu,Yves Lucet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究将给定一元分段线性二次（PLQ）函数逼近为具有最少分段数的最近凸PLQ函数，以欧氏范数度量函数间距离。方法包括：固定断点数量与位置时求解凸优化问题；固定断点数量但不固定位置时求解非凸优化问题确定最优断点布局；进而提出由贪心搜索预处理与二分搜索组成的算法，通过对数数量级次优化问题求解，两步得到最少分段数的最近凸函数。实验以多组算例验证，并与已有全局最优一元样条逼近算法对比，应用于道路设计中的竖向对齐曲线简化。使用MATLAB的YALMIP库配合CPLEX、Gurobi和BARON选择最有效求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将任意一元PLQ函数简化为最少分段数的最近凸PLQ函数，在保证逼近精度的同时降低分段复杂度，并服务于道路设计中竖向对齐曲线简化等实际需求。

Method: 以欧氏范数度量函数距离；先固定输出函数的断点数量与位置，求解凸优化问题；再固定断点数量而不固定位置，求解非凸优化问题以获得最优断点布局；最后提出由贪心搜索预处理和二分搜索组成的算法，通过对数数量级的优化问题求解获得任意PLQ函数的最少分段近似。

Result: 通过多组算例验证算法，并与先前的全局最优一元样条逼近算法进行比较；将方法应用于道路设计优化中的竖向对齐曲线简化；在MATLAB的YALMIP环境下使用CPLEX、Gurobi和BARON有效选择最高效求解器。

Conclusion: 所提两步算法能够获得最少分段数的最近凸PLQ函数近似，在算例与道路竖向对齐曲线简化中验证了有效性，并通过多种求解器对比实现了高效求解。

Abstract: We compute the closest convex piecewise linear-quadratic (PLQ) function with minimal number of pieces to a given univariate piecewise linear-quadratic function. The Euclidean norm is used to measure the distance between functions. First, we assume that the number and positions of the breakpoints of the output function are fixed, and solve a convex optimization problem. Next, we assume the number of breakpoints is fixed, but not their position, and solve a nonconvex optimization problem to determine optimal breakpoints placement. Finally, we propose an algorithm composed of a greedy search preprocessing and a dichotomic search that solves a logarithmic number of optimization problems to obtain an approximation of any PLQ function with minimal number of pieces thereby obtaining in two steps the closest convex function with minimal number of pieces.
  We illustrate our algorithms with multiple examples, compare our approach with a previous globally optimal univariate spline approximation algorithm, and apply our method to simplify vertical alignment curves in road design optimization. CPLEX, Gurobi, and BARON are used with the YALMIP library in MATLAB to effectively select the most efficient solver.

</details>


### [176] [Persistent mixed worldviews in the theory of chosen preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19316)
*Ziran Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Bernheim等(2021, AER)提出的"选择偏好"理论中的世界观收敛猜想，证明原猜想不成立：当世界观数量任意多时，混合世界观可在相对开放的初始条件下于每个平稳均衡中持续存在；但有限线性规划可刻画充分的持续条件，并且在唯一最大化评价者等条件下收敛到纯世界观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernheim等(2021, AER)提出选择偏好理论，并猜想在低"思维灵活性"(mindset flexibility)下世界观会收敛到纯世界观。本文旨在检验并修正这一猜想。

Method: 理论建模与均衡分析。构造具有任意多世界观的模型，分析平稳均衡；使用有限线性规划刻画持续条件；引入"唯一最大化评价者"(unique maximizing evaluator)、严格定制世界观(strictly tailored worldviews)等条件讨论收敛性与存在性；并做有界视野下的微小支付扰动分析。

Result: 原猜想失败：存在相对开放的初始世界观集合，使混合世界观在每个平稳均衡中持续，即使所有纯世界观均为吸收态。有限线性规划可刻画充分的持续性条件；反之，混合积累点要求共同的估值最大化。若每个可实施行动有唯一最大化评价者，则对所有内点灵活性与贴现参数，每个现存平稳均衡都收敛到纯世界观；经验效用收敛不需要该唯一性。在现存均衡中，小支付扰动以高概率在固定视野内保持妥协。严格定制世界观可在足够低灵活性下保证存在性并给出一阶均衡刻画；弱实施可能排除平稳纯策略存在。持续性与收敛结果可推广到平稳随机化。

Conclusion: Bernheim等(2021)关于低灵活性下纯世界观收敛的猜想一般不成立，需以更精细的条件（如有限线性规划持续条件、唯一最大化评价者、严格定制世界观）来刻画混合世界观的持续与收敛。

Abstract: Bernheim, Braghieri, Martínez-Marquina, and Zuckerman (2021, American Economic Review) develop a theory of chosen preferences and conjecture convergence to pure worldviews at low mindset flexibility. We show that the (original) conjecture fails: with arbitrarily many worldviews, a common-value action sustains mixed worldviews in every stationary equilibrium from a relatively open set of initial worldviews, even when all pure worldviews are absorbing.
  However, finite linear programs characterize a sufficient persistence condition. Conversely, mixed accumulation points require shared valuation maxima. If each implementable action has a unique maximizing evaluator, every existing stationary equilibrium converges to purity for all interior flexibility and discount parameters. Experienced utility converges without uniqueness. In existing equilibria, small payoff perturbations preserve compromise over fixed horizons with high probability. Strictly tailored worldviews yield existence and a one-step equilibrium characterization at sufficiently low mindset flexibility; weak implementation can preclude stationary pure-strategy existence. The persistence and convergence results extend to stationary randomization.

</details>


### [177] [NomadBBO: A direct-search framework for constrained mixed-variable blackbox optimization in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19333)
*Edward Hallé-Hannan,Christophe Tribes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 NomadBBO，一个面向混合变量黑箱优化的用户友好型 Python 库，核心算法为将 ADS 扩展至混合变量的直接搜索方法 CatADS，并集成渐进障碍约束处理与高斯过程/经验 Wasserstein 距离邻域机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合变量黑箱优化广泛存在于仿真工程与机器学习中，其目标与约束函数评估代价高昂，变量类型可包含连续、整数、二值与分类变量。现有无导数优化库虽多，但很少同时兼顾易用性、计算效率、灵活性及理论收敛保证。

Method: 提出 CatADS 直接搜索方法，将自适应直接搜索（ADS）针对定量变量的机制与处理分类变量的邻域机制相结合；邻域采用基于高斯过程或由数据导出的经验 Wasserstein 距离；不等式约束通过渐进障碍（progressive barrier）处理。混合变量与代理模型机制以 Python 实现，并通过 Cython 连接 Nomad 的高效 C++ 后端，使 Nomad 能够处理分类变量，同时支持 SMT 2.0 高斯过程模型与贝叶斯优化等外部库及混合优化策略。

Result: 在 Cat-Suite 基准测试集的约束与无约束混合变量问题上，将 NomadBBO 与其他求解器进行了数值实验对比。

Conclusion: NomadBBO 实现了易用性、效率、灵活性与理论收敛保证的结合，扩展了 Nomad 对分类变量的支持，并提供了开展混合变量黑箱优化的可扩展平台；beta 版本发布于指定 PyPI 测试地址。

Abstract: Mixed-variable blackbox optimization arises in simulation-based engineering and machine learning, where objective and constraint functions are expensive to evaluate and variables may be continuous, integer, binary, or categorical. Several software libraries are available for derivative-free optimization, but few combine ease of use, computational efficiency, flexibility, and theoretical convergence guarantees. This work presents NomadBBO, a user-friendly Python library for inequality-constrained mixed-variable blackbox optimization built around Nomad. Its core optimization framework is CatADS, a new direct-search method that extends Adaptive Direct Search (ADS) to mixed-variable problems. CatADS combines the mechanisms of ADS for quantitative variables with neighborhoods for handling categorical variables. These neighborhoods use Gaussian process-based or empirical Wasserstein distances derived from available data. Inequality constraints are handled through the progressive barrier. The mixed-variable and surrogate-based mechanisms are implemented in Python and connected to the efficient C++ backend of Nomad via Cython. This integration allows Nomad to handle categorical variables. It also enables the use of external libraries and hybrid optimization strategies, including Gaussian process models from SMT 2.0 and Bayesian optimization. Numerical experiments compare NomadBBO with other solvers on constrained and unconstrained mixed-variable problems from the Cat-Suite benchmark collection. The beta release is available at https://test.pypi.org/project/NomadBBO/

</details>


### [178] [Computing the convex envelope of bivariate piecewise linear-quadratic functions in linear time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19343)
*Tanmaya Karmarkar,Yves Lucet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在凸包计算中处理二元分段线性二次函数（PLQ）的线性时间算法，通过共轭变换与最大化操作，证明其凸包为分段有理函数并给出具体实现步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元分段线性二次函数（在多面体剖分上定义的二次函数）的凸包计算在优化、对偶理论等领域有重要应用，但现有方法缺乏高效且实用的算法，且凸包的函数形式尚不明确，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 算法分五步：(1) 计算每个二次分片的凸包，得到多面体剖分上的分段有理函数（二次除以线性）；(2) 计算各分片的Legendre-Fenchel共轭，得到抛物剖分上的分段二次函数；(3) 取所有函数的最大值，得到原PLQ函数的共轭，表示为抛物剖分上的分段二次函数；(4) 计算每个结果分片的共轭；(5) 对所有函数取最大值，得到凸包（双共轭），表示为多面体剖分上的有理函数。

Result: 算法以线性时间运行，并证明了凸包为分段有理函数（二次除以线性函数）的形式。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实用且高效的凸包计算算法，理论上证明了二元PLQ函数凸包的分段有理结构，为相关优化问题提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We compute the convex envelope of (nonconvex) bivariate piecewise linear-quadratic (PLQ) functions (bivariate quadratic functions defined on a polyhedral subdivision). Our algorithm is composed of the following steps: (1) compute the convex envelope of each quadratic piece obtaining piecewise rational functions (quadratic divided by linear function) defined over a polyhedral subdivision; (2) compute the (Legendre-Fenchel) conjugate of each resulting piece to obtain piecewise quadratic functions defined over a parabolic subdivision; (3) compute the maximum of all those functions to obtain the conjugate of the original PLQ function as a piecewise quadratic function defined on a parabolic subdivision; (4) compute the conjugate of each resulting piece; and finally (5) compute the maximum over all those functions to obtain the convex envelope (biconjugate) as rational functions (quadratic divided by linear function) defined over a polyhedral subdivision. Our contribution includes a practical algorithm running in linear time, and proving that the convex envelope is a piecewise rational function.

</details>


### [179] [Where the LLM Ends and Reliable Decisions Begin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19545)
*Priyadarshan Patil,Abhishek Basu,Vikas Reddy,Abhinav Gupta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ANVIL将优化问题自然语言描述转代码的流程解耦：仅用一次语言模型生成LaTeX建模表述，随后由确定性编译器（无模型参与）翻译为可执行代码，在NLP4LP基准上354题中329题编译成功（92.9%），易题准确率98.9%、难题91.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在包括“数学表述到建模代码”的最终翻译在内的每个阶段都调用语言模型，导致流程冗长且将模型引入到其并不擅长的形式化翻译环节。作者希望让语言模型只在其有效的环节发挥作用：将建模表述到代码的翻译交给确定性编译器，从而提升可编译性、速度与可靠性。

Method: 提出ANVIL编译架构，分离关注点：语言模型仅在基于问题类型的约束引导下调用一次，产出LaTeX建模表述。随后由确定性编译器完成翻译，包括：规范化器（normalizer）、生成带类型中间表示（typed IR）的递归下降解析器、将符号绑定到数据集schema的分析遍（analysis passes），以及代码生成器（code emitter）。在NLP4LP的354道易/难题上评估。

Result: 编译器为354个表述中的329个（92.9%）生成了代码，且全部走确定性路径，从未回退到模型生成代码。中位编译时间低于计时器10ms分辨率。整体准确率：易题98.9%，难题91.1%。作者对“编译率与准确率之间的差距”做了重点分析，错误来源包括：编译失败的表述、判定为不可行的问题、运行时报错的问题、以及目标值错误的问题；几乎所有这些错误都可追溯到表述本身而非翻译环节。

Conclusion: ANVIL通过只在语言模型真正有效的环节使用它、而非将其当作万能工具，在主流基准上取得了优异表现。将语言模型与确定性编译分离，可显著提升翻译的可靠性、可解释性与速度；剩余错误主要来自上游表述质量，而非翻译。

Abstract: Systems that turn natural-language descriptions of optimization problems into solver-ready code generally use a language model at every stage, including the final translation from a mathematical formulation into executable model-building code. We propose the ANVIL compiler architecture, where we separate these concerns. A language model is called only once, assisted by constraint guidance based on problem type, to produce a LaTeX formulation. A deterministic compiler then translates that LaTeX into code with no language model involvement. We describe the deterministic compiler (a normalizer, a recursive-descent parser producing a typed intermediate representation, analysis passes that bind symbols to a dataset schema, and a code emitter) and evaluate it on the 354 easy and hard problems of the NLP4LP benchmark. The compiler produced code for 329 of 354 formulations (92.9%), taking the deterministic path in every one of those cases and never falling back to model-generated code. Median compile time was below the 10ms resolution of our timer. Overall, our formulations achieved an accuracy of 98.9% over easy problems and 91.1% for hard problems. The gap between these compilation and accuracy figures is a key point of analysis, and we analyze it: formulations that failed to compile, problems that returned as infeasible, problems raising errors at runtime, and problems returning a wrong objective. Almost all of these errors trace back to the formulation rather than to the translation. ANVIL performs exceptionally well on leading benchmarks by using language models purely where they are effective, rather than as a catch-all tool.

</details>


### [180] [PDNQP: A GPU-based Factorization-free Method for Large-scale Nonconvex Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19557)
*Zixi Chen,Haihao Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出无分解一阶求解器PDNQP，用于大规模非凸二次规划的驻点求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解器依赖稀疏矩阵分解，计算与内存开销大，且难以在GPU上高效并行。

Method: 结合近端增广拉格朗日框架与重启加速原始-对偶混合梯度法求解强凸QP子问题；采用残差形式重构避免依赖惩罚项的normal矩阵并保持固定稀疏约束算子，并用自适应策略协调内层求解精度与外层进度；计算仅涉及稀疏矩阵-向量乘积、投影和向量运算，适合GPU。

Result: 在107个非凸CUTEst实例上成功率最高；容差10^-4时shifted几何平均运行时间加速2.6x-6.0x，10^-6时总运行时间与最快求解器相当；在30个约百万变量的大规模合成非凸QP上，27个实例满足外部终止准则，而其他求解器均未成功。

Conclusion: PDNQP作为无分解的一阶方法，在大规模非凸QP上兼顾精度与效率，并在大规模问题上显著优于现有求解器。

Abstract: Large-scale nonconvex quadratic programming remains challenging because the sparse matrix factorizations used by many existing solvers can incur substantial computational and memory costs and are difficult to parallelize efficiently on GPUs. We present PDNQP, a factorization-free first-order solver for finding stationary points of large-scale nonconvex quadratic programs. The method combines a proximal augmented-Lagrangian framework with restarted accelerated primal--dual hybrid gradient to solve the resulting strongly convex QP subproblems. Its key ingredients are a residual-form reformulation that avoids penalty-dependent normal matrices and preserves a fixed sparse constraint operator across outer iterations, and an adaptive strategy that coordinates inner-solve accuracy with outer progress. The resulting computations rely on sparse matrix--vector products, projections, and vector operations suited to GPU execution. On $107$ nonconvex CUTEst instances, PDNQP achieves the highest success rate among the tested solvers at both accuracy levels. With absolute and relative tolerances set to $10^{-4}$, it achieves speedups of $2.6\times$--$6.0\times$ in shifted geometric mean runtime; at $10^{-6}$, its aggregate runtime remains comparable to that of the fastest tested solvers. On $30$ large-scale synthetic nonconvex QPs with approximately one million variables each, PDNQP returns points satisfying the common external termination criteria on $27$ instances, while none of the other tested solvers succeeds under the same experimental protocol.

</details>


### [181] [Epidemic Control using Stackelberg Mean Field Game with Budget Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19641)
*Huaning Liu,Gokce Dayanikli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在预算有限背景下，用Stackelberg平均场博弈研究政府与大量理性个体之间的疫情防控干预优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情防控干预存在成本，预算有限时如何有效分配是重要的公共政策问题；需要刻画政府与大量理性个体之间的层级互动。

Method: 构建Stackelberg平均场博弈：政府（主方）在预算支出过程约束下选择社交距离指引与疫苗接种水平，个体（次方）形成平均场纳什均衡；由此导出双层最优控制问题。定义Stackelberg平均场博弈均衡（SMFE），用前向-后向常微分方程（FBODE）系统给出必要条件，并建立存在唯一性结果，进而提出迭代不动点算法进行数值求解。

Result: 给出了SMFE的必要条件（FBODE系统）及存在唯一性结果，并通过迭代不动点算法获得数值解与均衡行为的计算观察。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限预算下政府最优防疫干预提供了均衡分析与可计算求解路径，并展示了均衡行为特征。

Abstract: Epidemic mitigation comes with a price in practice, and when budget is limited, its effective allocation becomes an important question on public policy. In this paper, we study epidemic control through a Stackelberg mean-field game in which a principal, representing the government, chooses social distancing guidelines and vaccination levels for a large population of rational minor agents subject to a budget-spending process. The principal anticipates the minor agents mean-field Nash equilibrium (MFNE) and selects intervention policies to minimize her own objective, inducing a bi-level optimal control problem. We define the Stackelberg mean-field game equilibrium (SMFE) and give its necessary condition by a forward-backward ordinary differential equation (FBODE) system, and provide existence and uniqueness results. We further propose an iterative fixed-point algorithm to solve the FBODE numerically and present computational observations that illustrate the resulting equilibrium behavior.

</details>


### [182] [Optimal Strategies in a Sequential Contest with Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19706)
*Alec Pannunzio,Ashley Shaffer,Gregory Shaver,Shreyas Sundaram*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立序贯竞赛的Stackelberg博弈模型，引入随努力量递增的失败概率函数及玩家异质能力与失败成本，求解跟随者最优反应、领导者预期最优策略，并分析均衡下的期望效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实竞赛中玩家投入努力时可能无法成功实现既定努力水平，且玩家在能力上限和失败成本上存在异质性；已有竞赛模型多假设努力必然实现或玩家同质，难以刻画此类不确定性对策略与结果的影响。

Method: 构建两玩家序贯竞赛的Stackelberg模型：成功投入最多努力者获胜；每个玩家的努力实现存在失败概率，由随努力量递增的失败函数刻画；各玩家具有不同失败成本与最大努力上限。先推导后手（Player 1）在观察领导者努力后的最优策略；再在预期后手最优反应下刻画先手（Player 2）期望效用最大化策略；最后计算双方均采用最优策略时的期望效用。

Result: 给出后手的最优反应策略；刻画先手在预期后手最优反应下的最优策略；得到双方采用最优策略时的期望效用，并揭示玩家能力异质性与失败成本差异如何影响博弈结果。

Conclusion: 努力实现的不确定性与玩家异质性会显著改变序贯竞赛的策略结构与均衡效用；考虑失败概率、失败成本及能力上限有助于更准确解释现实竞赛中先动优势、投入选择与结果差异。

Abstract: We study a Stackelberg model where two players compete in a sequential contest, and the player that successfully invests the most effort wins. Our formulation considers a probability that each player fails to invest their chosen level of effort, as captured by a failure function that increases with the amount of effort. The players each have their own cost of failure, as well as a maximum level of effort (capturing different abilities of the players). We prove three key results. First, we provide the optimal strategy for Player 1 (the follower), to maximize their utility given an observed effort exerted by Player 2 (the leader). Next, we characterize the optimal strategy to maximize the expected utility of Player 2, anticipating the optimal response by Player 1. Finally, we analyze the expected utilities of each player when both play their optimal strategy, and provide key insights into how heterogeneous player abilities and failure costs affect the outcomes of the game.

</details>


### [183] [(Cheap) Stochastic Policy Gradient Converges with High Probability for Linear Quadratic Regulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19712)
*Yan Li,Chengze Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究朴素随机策略梯度方法在LQR问题上的收敛性，提出一种每次迭代仅需Õ(1)环境交互、总交互复杂度为O(Polylog(1/δ)/ε)的随机无模型策略优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LQR是控制与强化学习交叉领域的经典问题。已有策略优化方法要么依赖模型、要么每次迭代计算开销大或对置信水平依赖非对数。作者希望证明朴素的随机策略梯度法在LQR上能以低计算开销、高概率收敛到ε-最优策略，并给出与LQR细节无关、可推广的收敛分析框架。

Method: 采用vanilla stochastic policy gradient方法求解LQR问题。该方法每次迭代仅需Õ(1)次与环境交互，从而支持频繁的策略改进；收敛性分析不依赖LQR的具体结构，具有一定通用性。

Result: 在保证全程稳定性的前提下，方法以概率1-δ收敛到ε-最优策略，所需总交互次数仅为O(Polylog(1/δ)/ε)。据作者所知，这是首个在每次迭代计算量为Õ(1)、且对置信水平仅有多对数依赖下以高概率收敛的LQR随机无模型策略优化方法。

Conclusion: 随机策略梯度法可在LQR上实现低成本、高概率的ε-最优收敛，且分析框架不依赖LQR特性，有望推广到更广泛的问题类别。

Abstract: We study the convergence of the vanilla stochastic policy gradient method applied to the linear quadratic regulator (LQR) problem. The method is cheap in the following sense: (1) at each iteration only $\tilde{O}(1)$ interactions with the environment are needed, therefore allowing frequent policy improvement steps, and (2) to ensure stability throughout and convergence to an $ε$-optimal policy with probability $1-δ$, only $O(\mathtt{Polylog}(1/δ)/ε)$ interactions are needed. To the best of our knowledge, this appears to be the first time that a stochastic model-free policy optimization method for LQR converges with high probability with $\tilde{O}(1)$ per-iteration computation and polylogarithmic dependence on the confidence level. The convergence analysis presented here is agnostic to LQR specifics and hence could be potentially generalized to a broader class of problems.

</details>


### [184] [Extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN and stochastic optimal controls of linear system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19779)
*Hao Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文拓展支配-单调性条件，研究带公共噪声的扩展平均场预期正倒向随机时滞微分方程（extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN）的适定性，并求解两类随机最优控制问题，给出最优控制的显式闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有支配-单调性条件仅适用于线性情形，难以保证带公共噪声的扩展平均场预期正倒向随机时滞微分方程的适定性，且在初值路径依赖等情形下控制问题存在本质困难。

Method: 将支配-单调性条件由线性推广到非线性，引入非线性伴随函数以保证 extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN 的适定性；基于该适定性框架并结合精细分析工具，研究一类线性-凸问题和一类带时变且随机输入约束的线性-二次问题。

Result: 对每一类问题证明了最优控制的存在唯一性，并给出了最优控制的显式、闭式表示。

Conclusion: 非线性伴随函数的引入扩大了适定性适用范围，为含公共噪声、时滞与路径依赖初值的随机最优控制问题提供了可求解的统一框架。

Abstract: The research studies the extended mean-field anticipated forward-backward stochastic delayed differential equations with common noise (extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN) by expanding the domination-monotonicity conditions. We generalize the conditions from a linear setting to a nonlinear one by including nonlinear adjoint functions which are key to guaranteeing the well-posedness of extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN. Utilizing this broader well-posedness framework for extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN, combined with other refined analytical tools, we examine two classes of stochastic optimal control problems. These include a linear-convex problem and a linear-quadratic problem with input constraints that can be both time-dependent and random. Moreover, the control elements regarding the initial values are path-dependent, which will cause some essential difficulties. For each problem, we establish the existence and uniqueness of optimal controls and provide their explicit, closed-form representations.

</details>


### [185] [Pairwise comparisons meet optimal transport theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19841)
*Matteo Brunelli,Silvia Lorenzini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过最优传输视角重新诠释成对比较理论，建立成对比较矩阵、双随机缩放与熵正则化传输问题之间的联系，并据此提出一致性新刻画、基于Sinkhorn的优先级导出方法及基于熵的不一致性指标，并与现有方法进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对比较理论中的一致性、优先级导出与不一致性评估通常被作为相对独立的问题处理，缺乏统一的理论框架。作者希望借助最优传输与熵正则化的成熟工具，为这些核心概念提供共同的理论基础与分析语言。

Method: 将成对比较矩阵与双随机缩放、熵正则化最优传输问题相联系，利用Sinkhorn缩放算法作为核心工具，把一致性、优先级导出与不一致性评估纳入统一的最优传输框架，并从解析与数值两方面对所提方法进行研究。

Result: 得到三项方法论成果：一致性的一种新刻画、一种基于Sinkhorn的优先级导出方法、以及一种基于熵的不一致性指标；并将这些方法与文献中的经典方法进行了分析与数值对比。

Conclusion: 成对比较理论可在最优传输框架下统一表述，Sinkhorn缩放为一致性刻画、优先级导出与不一致性度量提供了共同的计算与分析基础，为相关方法的理论理解与实证比较提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we reinterpret pairwise comparison theory through the lens of optimal transport. The main contribution is the connection established between pairwise comparison matrices, doubly stochastic scaling, and entropy-regularized transport problems. Using Sinkhorn scaling, we show how consistency, priority derivation, and inconsistency assessment can be studied within a common optimal-transport framework. As methodological consequences of this connection, we obtain a new characterization of consistency, a Sinkhorn-based prioritization method, and an entropy-based inconsistency index. We study these proposals both analytically and numerically and compare them with established methods from the literature

</details>


### [186] [Percolation Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19905)
*Melissa González García,Guillaume Vigeral*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究渗流马尔可夫决策过程（PMDPs），证明一致价值与0-最优策略的存在性，并在Bernoulli收益下给出连续性结果，最后提出降维逼近方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策者在d维整数格上反复移动代币，边收益随机且在决策过程开始前揭示，目标是最大化固定期内的平均累积收益；该模型与渗流理论存在联系，尚缺乏对一致价值、最优策略及临界阈值逼近的系统分析。

Method: 定义PMDPs模型（d维整数格、确定性转移、随机边收益、平均收益准则）；证明当期趋向无穷时一致价值存在及0-最优策略存在；针对Bernoulli收益情形分析一致价值的连续性；构造一维反例说明最优策略的复杂性；引入"维度提升"性质，用低维PMDPs逼近高维PMDPs。

Result: 一致价值（期趋于无穷）与0-最优策略均存在；Bernoulli收益下一致价值具有连续性；一维示例表明最优策略可能非常复杂；维度提升性质使得高维PMDP可由低维PMDP逼近，可用于逼近渗流理论中的临界概率阈值。

Conclusion: PMDPs在理论上具有良好的价值存在性与连续性，同时揭示最优策略的复杂性，并通过维度提升为渗流临界阈值的数值逼近提供了新途径。

Abstract: We study Percolation Markov Decision Processes (PMDPs), in which the decision maker repeatedly moves a token through the d-dimensional integer lattice with deterministic transitions and random payoffs assigned to the edges. Payoffs are revealed before the beginning of the decision process and the decision maker aims to maximize the average accumulated payoff over a fixed horizon. We establish the existence of the uniform value (as the horizon tends to infinity) and 0-optimal strategies. In the particular case of Bernoulli payoffs, we establish continuity results for the uniform value. We also present several one-dimensional examples to illustrate that optimal strategies may be very complex. Then, we introduce a dimensional lifting property that allows PMDPs to be approximated by PMDPs of lower dimension and could be used to approximate critical probability thresholds in percolation theory.

</details>


### [187] [Strategic Pricing in Electricity Markets with Pollution Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19968)
*Luce Brotcorne,Gaël Guillot,Alejandro Jofré,Benjamín Vera*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受监管的日前拍卖型电力市场新模型，允许系统运营者在最优调度中测量并限制发电商排放，证明该模型可推广既有电力市场文献中的相关模型，并用灵敏度分析量化污染变量对市场均衡的影响，附数值算例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有电力市场模型多聚焦于价格与电量出清，较少将排放约束内生化于系统运营者的最优调度之中；作者希望构建一个能刻画排放监管的拍卖型市场框架，并厘清其与已有模型的关系。

Method: 建立受监管的日前拍卖型电力市场模型，使系统运营者可在最优调度中测量并限制发电商排放；通过理论证明说明该模型是既有电力市场模型的推广；采用标准灵敏度分析工具刻画污染变量对市场均衡的影响，并给出数值算例。

Result: 理论上证明该市场模型可推广此前电力市场文献中的若干模型；灵敏度分析定量揭示了污染变量对市场均衡的影响方向与程度；数值算例展示了该效应的具体表现。

Conclusion: 将排放监管纳入拍卖型电力市场调度框架具有理论一般性与可分析性，所提模型与灵敏度分析方法可用于评估环境规制对市场均衡的影响。

Abstract: We introduce a new model for a regulated day-ahead type auction-based electrical market in which the system operator can measure and limit the producer's emissions when choosing its optimal dispatch. We prove properties of this market model that describe it as a generalization to other previous works in the electrical market literature. Furthermore, we use standard sensitivity analysis tools to measure the impact of these pollution variables on market equilibria and present numerical examples of this effect.

</details>


### [188] [Distributed Continuous-Time Optimization on the Special Orthogonal Group SO(3) over Tree Interaction Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20031)
*Dongming Wang,Wei Ren*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究无向树图上三维旋转群（SO(3)）的连续时间分布式优化：在测地强凸和测地凸操作球内，提出结合局部黎曼梯度下降与基于李群对数的signum一致性反馈的非光滑协议。通过内向边界条件保证球的不变性，构造内禀非光滑不一致性Lyapunov函数并利用树结构的簇收缩论证得到耗散界，进而给出精确有限时间一致性的增益比条件及与图结构常数无关的收敛时间估计；一致性后滑模动态为求和目标的缩放黎曼梯度流，当极小值位于球内时按测地距离指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在旋转群这类非欧几里得流形上进行分布式优化具有实际需求，但现有工作多局限于欧氏空间或一般流形，且测地强凸性不足以保证约束球的不变性。本文旨在解决树图结构下、仅凭测地强凸无法保证不变性的问题，并实现有限时间一致性。

Method: 1) 针对三维旋转群SO(3)，定义局部异质代价之和在测地凸操作球上的测地强凸优化问题；2) 设计固定增益的非光滑协议：局部黎曼梯度下降 + 利用相邻智能体相对旋转的李群对数构成的signum一致性反馈；3) 认证反例说明测地强凸不保证不变性，故施加内向边界条件，并证明所有初始在球内的Filippov解保持在该球内；4) 构造内禀非光滑不一致性Lyapunov函数，通过利用树结构的簇收缩论证导出均匀耗散界；5) 在有界局部梯度下由耗散界得到显式增益比条件，建立精确有限时间一致性并给出收敛时间估计。

Result: 1) 证明了在每个Filippov解且所有智能体初始化于球内时，解始终保持在球内（不变性）；2) 得到显式增益比条件下对每个此类解的精确有限时间一致性，并给出不依赖除智能体数量外任何图结构常数的收敛时间估计；3) 一致性达成后，识别滑模动态为求和目标的缩放黎曼梯度流；4) 当极小值位于球内部时，动态按测地距离指数收敛至该极小值；5) 仿真验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为三维旋转群上树图分布式优化提供了非光滑有限时间一致性理论，克服了测地强凸性不足以保证不变性的困难，并给出不依赖图结构常数的收敛时间结果；一致性后动态等价于缩放黎曼梯度流，内点极小值情形下呈指数收敛。

Abstract: We study continuous-time distributed optimization on the three-dimensional rotation group over undirected tree graphs, where agents with heterogeneous local costs seek a common attitude minimizing the geodesically strongly convex sum of their costs on a geodesically convex operating ball. We propose a nonsmooth protocol with fixed gains that combines local Riemannian gradient descent with signum-based consensus feedback computed from the Lie-group logarithms of relative rotations between neighboring agents. Since geodesic strong convexity alone does not guarantee invariance, as demonstrated by a certified counterexample, we impose an inward-pointing boundary condition and prove that every Filippov solution with all agents initialized in the ball remains there. We construct a nonsmooth intrinsic disagreement Lyapunov function and derive a uniform dissipation bound through a cluster-contraction argument exploiting the tree structure. With bounded local gradients, this bound gives an explicit gain-ratio condition under which we establish exact finite-time consensus for every such solution and obtain a settling-time estimate requiring no structural graph constant beyond the number of agents. After consensus, we identify the sliding dynamics as a scaled Riemannian gradient flow of the summed objective. When the minimizer lies in the interior of the ball, the dynamics converge to it exponentially in geodesic distance. Simulations illustrate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [189] [Optimal Design of Distributed Inexact Gradient Tracking: Exact Worst-case Convergence Rate and Explicit Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20265)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式优化算法设计，提出一个可确定精确最坏情况收敛速率的框架，并以DIGing算法为例给出系统化设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常只能给出充分条件和保守的收敛速率，无法刻画精确的最坏情况收敛性能，因此需要一种能确定精确最坏情况收敛速率的框架。

Method: 将最坏情况收敛定义在μ-强凸、L-Lipschitz目标函数集合与给定代数连通度的连通图集合上；聚焦分布式非精确梯度跟踪（DIGing）算法，融合图信号处理与鲁棒控制理论，提出新的分解结构以实现部分子系统解耦，并推导精确最坏情况收敛速率及相应参数的显式公式。

Result: 推导出DIGing算法精确最坏情况收敛速率及对应参数的显式表达式，数值实验验证了理论结果的正确性与有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架与设计方法能够突破传统充分条件与保守速率的局限，为分布式优化算法提供精确的最坏情况收敛分析。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of designing distributed optimization algorithms. Different to existing methods that usually give sufficient conditions and conservative convergence rates, we propose a framework for determining the exact worst-case convergence rate. The worst-case convergence is defined over the set of $μ$-strongly convex and $L$-Lipschitz objective functions and the set of connected graphs with given algebraic connectivity. Focusing on the Distributed Inexact Gradient Tracking (DIGing)--an algorithm widely deployed in applications and extensively studied in recent years, we develop a systematic design methodology by integrating techniques from graph signal processing and robust control theory. First, we present a novel decomposition structure that enables partial subsystem decoupling. Then we derive explicit formulas for the exact worst-case convergence rate and the corresponding parameters. Numerical experiments validate the correctness and effectiveness of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [190] [Continuous Dual Maximizers for Fixed-Reference Entropy-Regularized Wasserstein Barycenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20316)
*Chamila Malagoda Gamage*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 在紧度量空间与连续运输成本下，固定参考熵正则Wasserstein重心问题的对偶问题可在连续函数类中取到最大化势函数，并给出最优运输计划的存在唯一性、重心密度的显式表达及其连续性与严格正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅建立了固定参考熵正则Wasserstein重心问题的对偶形式及原始-对偶最优值相等，但值相等并不能保证对偶上确界可达，因此需要证明对偶极大元的存在性并给出完整的连续原始-对偶刻画。

Method: 在紧度量空间、连续运输成本的设定下，研究以ν_i⊗η为参考测度的熵正则重心问题；通过对偶分析证明最大化势函数存在于原连续函数类中，并利用指数原始-对偶关系由势函数恢复最优运输计划及重心密度。

Result: 证明对偶问题在连续函数类中可取到最大化势；建立最优运输计划元组与重心的存在唯一性；势函数通过指数原始-对偶关系恢复最优计划，并给出重心关于η的显式密度；该密度连续且严格正；势函数继承成本的正则性，并在自然加性规范变换下唯一。

Conclusion: 结果强化了已知的值对偶理论，为固定参考正则重心问题提供了完整的连续原始-对偶描述。

Abstract: We study the fixed-reference entropy-regularized Wasserstein barycenter problem, in which a probability measure $η$ on the barycenter space is chosen before optimization and the $i$th transport plan is regularized relative to $ν_i\otimesη$. Earlier work established a dual formulation for this model and equality between the primal and dual optimal values. However, equality of values does not by itself guarantee that the dual supremum is achieved. Our main result shows that, on compact metric spaces with continuous transport costs, the dual problem admits maximizing potentials in the original class of continuous functions. For completeness, we also establish existence and uniqueness of the optimal tuple of transport plans and of the barycenter. The maximizing potentials recover the optimal plans through exponential primal--dual relations and give an explicit density of the barycenter with respect to $η$. This density is continuous and strictly positive. We further show that the potentials inherit regularity from the costs and are unique up to the natural additive gauge transformations. These results strengthen the known value-duality theory and provide a complete continuous primal--dual description of the fixed-reference regularized barycenter problem.

</details>


### [191] [Near-Optimal Pure Single-Loop Extragradient Method for Strongly Convex--Strongly Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20327)
*Minhao Zhang,Zi Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文针对确定性无约束、强凸-强凹、非线性耦合的极小极大优化，提出一种纯单环、固定参数的阻尼外推梯度法（每步两次全梯度评估，含辅助反馈递推，无需内层求解/精度调度/分段重启），并证明其最后迭代线性收敛，查询复杂度为 O(√(κ_xκ_y)log(2κ_xκ_y/ε))，达到最优条件数阶（差对数因子），数值实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般非线性耦合的强凸-强凹极小极大优化在确定性无约束场景下，既希望算法结构简单（单环、无内层求解、无重启或精度调度），又希望达到接近最优的条件数依赖复杂度；现有方法常在实现复杂度与理论最优性之间难以兼顾，因此需要一种固定参数、显式更新即可达到最优阶的算法。

Method: 提出纯单环阻尼外推梯度法：固定参数，每次迭代在初始化查询后进行两次全梯度评估；引入辅助反馈递推以稳定外推更新；不使用内层求解、精度调度或阶段性重启。在确定性无约束设定下分析其最后迭代线性收敛性与全梯度查询复杂度，并辅以数值实验。

Result: 建立了最后迭代线性收敛；将到鞍点的平方欧氏距离降至初始值的 ε 倍所需全梯度查询数为 O(√(κ_xκ_y)log(2κ_xκ_y/ε))，其中 κ_x=L/μ_x、κ_y=L/μ_y；该界在固定显式更新的条件下达到最优条件数阶（仅差对数因子）。数值实验显示方法有效。

Conclusion: 该单环、固定参数、无内层求解的阻尼外推梯度法在一般非线性耦合的强凸-强凹极小极大优化中实现了最后迭代线性收敛，并取得接近最优的条件数依赖复杂度，兼顾了算法简洁性与理论最优阶，具有实际有效性。

Abstract: We study smooth strongly convex--strongly concave minimax optimization with general nonlinear coupling in the deterministic unconstrained setting. We propose a pure single-loop damped extragradient method with fixed parameters and two new full-gradient evaluations per iteration after one initialization query. The method uses an auxiliary feedback recursion and requires no inner solves, accuracy schedules, or staged restarts. We establish last-iterate linear convergence and show that reducing the squared Euclidean distance to the saddle point to an $\varepsilon$ fraction of its initial value requires $O(\sqrt{κ_xκ_y}\log(2κ_xκ_y/\varepsilon))$ full-gradient queries, where $κ_x=L/μ_x$ and $κ_y=L/μ_y$. This bound attains the optimal condition-number order up to logarithmic factors through fixed explicit updates. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [192] [Constraint Qualifications and Gradient Flows for Block Vanishing Constraint Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20368)
*Julian Niederer,Christoph Hansknecht,Andreas Potschka,Ekaterina Kostina*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将经典MPVC的约束品性理论推广到块消失约束数学规划（MP-BVC），证明其LICQ型条件蕴含GCQ，并基于GCQ提出分段梯度流方法，直接计算强稳定点，在桁架拓扑设计算例上验证了方法的鲁棒性与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MP-BVC因标准约束品性失效而成为一类困难的优化问题。经典MPVC（块大小为1）已有较完善的约束品性理论，但块消失约束情形下的理论尚不完整，且缺乏在较弱约束品性（如GCQ）下仍能收敛的求解算法，因此需要扩展约束品性理论并设计相应的收敛算法。

Method: 1) 以经典MPVC理论为基础，将约束品性理论推广至一般块大小的MP-BVC；2) 证明MP-BVC的LICQ型条件可推出Guignard约束品性（GCQ）；3) 提出一种新的分段梯度流方法，该方法是经典MPVC分段梯度流方法的推广，在GCQ下具有收敛性，并直接计算强稳定点。

Result: 理论上，LICQ型条件被证明蕴含GCQ，且所提分段梯度流方法在GCQ下收敛并直接得到强稳定点。数值上，在施加两个甚至三个载荷力的桁架拓扑设计算例中，该方法表现出良好的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将MPVC的约束品性与算法理论系统地推广到块消失约束情形，为MP-BVC提供了更弱的可验证约束品性条件和具有收敛保证的求解方法，并通过多载荷桁架拓扑设计实例验证了其实用性。

Abstract: Mathematical Programs with Blocks of Vanishing Constraints (MP-BVCs) are a challenging class of optimization problems due to the loss of standard constraint qualifications. Based on existing theory for classical MPVCs, i.e., MPBVC with block size one, we extend the constraint qualification theory for MPBVCs and show that the known LICQ-type condition for MPBVCs implies the Guignard Constraint Qualification (GCQ). A novel piecewise gradient flow approach converges under GCQ. It directly computes strongly stationary points and is an extension of the piecewise gradient flow approach for classic MPVC problems. Numerical experiments on several truss topology design instances with two and even three applied load forces demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [193] [Geometry and Convergence of Quadratically Regularized Optimal Transport I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20400)
*Alberto González-Sanz,Marcel Nutz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了二次代价下二次正则化最优传输在小正则化 regime 的尖锐收敛速率，量化了正则化优化器支集的稀疏性，并给出对偶势的 Hessian 双边界与一致收敛速率，同时确定了收敛性失效的正则性阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二次正则化最优传输在小正则化参数 ε 下的收敛行为，特别是刻画正则化解的稀疏性、支集几何与对偶势的收敛速率，并寻找收敛性失效的正则性阈值。

Method: 针对 R^d 中光滑边缘密度、二次代价的二次正则化最优传输，分析正则化优化器支集与 Brenier 图之间的距离，研究支集每个截面被同阶半径球夹逼的性质，并建立对偶势的一致双边 Hessian 界及一致收敛估计。

Result: 正则化优化器的支集位于 Brenier 图距离 O(ε^{1/(d+2)}) 范围内，支集每个截面被半径同阶的球夹逼；对偶势满足一致双边 Hessian 界，以 ε^{2/(d+2)} 速率一致收敛，其梯度以 ε^{1/(d+2)} 速率收敛；这些速率是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 二次正则化最优传输在小正则化下呈现由 ε^{1/(d+2)} 控制的尖锐收敛与稀疏结构，且对偶势的正则性存在明确阈值，超过该阈值收敛性失效。

Abstract: We establish sharp convergence rates for quadratically regularized optimal transport with quadratic cost in the regime of small regularization $\varepsilon$. In particular, we quantify the sparsity of the support of the regularized optimizer. For smooth marginal densities in $\mathbb{R}^d$, this support lies within a distance of order $\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$ from the Brenier graph, and every section of the support is sandwiched between balls with radii of that order. The geometry of the support is closely linked to the dual potentials. We show that the potentials satisfy uniform two-sided Hessian bounds and converge uniformly at rate $\varepsilon^{2/(d+2)}$, while their gradients converge at rate $\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$. The rates are sharp, and we further identify the regularity threshold where convergence breaks down.

</details>


### [194] [Convergence rate of the moment-SOS hierarchy for univariate polynomial optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20544)
*Didier Henrion,Mohab Safey El Din*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一元多项式优化问题在矩-SOS（平方和）层级方法下的收敛速率，证明对固定的一元问题，松弛误差为 O(1/r²)，并构造四元多项式例子说明二次速率最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩-SOS层级是多项式优化的重要方法，但其收敛速率（尤其是收敛指数）依赖可行集描述的代数结构。边界退化是否降低收敛速率（指数）此前并不清楚，作者希望精确刻画一元情形下的收敛速率并判断其最优性。

Method: 证明结合了两类工具：一是有限生成一元二次模的结构分析；二是Chebyshev多项式构造，用以近似恢复可行集的自然生成元并控制证书次数。此外，通过等价变形把四元例子分别联系到一个三次一元问题和一个可行集在极小点处具有尖点奇异的二元POP。

Result: 对任意固定的一元POP（可行集为实轴上由任意多项式不等式描述的有界子集），第 r 阶松弛误差为 O(1/r²)；可行集多项式描述中的边界退化只影响常数而不改变收敛指数。构造的四元例子松弛误差恰为 1/(2r(r-1))，表明二次速率不可改进。

Conclusion: 一元矩-SOS层级具有普适的二次收敛速率，边界退化不损害收敛指数；且该速率在一般情形下是最优的。

Abstract: We study the convergence rate of the moment-SOS (sum-of-squares) hierarchy for polynomial optimization problems (POPs) on a bounded subset of the real line described by arbitrary polynomial inequalities. We prove that, for every fixed univariate POP, the relaxation error is bounded by $O(1/r^2)$, where $r$ is the relaxation order. In particular, boundary degeneracies in the polynomial description of the feasible set affect the constant but not the convergence exponent. The proof combines the structure of finitely generated univariate quadratic modules with a Chebyshev polynomial construction that approximately recovers the natural generators of the feasible set while controlling the degree of the certificate. We also give an elementary degree-four example for which the relaxation error is exactly $1/(2r(r-1))$, showing that the quadratic rate is optimal. Equivalent reformulations connect this example to a cubic univariate problem and to a bivariate POP whose feasible set has a cusp singularity at the minimizer.

</details>


### [195] [Complexity Of Output Feedback Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20636)
*Amir Ali Ahmadi,Abraar Chaudhry,Ijay Narang,Yukai Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了除非 P = NP，否则线性动力系统的输出反馈镇定（使用线性控制器）不存在多项式时间甚至伪多项式时间算法，从而解决了控制理论中一个著名公开问题，且结论在连续与离散时间下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输出反馈镇定是控制理论中最基础且重要的问题之一，其可计算复杂度长期未明。作者希望厘清该问题的计算复杂性，明确其是否属于可在多项式时间内求解的范畴。

Method: 通过计算复杂性理论（特别是 P 与 NP 的关系）进行归约论证，从复杂度角度分析线性控制器下输出反馈镇定问题的可解性；论证覆盖连续时间与离散时间两种情形。

Result: 证明除非 P = NP，否则不存在多项式时间或伪多项式时间算法能求解线性控制器下的输出反馈镇定问题；并构造出一类可镇定的线性动力系统，其镇定控制器无法在多项式时间内以标准表示形式写出。

Conclusion: 输出反馈镇定问题在计算上是困难的（NP-hard 意义上），无法期待通用高效算法；这为此前控制理论中关于该问题的复杂性猜想给出了确切的否定性答案。

Abstract: We show that unless P = NP, there cannot be a polynomial-time (or even pseudo-polynomial-time) algorithm for output feedback stabilization of a linear dynamical system with a linear controller. This settles one of the best-known open problems in control theory. The result holds in both continuous and discrete time. We also present a family of stabilizable linear dynamical systems for which no polynomial-time algorithm can write down a stabilizing controller in its standard representation.

</details>


### [196] [The First-Order Oracle Complexity of Lipschitz Convex Optimization in Nondual Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20687)
*David Martínez-Rubio,Brian Bullins,Cristóbal Guzmán,Mathieu Molina*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究ℓ_p球上一阶黑盒凸优化（目标在ℓ_q范数下Lipschitz），正面解决了COLT开放问题（Guz15b）的非光滑版本——较小可行集几何（p<q）能否改进凸优化收敛率，并将结果匹配已有下界至对数因子。核心技术是一个新的在线学习博弈，其比较器由至今观测到的仿射损失的最大值评估，博弈值用序列fat-shattering维刻画。结果还给出若干Banach几何中样本凸包到均值期望距离的估计，即Wendel定理的定量、有界一般分布版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决COLT开放问题（Guz15b）的非光滑版本：当可行集几何更"小"（p<q）时，凸优化收敛率是否能改进，并期望匹配已知下界。同时推进Sridharan（Sri12）提出的基础性问题。

Method: 提出一个新的在线学习博弈，其中比较器由至今观测到的仿射损失的最大值来评估；利用组合在线学习量——序列fat-shattering维——对该博弈值进行上界与下界估计，并刻画ℓ_p/ℓ_q情形下的该维度。方法适用于可行集X与可能次梯度集H为凸、中心对称且满足某类minmax定理的情形。

Result: 对ℓ_1球上凸的Euclidean-Lipschitz优化取得Õ(1/T)速率，改进了通用假设下的经典O(1/√T)速率；总体收敛率与已有下界匹配至对数因子。另外给出若干Banach几何中样本凸包到其均值期望距离的估计，即Wendel定理的定量版本，适用于有界一般分布而非仅中心对称分布。

Conclusion: 证实了更小可行集的几何可改进凸优化收敛率，正面回答COLT开放问题的非光滑版本；所提在线学习博弈框架与序列fat-shattering维刻画是关键工具，并推进了Sridharan的基础性问题，同时得到具独立价值的几何结果。

Abstract: We study first-order black-box convex optimization over an $\ell_p$-ball for objectives Lipschitz in the $\ell_q$-norm, solving in the affirmative the nonsmooth version of the COLT open question (Guz15b) on whether the geometry of a smaller feasible set ($p < q$) can improve convergence rates in convex optimization, and matching prior lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. Our rates include \(\widetilde O(1/T)\) for convex Euclidean-Lipschitz optimization over the $\ell_1$-ball, improving on the $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ classical rate under general assumptions. The key technical device is a new online learning game, where the comparator is evaluated using the maximum of affine losses observed so far. We bound the value of this game above and below in terms of a combinatorial online learning quantity: the sequential fat-shattering dimension, which we characterize for the $\ell_p / \ell_q$ case. Our results generally apply when the feasible set $X$ and the set of possible subgradients $H$ are convex, centrally symmetric, and admit a type of minmax theorem, advancing on a fundamental question by Sridharan [Sri12, Section 10.1.2, Q3]. As a geometric consequence of our analysis, of independent interest, we obtain estimates for the expected distance of a convex hull of samples to their mean in several Banach geometries, a version of the celebrated Wendel's theorem (Wen62), but quantitative and for bounded general distributions as opposed to centrally symmetric ones.

</details>


### [197] [Stable Movement for Nondual Lipschitz Convex Optimization: Efficiency and Nearly Optimal Oracle Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20701)
*David Martínez-Rubio,Cristóbal Guzmán*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究$\ell_p$球上$G$-Lipschitz凸函数优化的最优一阶预言机复杂度，通过将凸优化归约为chasing嵌套凸集问题，并提出"稳定中心"新概念，首次高效实现了 $p<q$ 时的近乎最优收敛率，解决了COLT 2015开放问题的非光滑端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COLT 2015 提出的开放问题中，非光滑端（$p<q$ 情形）的最优一阶预言机复杂度此前仅知下界而缺乏高效实现。已有最优率（MBG+26）未被算法有效达到，尤其是 $\ell_1$ 球上欧氏 Lipschitz 的 $O(GR/T)$ 速率是否可实现存疑。

Method: 将凸 Lipschitz 优化归约为 evolving bundle 子水平集上的 chasing nested convex sets 问题（LNN95; BBE+20）：每次查询要么找到低函数值点，要么在子水平集上产生深割，然后对割进行"追逐"。利用选择器的稳定性与被深割强制移动之间的二分性来界定迭代次数；对 $R B_p^d$ 的嵌套子集引入"稳定中心"新概念，其 $\ell_q$ 范数下的移动被 $\widetilde O_{p,q}(RT^{1-1/p+(1/q-1/2)_+})$ 界住。对选择器做 Monte Carlo 平均，以高概率达到近最优率，并在实算术模型多项式时间内实现。

Result: 对 $p<q$，$T$ 次查询后误差为 $\widetilde O_{p,q}(GR/T^{1/p-(1/q-1/2)_+})$，高效实现了近乎最优速率；特别地，$p=1,q=2$（$\ell_1$ 球上欧氏 Lipschitz）时速率 $\widetilde O(GR/T)$。所提稳定中心的移动界在高维下近乎最优；Monte Carlo 平均选择器以高概率达到近最优率。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了 COLT 2015 开放问题的非光滑端，证明了 $G$-Lipschitz 凸函数在上述范数配置下的最优预言机复杂度可被高效、多项式时间实现，为 chasing 方法与稳定中心分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study efficient algorithms for realizing the first-order oracle complexity of optimization of $G$-Lipschitz convex functions with respect to the $\ell_{q}$-norm over an $\ell_{p}$-ball of radius $R$, where $1\leq p,q\leq \infty$. For $p<q$, we obtain error $\widetilde{O}_{p,q}(GR/T^{1/p-(1/q-1/2)_{+}})$ after $T$ oracle queries, efficiently realizing the nearly optimal rates of (MBG+26), thereby resolving the nonsmooth end of the COLT 2015 open problem (Guz15b). In particular, the rate is $\widetilde{O}(GR/T)$ for Euclidean Lipschitzness over an $\ell_1$-ball of radius $R$ ($p=1,q=2$). Our solution consists of reducing convex Lipschitz optimization to the chasing nested convex sets problem in sublevel sets of an evolving bundle (LNN95; BBE+20): at each query we either find a point with low function value or we produce a deep cut in the current sublevel of the bundle, that we chase. The dichotomy between stability of selectors and forced movement by deep cuts bounds the number of iterations of the algorithm near optimally. For nested subsets of $R B_{p}^{d}$, we introduce a novel notion of stable center whose movement is bounded by $\widetilde{O}_{p,q}(RT^{1-1/p+(1/q-1/2)_{+}})$ in the $\ell_{q}$-norm after $T$ steps, which we show is nearly optimal in high dimensions. A Monte Carlo average of the proposed selector achieves near-optimal rates with high probability and can be implemented in polynomial time for our optimization algorithm in the real-arithmetic model.

</details>
