# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Bad metallicity in the semi-quantum regime of the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19264)
*Evyatar Tulipman,Vadim Oganesyan,Thomas P. Devereaux,Steven A. Kivelson*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在无限U哈伯德模型小空穴密度下，精确对角化揭示了一个电子版"半量子区"，其中压缩率近似T无关而电阻率呈T线性并超过Ioffe-Regel极限，伴随掺杂空穴周围形成准局域铁磁"自旋笼"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坏金属表现出超出Ioffe-Regel极限的近似T线性直流电阻率，且该行为出现在基态迥异的多种系统中，暗示它是强局域关联的普适表现。作者希望检验这一假设。

Method: 采用无限U哈伯德模型，在较小空穴密度下通过精确对角化方法计算有限团簇的热力学与输运性质。

Result: 降温过程中发现一个中间温区，类似于液氦的"半量子区"电子类比物：量子效应产生近似T无关的压缩率，但电阻率呈T线性且超过Ioffe-Regel极限。进入该温区伴随着掺杂空穴周围形成准局域铁磁"自旋笼"，这些区域在涨落自旋背景中促成局域量子运动。

Conclusion: 尽管模型简单，所发现的坏金属行为与多种材料平台中观察到的行为相似，支持坏金属行为是强局域关联普适表现的假设。

Abstract: Bad metals exhibit approximately $T$-linear dc resistivity beyond the Ioffe--Regel limit. That this behavior occurs in systems with radically different ground states suggests it is a generic manifestation of strong local correlations. We test this hypothesis in the infinite-$U$ Hubbard model with small hole densities using exact diagonalization to compute thermodynamic and transport properties of finite clusters. Upon cooling, we find an intermediate temperature range, an electronic analogue of the ``semi-quantum regime'' of liquid helium, in which quantum effects produce a roughly $T$-independent compressibility, yet the resistivity is $T$-linear and exceeds the Ioffe--Regel limit. Entry into this regime is accompanied by the formation of quasi-local ferromagnetic ``spin cages'' around doped holes, regions that facilitate local quantum motion embedded in a fluctuating spin background, analogous to the transient crystalline cages thought to control incoherent transport in semi-quantum liquid helium. Remarkably, despite the simplicity of the model, the bad metal behavior found here resembles that seen in various material platforms.

</details>


### [199] [Non-Abelian Anyon Condensation: a Path-Integral Monte Carlo Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19282)
*Rafael Flores-Calderón,Frank Pollmann,Michael Knap*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作为Kitaev量子双模型D(G)发展了一套无符号问题的路径积分框架，通过非可逆1-形式对称性组织单键微扰，并借助matterization等距映射将其转化为G规范-希格斯理论；以G=S_3为例，量子蒙特卡洛揭示了非阿贝尔电任意子增殖导致的一阶相变与纯磁极限下的一阶禁闭相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔拓扑序的相变难以用可扩展的微观数值方法描述，尤其是任意子凝聚与禁闭过程缺乏统一、可控且在大尺度上可行的计算框架。

Method: 针对Kitaev量子双D(G)，以非可逆电/磁1-形式对称性组织单键微扰；对任意有限群G构造精确的matterization等距，引入顶点自由度，将纯链模型映射为G规范-希格斯理论；利用不动点的1-形式对称性定义广义Fredenhagen-Marcu序参量以刻画任意子凝聚；对G=S_3进行量子蒙特卡洛模拟，并分析磁极限下的纯G规范理论、Wilson圈面积律与涌现磁1-形式对称性。

Result: 所提框架无符号问题；当非阿贝尔电任意子增殖时，G=S_3系统发生一阶相变，所有非平凡电任意子同时凝聚；在纯磁极限下模型退化为(2+1)维纯G规范理论，G=S_3表现为一阶禁闭相变，由Wilson圈面积律的出现及涌现磁1-形式对称性的恢复所诊断。

Conclusion: 该研究为非阿贝尔任意子凝聚、禁闭以及广义对称性破缺提供了一个统一的数值框架。

Abstract: Transitions out of non-Abelian topological order are difficult to describe in microscopic quantum models with numerical methods that remain tractable at large scales. We develop a sign-free path-integral framework for Kitaev quantum doubles $\mathcal D(G)$ by organizing single-link perturbations in terms of non-invertible electric and magnetic 1-form symmetries that proliferate distinct anyon species. For any finite group $G$, an exact $\textit{matterization}$ isometry introduces vertex degrees of freedom and maps the link-only model onto a $G$ gauge--Higgs theory. Furthermore, the 1-form symmetries of the fixed point allow us to define generalized Fredenhagen--Marcu order parameters that become finite when the corresponding anyons condense. For $G = S_3$, quantum Monte Carlo simulations show that proliferating a non-Abelian electric anyon drives a first-order transition in which all nontrivial electric anyons condense. In the purely magnetic limit, the model reduces to a $(2+1)$D pure $G$ gauge theory; for $G=S_3$, it exhibits a first-order confinement transition, diagnosed by the onset of a Wilson-loop area law and the restoration of an emergent magnetic 1-form symmetry. These results provide a unified numerical framework for non-Abelian anyon condensation, confinement, and generalized symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [200] [Valley-Helical Superconductivity Driven by Repulsion and Quantum Geometry: Applications to Time-Reversal Symmetric Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19283)
*Julian May-Mann,Trithep Devakul,Gal Shavit*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究在无能谷极化、无时间反演对称性破缺条件下实现手性配对的可能性，发现过屏蔽排斥（Kohn-Luttinger机制）可在两谷中诱导相反手性的配对，形成谷螺旋态，并在中等耦合下超越传统的谷间配对态；在菱形石墨烯模型中，4≤N≤8均存在占主导地位的谷螺旋超导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期在谷极化石墨烯及过渡金属硫族化合物体系中观测到手性超导的实验信号，作者希望探究在既无谷极化也无时间反演对称性破缺的情况下是否仍能实现手性配对。

Method: 针对两谷具有相反贝里曲率的系统，采用Kohn-Luttinger（过屏蔽排斥）机制分析配对不稳定性，并构建时间反演对称的菱形石墨烯实际模型进行计算。

Result: 过屏蔽排斥可在两谷中诱导出相反手性的配对，形成谷螺旋态；该态在中等耦合下可胜过传统的谷间配对态，尽管后者才具有弱耦合不稳定性。在时间反演对称菱形石墨烯模型中，谷螺旋超导在较宽的填充与位移场范围内占主导，且适用于4≤N≤8的所有层数。

Conclusion: 谷螺旋态具有拓扑投影边缘模、非常规磁响应及交织的Kekulé型键序等特征，可作为将其与更常规超导体区分开的实验判据。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent experimental signatures of chiral superconductivity in valley-polarized graphene- and transition-metal-dichalcogenide-based systems, we investigate the possibility of chiral pairing in the absence of valley polarization or time-reversal symmetry breaking. For systems with opposite Berry curvature in the two valleys, we find that overscreened repulsion (i.e., the Kohn-Luttinger mechanism) can indeed induce pairing of opposite chiralities in each valley, producing a valley-helical state. Moreover, the valley-helical state can outcompete more conventional intervalley paired states at intermediate coupling, despite only the latter having a weak-coupling instability. Using a realistic model of time-reversal-symmetric rhombohedral graphene, we find extended ranges of fillings and displacement fields with dominant valley-helical superconductivity. This occurs for all layer numbers considered here, $4\leq N\leq 8$. We show that the valley-helical states can display topologically projected edge modes, unusual magnetic responses, and intertwined Kekulé-like bond-order; features that can be used to distinguish them from more conventional superconductors.

</details>


### [201] [Energy partitioning of wave packets in one-dimensional systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19450)
*Flavia B. Ramos,Imke Schneider,Sebastian Eggert,Rodrigo G. Pereira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究一维临界费米子系统中等能波包的非平衡动力学，观察到相互作用导致激发分数化为反向传播的左右模式，并利用Luttinger液体理论解释能量分配，发现能量分配是非普适的，依赖于波包细节如宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一维临界费米子系统中波包的非平衡动力学，特别是能量在新兴激发之间的分配机制，并与电荷分数化的普适性进行对比。

Method: 从多体基态上注入高斯波包，使用时间依赖密度矩阵重正化群（tDMRG）追踪实时演化；采用Luttinger液体理论推导新兴模式所携带能量的解析预测。

Result: 相互作用导致初始激发分数化为反向传播的左右移动模式，其能量可在实空间分辨；低能区场论预测与数值模拟吻合良好；与完全由Luttinger液体参数决定的电荷分数化不同，能量分配是非普适的，依赖于注入波包的宽度等细节。

Conclusion: 研究提供了能量分配在一维系统中的实空间表征，并建立了非平衡数值模拟与有效场论描述之间的定量比较。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium dynamics of wave packets in a one-dimensional critical fermionic system and analyze the resulting partitioning of energy between emergent excitations. Starting from a Gaussian wave packet injected on top of the many-body ground state, we follow its real-time evolution using the time-dependent density-matrix renormalization group. We observe that interactions lead to fractionalization of the initial excitation into counter-propagating left- and right-moving modes, whose energies can be resolved in real space. To interpret these results, we employ Luttinger liquid theory, which allows us to derive analytical predictions for the energy carried by the emergent modes. We find good agreement between field-theoretical predictions and numerical simulations in the low-energy regime. In contrast to charge fractionalization, which is completely determined by the Luttinger liquid parameter, we show that energy partitioning is non-universal and depends on details of the injected wave packet, such as its width. Our results provide a real-space characterization of energy partitioning in one-dimensional systems and establish a quantitative comparison between non-equilibrium numerical simulations and the effective field-theory description.

</details>


### [202] [Universality of the $1/9$ Magnetization Plateau and Quantum-Disordered States in the Kagome Family $\mathrm{Cs_8AB_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ ($A=\mathrm{Rb},\mathrm{Li}$; $B=\mathrm{K},\mathrm{Na}$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19572)
*Prena Chaudhary,Asiri Thennakoon,Tommy Park,Hanru Wang,Leshan Zhao,Laurel Winter,Neil Herrison,Christina Hoffmann,Junghong H. He,Harald O. Jeschke,Hiroyuki Nojiri,Akira Matsuo,Koichi Kindo,Miwako Takahashi,Yukio Noda,Taku J. Sato,Shiyan Li,Hiroaki Ueda,Gia-Wei Chern,Seung-Hun Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该摘要研究钛基笼目反铁磁体Cs₈AB₃Ti₁₂F₄₈中化学压力对分数磁化平台层级结构的调控，发现晶格膨胀下1/9平台稳健存在而1/3平台缺失，压缩下两者均消失，表明1/9与1/3平台微观起源不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-1/2笼目反铁磁体中低场1/9磁化平台的微观起源长期未解决，需要阐明其与更稳健的1/3平台是否共享同一机制。

Method: 对Cs₈AB₃Ti₁₂F₄₈（A=Rb,Li; B=K,Na）系列进行最高60 T的高场磁化测量，结合比热测量与第一性原理计算，对比晶格膨胀与压缩样品的交换网络和基态性质。

Result: 膨胀的Cs₈RbK₃Ti₁₂F₄₈与Cs₈LiK₃Ti₁₂F₄₈中出现稳健的1/9平台样相，但1/3平台缺失；压缩的Cs₈LiNa₃Ti₁₂F₄₈既无1/9平台样相也无量子无序基态。比热与第一性原理计算表明：晶格膨胀保持阻挫、全连接的笼目交换网络及无能隙量子无序基态，而压缩使交换网络重组为弱耦合准一维子系统并诱发连续磁相变。

Conclusion: 1/9与1/3平台无需共享同一微观起源，1/9平台可能是阻挫自旋-1/2笼目磁性的更普适特征。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the low-field $1/9$ magnetization plateau in spin-$1/2$ kagome antiferromagnets remains unresolved. Here, we show that chemical pressure reshapes the hierarchy of fractional magnetization plateaus in the titanium-based kagome family $\mathrm{Cs_8AB_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ ($A=\mathrm{Rb},\mathrm{Li}$; $B=\mathrm{K},\mathrm{Na}$). High-field magnetization measurements up to 60 T reveal a robust $1/9$ plateau-like phase in the expanded $\mathrm{Cs_8RbK_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ and $\mathrm{Cs_8LiK_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ compounds, despite the absence of the conventionally more robust $1/3$ plateau. In contrast, compressed $\mathrm{Cs_8LiNa_3Ti_{12}F_{48}}$ exhibits neither the $1/9$ plateau-like phase nor a quantum-disordered ground state. Specific-heat measurements and first-principles calculations show that lattice expansion preserves a frustrated, fully connected kagome exchange network and gapless quantum-disordered ground states, whereas compression reorganizes the exchange network into weakly coupled quasi-one-dimensional subsystems and induces successive magnetic transitions. These results demonstrate that the $1/9$ and $1/3$ plateaus need not share a common microscopic origin and suggest that the $1/9$ plateau may represent a more universal feature of frustrated spin-$1/2$ kagome magnetism.

</details>


### [203] [Vestigial chirality from fluctuating loop currents on the kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19618)
*Yin Shi,Xuande Bu,Sheng Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟与Ginzburg-Landau分析，研究kagome晶格上三Q环流序的分步熔化，确立了残余环流序的畴壁判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分量序可能分阶段熔化，在主分量变为短程后留下复合序。作者希望探究kagome晶格上公度三Q环流序是否存在这种分步熔化，并为近期在kagome金属中观测到的、出现在常规电荷密度波临界温度之上的时间反演奇响应寻找热力学框架。

Method: 在有效固定振幅符号模型上进行大规模集团蒙特卡洛与并行回火蒙特卡洛模拟，结合有限尺寸标定的振幅分辨Ginzburg-Landau理论进行分析。

Result: 模拟揭示了直接熔化与两步熔化两种机制。两步机制中，平移扇区畴壁先于手性改变畴壁增殖，从而恢复晶格平移对称性同时保留长程时间反演奇序，中间相中主要M点关联为短程。当最低能手性改变畴壁仅比同手性平移畴壁贵约12%–13%时，两步机制开始。有限尺寸标定表明该相在振幅弛豫下依然稳定。

Conclusion: 结果建立了残余环流序的定量畴壁判据，并给出无需长程环流布拉格序即可破缺时间反演对称性的涨落路径，为kagome金属中常规电荷密度波有序温度之上的时间反演奇响应提供了可能的热力学解释。

Abstract: Multicomponent order can melt in stages, leaving a composite order after its primary constituents become short ranged. We study this possibility for commensurate three-$Q$ loop-current order on the kagome lattice. Large-scale cluster and parallel-tempering Monte Carlo simulations reveal direct and two-stage melting regimes in an effective fixed-amplitude sign model. In the latter regime, translation-sector domain walls proliferate before chirality-changing walls, restoring lattice translational symmetry while preserving long-range time-reversal-odd order. The primary $M$-point correlations are short ranged in this intermediate phase. The two-stage regime begins when the lowest-energy chirality-changing wall is only about $12$--$13\%$ more costly than a same-chirality translation wall. Finite-size scaling of a stable amplitude-resolved Ginzburg--Landau theory shows that the phase survives amplitude relaxation. Our results establish a quantitative domain-wall criterion for vestigial loop-current order and a fluctuation route to time-reversal symmetry breaking without long-range loop-current Bragg order. This separation provides a possible thermodynamic framework for time-reversal-odd responses recently reported above the critical temperature for conventional charge-density-wave ordering in kagome metals.

</details>


### [204] [Fractional quantum spin Hall crystals from hidden density-wave order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19638)
*Matthew Shammami,Sudip Chakravarty*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出对称破缺与拓扑可互为因果：混合单-三重态d密度波（粒子-空穴凝聚体）具有隐藏键序，破坏平移对称性并生成一对时间反演Chern带，但不产生常规电荷或自旋密度序；在奇数分母分数填充下，排斥相互作用可将该对称破缺拓扑态转变为分数量子自旋霍尔液体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称破缺与拓扑通常被视为描述物质自组织的两种不同框架，作者希望探究二者之间的关系，提出其中之一可以衍生出另一个，并寻找可实现该机制的物理凝聚态体系。

Method: 引入混合单-三重态d密度波（粒子-空穴凝聚体）作为候选态，分析其隐藏键序对平移对称性和能带拓扑的影响；在奇数分母分数填充下采用有限尺寸精确对角化方法研究排斥相互作用的作用；并考察不同填充和自旋耦合强度下的相图边界。

Result: 该凝聚体破坏平移对称性并生成时间反演成对的Chern带，而无常规电荷/自旋密度序；奇数分母分数填充下排斥相互作用可将其转化为分数量子自旋霍尔液体，其相反手性产生电荷流输运自旋、自旋流输运电荷的交叉响应；强自旋间耦合和更稀填充倾向电荷序，残余能带色散变得关键；半填充自旋解耦极限给出均质关联液体，其信号与复合费米液体物理一致。

Conclusion: 对称破缺态可作为拓扑态的起源，隐藏键序的d密度波为分数量子自旋霍尔液体及相关交叉响应提供了母态，同时界定了该拓扑相的相邻相（电荷序、复合费米液体），揭示了对称破缺与拓扑之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Broken symmetry and topology are distinct descriptions for how matter organizes itself. Here, we propose one gives rise to the other. A mixed singlet-triplet $d$-density wave, which is a particle-hole condensate, has a hidden bond order that breaks translation symmetry and generates a time-reversed pair of Chern bands, without producing conventional charge or spin density order. At fractional fillings with odd denominators, finite size exact diagonalization studies show how repulsive interactions can transform this symmetry-broken topological state into a fractional quantum spin Hall liquid. Its opposite chiralities produce a crossed response in which charge flux transports spin, and spin flux transports charge. The same setting also reveals what borders such a phase. Strong interspin coupling and increasingly dilute fillings favor charge order and make residual band dispersion decisive, while at half filling, the spin-decoupled limit gives a homogeneous correlated liquid with signatures consistent with the physics of a composite Fermi liquid.

</details>


### [205] [Pressure induced structural phase transition and magneto-elastic coupling in Eu doped LaCrO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19795)
*Asish Kumar Mishra,Mrinmay Sahu,Bhagyashri Giri,Bidisha Mukherjee,Suvashree Mukherjee,Harekrishna Bhunia,Tamalkanti Mukherjee,Sujoy Ghosh,Partha Mitra,Peter Liermann,Goutam Dev Mukherjee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对5% Eu掺杂LaCrO₃(ELCO)进行高压XRD、拉曼光谱与低温磁化研究，发现~10.6 GPa正交→菱方结构相变，~4.5 GPa出现拉曼异常，并预测压力诱导磁基态转变为反铁磁有序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立ELCO在压力下的结构与磁性关联，并借助与纯LaCrO₃(LCO)的对比，阐明Eu掺杂及压力对磁基态的影响。

Method: 高压同步辐射X射线衍射(XRD)、显微拉曼光谱、低温磁化测量。

Result: 高压XRD显示约10.6 GPa发生正交→菱方结构相变；高压拉曼佐证该结果，表明低频软模相关的晶格不稳定性驱动该相变；多个拉曼模的位移与积分强度在约4.5 GPa出现明显异常。

Conclusion: 结合低温磁化与XRD、拉曼异常，并与LaCrO₃结果对比，预测ELCO在压力下磁基态转变为反铁磁有序。

Abstract: In this study, we have carried out a detailed high pressure investigation on 5$\%$ Eu doped LaCrO$_3$ (ELCO) using synchrotron X-ray diffraction (XRD), micro Raman spectroscopy, and low-temperature magnetization measurements to correlate the structural and magnetic properties under pressure. The high pressure XRD reveals the orthorhombic to rhombohedral structural phase transition around 10.6 GPa. The high pressure Raman data corroborate this result and indicate that the lattice instabilities associated with the low-frequency soft modes play a crucial role in driving this transition. In addition, a pronounced anomaly in the Raman shift and integrated intensity of several Raman modes is observed around 4.5 GPa. By combining the low-temperature magnetization measurements with the anomalies observed in the XRD and Raman data and by comparing with the similar results in LaCrO$_3$ (LCO), a pressure-induced change in the magnetic ground state to antiferromagnetic ordering is predicted.

</details>


### [206] [Tunable flat band on the surface of a rhombohedral kagome ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19857)
*Xinglu Que,Qingyu He,Lihui Zhou,Lilian Prodan,Vladimir Tsurkan,István Kézsmárki,Hidenori Takagi,Dennis Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在Kagome铁磁体Fe₃Sn₂表面观测到近费米能的平带，其成因并非层内跳跃的破坏性干涉，而是菱方堆垛Kagome层间的层间跳跃，该表面平带具有丰富的磁场依赖行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome材料研究的核心目标之一是获得带宽为meV量级且靠近费米能的平带。主流观点认为平带源于Kagome晶格上层内跳跃的破坏性干涉（量子相消）。作者希望厘清Fe₃Sn₂中平带的真实来源与空间分布。

Method: 对层状Kagome铁磁体Fe₃Sn₂进行扫描隧道显微镜（STM）测量，获取微分隧道电导谱；结合第一性原理平板（slab）计算，分析能带色散的来源与空间局域性，并在磁场下研究其演化。

Result: STM在费米能附近观测到平带，表现为微分隧道电导中的尖锐峰。第一性原理计算表明该平带并非由层内跳跃的破坏性干涉致平，而是由Fe₃Sn₂中菱方堆垛Kagome面之间的层间跳跃所致，且局域于表面层，形成于布里渊区角点K̄与K̄′附近。该表面带表现出丰富的磁场依赖：因旋转Fe磁矩破坏谷对称性而产生精细结构，并伴有与轨道磁矩相关的持续抗磁位移，计算结果与实验一致。

Conclusion: 层堆垛方式对Kagome磁体能带结构具有关键作用；本文实验上展示了一种在拓扑磁体原子级薄层中产生磁可调平带的替代机制。

Abstract: A central goal in the exploration of kagome-based materials is the realization of a flat band that has meV bandwidth and lies close to the Fermi energy. The prevailing assumption is that band flattening originates from destructive hopping processes on the kagome lattice. We perform scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) on the layered kagome ferromagnet Fe$_3$Sn$_2$ and show that it indeed hosts a flat band near the Fermi energy, which is manifested as a sharp peak in the differential tunneling conductance. First-principles slab calculations reveal, however, that this band is flattened not by the destructive interference of intralayer hopping, but by interlayer hopping between rhombohedral-stacked kagome planes in Fe$_3$Sn$_2$, and is confined to the surface layer. This surface band, forming in the vicinity of the Brillouin zone corners $\bar{K}$ and $\bar{K'}$, exhibits rich magnetic-field dependence, including fine structure due to valley-symmetry breaking by rotated Fe moments, as well as a persistent diamagnetic shift associated with orbital magnetic moments, all reproduced by our calculations. Our results, highlighting the crucial role of layer stacking on the band structure of kagome magnets, demonstrate experimentally an alternative mechanism of generating magnetically tunable flat bands in atomically thin volumes of topological magnets.

</details>


### [207] [Corner entanglement scaling with projected entangled pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20020)
*Chloé Van Bastelaere,Rui-Zhen Huang,Laurens Vanderstraeten*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在热力学极限下从投影纠缠对态（PEPS）中直接提取角纠缠熵的方法，验证了其在二维量子临界点诊断中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角纠缠熵是探测二维量子临界点普适性质的有力工具，其角形双分区贡献由底层共形场论决定。然而如何从张量网络态中高效、直接地提取该角纠缠熵尚缺乏有效方法。

Method: 开发了一种在热力学极限下直接从投影纠缠对态（PEPS）中提取角纠缠熵的方法。

Result: 应用于处于量子临界点的模型时，角贡献表现出与有效关联长度的标度关系，符合有限纠缠标度假说；所得的角系数与其他方法一致。

Conclusion: 该结果证明了本方法在诊断二维强关联量子临界点方面的高效性。

Abstract: Entanglement scaling provides a powerful probe of universal properties at quantum critical points. In two dimensions, contributions originating from a corner-shaped bipartition exhibit a universal scaling, which is determined by the underlying conformal field theory. We develop a method to extract this corner entanglement entropy from projected entangled pair states directly in the thermodynamic limit. When applied to models at a quantum critical point, we show that the corner contribution exhibits scaling with the effective correlation length, in agreement with the hypothesis of finite-entanglement scaling. Our results for the corner coefficients are consistent with other methods, demonstrating the efficiency of our method for diagnosing strongly-correlated quantum critical points in two dimensions.

</details>


### [208] [When Is Kramers-Wannier Duality Invertible?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20090)
*Akash Sinha,Pramod Padmanabhan,Vladimir Korepin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子Ising链上Kramers-Wannier对偶何时能在有限Hilbert空间中实现为可逆酉变换，并借助Ising键代数的表示给出了充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Ising链的Kramers-Wannier对偶在有限周期链上通常表现为非可逆变换；引入对称性扭曲扇区后可提升为可逆酉变换，但缺乏对何时可实现可逆化的统一刻画。

Method: 将对偶的可逆实现问题归结为Ising键代数的表示问题，利用该代数的两个中心元构造判据，分析不同表示下（含对称性扭曲扇区）对偶的可逆性。

Result: 建立了Kramers-Wannier对偶存在可逆实现的充要条件，统一了传统非可逆实现与涉及对称性扭曲扇区的可逆构造；并在Ising键代数的一个非平凡表示中找到了具有可逆Kramers-Wannier对偶的序-无序对偶实例。

Conclusion: 表示论判据为Kramers-Wannier对偶的可逆实现提供了统一框架，且可推广至具有相同底层键代数的其他模型。

Abstract: {\it Kramers-Wannier} duality of the quantum Ising chain is naturally realized as a noninvertible transformation on a finite periodic chain. Upon incorporating appropriate symmetry-twisted sectors, however, the duality can be promoted to an invertible unitary transformation. In this Letter, we classify when the Kramers-Wannier duality admits an invertible realization on a finite Hilbert space. We show that this is determined by the representation of the Ising bond algebra and establish a necessary and sufficient condition in terms of its two central elements. This representation-theoretic criterion unifies the conventional noninvertible realization with invertible constructions involving symmetry-twisted sectors and applies equally to other models with the same underlying bond algebra. As an explicit example, we find an order-disorder duality in a non-trivial realization of the Ising bond algebra that admits an invertible Kramers-Wannier duality.

</details>


### [209] [Pressure-induced electronic and structural evolution of EuIrGe3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20376)
*N. S. Dhami,V. Baledent,I. Batistic,C. M. N. Kumar,D. Kaczorowski,L. Nataf,J. M. Ablett,J. -P. Rueff,J. P. Itie,P. Fertey,S. R. Shieh,P. Popcevic,Y. Utsumi Boucher*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过X射线吸收谱、高压同步辐射XRD、DFT计算及电阻测量，研究了非中心对称BaNiSn3型反铁磁体EuIrGe3在压力下的电子与晶体结构演化：Eu价态随压力连续升高，四方相出现各向异性压缩并在38 GPa以上发生结构相变，反铁磁序在18 GPa内反而增强，表现出与EuCoGe3、EuRhGe3不同的压力响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EuTGe3（T为过渡金属）家族兼具非中心对称结构、反铁磁性与可能存在的中间价态，是研究压力调控电子态、晶体结构与磁序耦合的理想体系。此前EuCoGe3与EuRhGe3已显示出不同压力行为，为厘清过渡金属d电子在其中的作用，需对EuIrGe3系统开展高压研究。

Method: 综合运用多种高压实验与理论手段：Eu L3边X射线吸收谱追踪Eu平均价态及Ge、Ir电子态变化；高压同步辐射X射线衍射确定晶格压缩各向异性与结构相变；结合密度泛函理论计算晶格与状态方程参数；并进行电阻率测量以获取反铁磁有序温度随压力的演化。

Result: Eu L3边谱显示Eu平均价态随压缩持续升高，Ge与Ir电子态同步改变；XRD表明四方相（I4mm）存在各向异性压缩，并在约38 GPa以上出现结构相变，实验所得晶格与状态方程参数与DFT计算吻合良好；电阻测量显示反铁磁有序温度在18 GPa内单调升高，说明尽管非磁性Eu3+组分贡献增大，反铁磁基态仍保持稳固。

Conclusion: EuIrGe3的压力响应明显区别于EuCoGe3和EuRhGe3，表明过渡金属d电子态在EuTGe3家族压力诱导的电子与结构演化中起关键作用。该工作揭示了中间价态、晶格失稳与磁序之间复杂的竞争与耦合关系。

Abstract: We investigated the pressure-induced evolution of the electronic and crystal structure of the noncentrosymmetric BaNiSn3-type antiferromagnet EuIrGe3 using x-ray absorption spectroscopy, synchrotron x-ray diffraction complemented by density functional theory calculations, and electrical resistivity measurements. The Eu L3-edge spectra reveal a continuous increase in the mean Eu valence under compression, accompanied by modifications of the Ge and Ir electronic states. High- pressure x-ray diffraction shows anisotropic lattice compression in the tetragonal (I4mm) phase and provides evidence for a structural phase transition above 38 GPa. The experimentally deter- mined lattice and equation-of-state parameters are in good agreement with the DFT calculations. Electrical resistivity measurements reveal a monotonic increase in the antiferromagnetic ordering temperatures up to 18 GPa, indicating that the antiferromagnetic ground state remains robust despite the increasing contribution of the nonmagnetic Eu3+ configuration to the intermediate va- lence state. These results demonstrate that EuIrGe3 exhibits a pressure response distinct from EuCoGe3 and EuRhGe3, highlighting the important role of the transition metal d-electron states in the pressure-induced electronic and structural evolution of the EuT Ge3 family

</details>


### [210] [Emergent Surface Kondo Flat Band Driven by Competing Interactions in a Topological Ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20432)
*Nazar Zaremba,Susmita Changdar,Haojie Guo,Iñigo Robredo,Daniel Lozano-Gomez,Andreas Leithe-Jasper,Rui Lou,Yurii Prots,Mitja Krnel,Markus König,Konstantin Semeniuk,Berit H. Goodge,Yuri Grin,Andrii Kuibarov,Oleksandr Suvorov,Alexander Fedorov,Adam Pikul,Ivan Soldatov,Rudolf Schäfer,Bernd Büchner,Oksana Kvitnitskaya,Yurii Naidyuk,Louis Lamm,Meike Pfeiffer,Elena Hassinger,Matthias Vojta,Sergey Borisenko,Miguel M. Ugeda,Jeroen van den Brink,Eteri Svanidze,Maia G. Vergniory*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文报道了UAsS晶体中由磁性驱动的一种新现象：不同强关联电子态在同一材料的不同区域自发空间分离共存。体态为金属性铁磁体并具有拓扑节点线与Weyl点，而表面则涌现出强关联Kondo态（平带与共振峰）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代凝聚态物理的核心目标之一是揭示强电子关联、磁性与能带拓扑相互交织所产生的新奇量子物态。重费米子相是电子关联最显著的体现之一，拓扑重费米子态已在若干非磁性材料中被发现，但其与磁序竞争的后果在很大程度上尚未被探索。本文旨在揭示磁性与关联竞争所导致的新现象。

Method: 结合体敏感探测手段（体态探针）与第一性原理计算确定UAsS的体态电子结构与拓扑性质，并利用角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）验证节点线与Weyl交叉；同时采用表面敏感的ARPES以及扫描隧道显微镜/谱（STM/STS）探测表面电子态。

Result: 体敏感测量表明UAsS晶体在体态为金属性铁磁体，仅存在中等程度的关联驱动能带重整化；第一性原理计算揭示其拓扑电子结构同时容纳节点线与Weyl点，ARPES测量与之相符，分辨出节点线与Weyl交叉。与之形成鲜明对比，表面敏感的ARPES与STM/STS显示存在一个钉扎在费米能级处的显著平带及伴随的尖锐共振，这是第一性原理计算未能捕捉的、涌现出的强关联Kondo态标志。

Conclusion: 磁性可以驱动不同的强关联电子态在同一材料的体态与表面区域共存，从而实现关联量子相的自发空间分离。该工作建立了一种此前未知的组织量子物质的机制，为关联拓扑体系的研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: A central goal of modern condensed matter physics is to uncover new quantum states of matter arising from the intertwined effects of strong electron correlations, magnetism, and band topology. Heavy-fermion phases, generated by Kondo interactions, represent one of the most remarkable manifestations of electronic correlations, and topological heavy-fermion states have been identified in several non-magnetic materials. Yet, the consequences of their competition with magnetic order have remained largely unexplored. Here, we reveal a new phenomenon: the spontaneous spatial separation of correlated quantum phases. By showing that magnetism can drive distinct strongly correlated electronic states to coexist in different regions of a single material, our work establishes a previously unknown mechanism for organizing quantum matter and opens a new direction in the study of correlated topological systems. Using \emph{bulk-sensitive} probes, we show that UAsS crystals are, in the bulk, metallic ferromagnets with only moderate correlation-driven band renormalizations. First-principles calculations reveal a topological electronic structure hosting both nodal lines and Weyl points, pointing to a rich underlying topology. Angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) measurements are consistent with these predictions, resolving the nodal lines and Weyl crossings. In striking contrast, \emph{surface-sensitive} ARPES and scanning tunneling microscopy/spectroscopy (STM/STS) measurements reveal a pronounced flat band pinned at the Fermi level, accompanied by a sharp resonance -- hallmarks of an emergent, strongly correlated Kondo state not captured by first-principles calculations.

</details>


### [211] [Non-Thermal Effects in Fermionic Atoms Coupled to Open Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20434)
*Jules Sueiro,Marco Schiro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究Fermi-Hubbard模型与开放耗散单腔模耦合的非平衡量子动力学，用Keldysh图解推导并求解费米子量子动理学方程，发现无相互作用时费米子进入非热稳态且热流为零，并讨论有效温度与超辐射相变相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔量子电动力学与强关联电子体系的交叉研究需要理解开放腔模如何作为非平衡噪声与耗散源影响费米子，尤其是非热稳态的形成、热流性质及对相变的影响，这些问题此前缺乏系统的动理学描述。

Method: 采用Keldysh非平衡场论图解方法，将玻色腔模处理为非平衡噪声与耗散源，推导并求解费米子的量子动理学方程；在无Hubbard相互作用情形下求解分布函数与热流，并分析低频有效温度描述及对超辐射平均场相图的影响；随后在弱耦合区引入Hubbard相互作用。

Result: 无Hubbard相互作用时，费米子普遍达到由非热分布函数表征的非平衡稳态，且该分布恰好使费米子与腔模之间的热流为零；明确了出现低频有效温度描述的参数区间，并揭示了涨落对超辐射相变平均场相图的影响；弱耦合Hubbard相互作用导致体系向完全平衡分布发生渡越。

Conclusion: 开放耗散腔模可作为可控的非平衡噪声与耗散源驱动费米子形成非热稳态，其零热流特性与有效温度描述为理解光-物质耦合体系的热化与相变提供了新视角，Hubbard相互作用驱动的向平衡分布渡越进一步刻画了关联效应与耗散竞争。

Abstract: We study a Fermi-Hubbard model coupled to a open dissipative single cavity mode. Using Keldysh diagrammatics we derive and solve the quantum kinetic equations for the fermions, taking the bosonic cavity mode as a source of non-equilibrium noise and dissipation. In absence of Hubbard interactions we show that the fermions reach generically a non-equilibrium steady-state, characterized by a non-thermal distribution function. Quite interestingly we demonstrate that the latter exactly nullifies the heat-current between fermions and cavity mode. We discuss the regimes of parameters where a low-frequency effective temperature description emerges and how the fluctuations affect the mean-field phase diagram for the superradiance phase transition. Finally we include Hubbard interaction in the weak-coupling regime and show that it leads to a crossover towards a full equilibrium distribution.

</details>


### [212] [Collective Charge-\(2e\) Bosonic Excitations in Charge-Ordered Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20472)
*Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出电荷序中存在一类新的集体玻色型准粒子“双电荷子（bichargon）”，通过电荷赝自旋模型与Holstein–Primakoff变换导出其能谱与输运行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷序通常仅以电子密度的静态调制来描述，其集体激发在准粒子图像上远不如自旋序系统（如反铁磁中的磁振子）成熟，缺少与磁振子对应的电荷序集体模式理论框架。

Method: 建立有效电荷-赝自旋模型，用交错赝自旋序表示电荷序基态；对该有序态附近的涨落作Holstein–Primakoff变换实现量子化，并分析其色散、电荷量子数以及在温度梯度、静电场和交流电场下的响应。

Result: 得到两支简并且有能隙的玻色准粒子，携带相反的量子化电荷±2e，称为“bichargon”；温度梯度驱动其扩散产生电荷Seebeck响应（需将化学势调离电荷中性点以解除简并）；热激发的bichargon气体虽带电荷，但因准粒子数不守恒在静电场下呈电绝缘；交流电场可参量产生相反电荷的bichargon对，在偏置电场下形成有限直流漂移电流，类似半导体中光激发电子-空穴对介导的光电导。

Conclusion: bichargon被确立为电荷序系统中一类新的集体玻色型电荷载流子，将自旋序系统磁振子的准粒子范式推广到电荷部门，以电荷替代自旋角动量作为输运量。

Abstract: Charge order is conventionally characterized by a static modulation of the electronic density, while its collective excitations remain less developed from a quasiparticle perspective than those of spin-ordered systems. Here, we formulate the collective excititations of charge order within an effective charge-pseudospin model, in which the charge-ordered ground state is represented by staggered pseudospin order. Quantizing fluctuations around this ordered state via a Holstein--Primakoff transformation, we reveal two branches of bosonic quasiparticles with degenerate, gapped dispersions that carry opposite quantized electric charges $\pm 2e$. We therefore term these charge-$2e$ bosons ``\textit{bichargons},'' closely paralleling the two magnon branches of a bipartite antiferromagnet that carry opposite spin angular momenta. We show that a temperature gradient drives the diffusion of thermally excited bichargons, generating a charge Seebeck response when the degeneracy between the oppositely charged branches is lifted by tuning the chemical potential away from the charge-neutrality point. In contrast, despite carrying finite electric charges, a thermal bichargon gas remains electrically insulating under a static electric field because bichargon quasiparticle number is not conserved. An ac electric field, however, can parametrically generate coherent pairs of oppositely charged bichargons that support a finite dc drift current in the presence of a bias electric field, offering a bosonic analogue of photoconductivity mediated by photoexcited electron--hole pairs in semiconductors. Our results establish bichargons as a new class of collective bosonic charge carriers in charge-ordered systems and extend the quasiparticle paradigm of magnons in spin-ordered systems to the charge sector, with electric charge replacing spin angular momentum as the transported quantity.

</details>


### [213] [Unconstrained compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ coupled to phonons: Gauss sectors, orthogonal semimetal, and deconfined criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20502)
*João C. Inácio,Fakher F. Assaad*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究无约束紧致格点QED$_{2+1}$耦合键声子，发现正交半金属相及对称破缺，证明投影到物理希尔伯特空间后退禁闭总不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子磁体的规范场描述通常需施加高斯定律约束在受限物理希尔伯特空间上。本文探究若不预先固定高斯算符本征值、仅要求其守恒时，物理高斯扇区如何变得能量有利，以及该过程与禁闭和对称破缺的联系。

Method: 对$N_f=2$的紧致格点QED$_{2+1}$耦合键声子系统，采用精确辅助场量子蒙特卡洛模拟，考察高斯扇区的能量学、规范涨落与自旋子-声子耦合对单极子竞争和相图的调控。

Result: 识别出正交半金属（OSM）相：费米子剥离规范荷形成狄拉克液体，混合不同高斯扇区，在显式施加高斯定律时不稳定。施加高斯定律后，紧致性允许携带反铁磁（AFM）与价键固体（VBS）量子数的单极子激发，规范涨落和自旋子-声子耦合调控其竞争，生成含OSM、AFM和VBS的相图。

Conclusion: OSM相的转变与高斯定律的动力学涌现同时发生；AFM与VBS带电单极子之间的竞争产生与退禁闭量子临界一致的转变。因此，投影到物理希尔伯特空间后，紧致格点QED$_{2+1}$中的退禁闭总是不稳定，倾向于AFM或VBS序。

Abstract: Gauge theoretic descriptions of 2D quantum magnets are defined on a restricted physical Hilbert space by imposing Gauss's law. In this work, we study an unconstrained compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ coupled to bond phonons, in which local Gauss operators are conserved, but their eigenvalues are not a priori fixed. Using exact auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo simulations at $N_f = 2$, we investigate how the physical Gauss sector becomes energetically favourable, and how this process is connected to confinement and symmetry breaking. We identify an orthogonal semimetallic (OSM) phase, in which fermions striped off their gauge charge and form a Dirac liquid. This phase mixes various Gauss sectors, and becomes unstable when Gauss's law is explicitly enforced. Upon the imposition of Gauss's law, compactness allows for monopole excitations carrying antiferromagnetic (AFM) and valence-bond-solid (VBS) quantum numbers. Gauge field fluctuations and spinon-phonon coupling tune between the competition of such monopoles and generate a phase diagram with OSM, AFM and VBS orders. The transitions out of the OSM phase coincide with the dynamical generation of Gauss's law, while the competition between AFM and VBS charged monopoles produces a transition consistent with deconfined quantum criticality. Thus our results establish that upon the projection to the physical Hilbert space, deconfinement in compact lattice QED$_{2+1}$ is always unstable towards either AFM or VBS order.

</details>


### [214] [Multiconfigurational Analysis of Local Electronic Structure of $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ Using Relativistic Embedded Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20526)
*Zhosan I. A.,Lomachuk Yu. V.,Maltsev D. A.,Moiseev I. A.,Andreev O. Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用多组态相对论嵌入簇方法研究RuO₂局域电子结构，发现其4d轨道能谱更接近四方对称性而非晶体学的正交对称性，轨道准简并抑制了交替磁性所需的局域xy四极序，从而解释了块体RuO₂中未观测到交替磁性的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO₂是交替磁性候选材料，但块体实验未观测到交替磁序，而应变薄膜却显示出稳健的交替磁特征，这一矛盾需要从局域电子结构层面加以澄清。核心问题在于独立粒子模型中交替磁自旋劈裂依赖于正交对称性降低所产生的xy四极序，因此有必要精确检验Ru位点的有效对称性与轨道简并情况。

Method: 构建逐步逼近真实环境的系列模型：从自由Ru离子出发，依次经过Ru+Q₆静电模型、裸[RuO₆]⁸⁻配体模型，最终建立高精度RuO₆@CTEP嵌入簇以重现晶体环境。所有体系均在SA-CASSCF和NEVPT2+SOC水平处理，以同等精度同时描述强电子关联与自旋轨道耦合。

Result: 尽管RuO₂中Ru位点的形式局域对称性为正交D₂h，但计算得到的4d轨道能谱及其劈裂模式更接近更高的四方D₄h对称性，相关4d轨道间保持强烈的准简并。

Conclusion: 局域xy四极序所依赖的对称性降低被轨道准简并所抑制，这为块体RuO₂实验中未观测到交替磁性、而应变薄膜中却出现稳健交替磁信号提供了自然解释。

Abstract: We present a multiconfigurational, relativistic embedded-cluster study of the local electronic structure of ruthenium dioxide ($\mathrm{RuO_2}$), a candidate altermagnetic material. Starting from free $\mathrm{Ru}$ ions, we progressively build up the local environment through a $\mathrm{Ru+Q_6}$ electrostatic model, a bare $\mathrm{[RuO_6]^{8-}}$ ligand model, and finally a high-accuracy $\mathrm{RuO_6}$@CTEP embedded cluster that reproduces the crystalline surroundings. All systems are treated at the SA-CASSCF and NEVPT2+SOC levels of theory to capture strong electron correlation and spin-orbit coupling on an equal footing. While the formal local site symmetry of the $\mathrm{Ru}$ sites in $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ is orthorhombic ($D_{2h}$), we find that the calculated $4d$-orbital energy spectrum and its splitting pattern behave much closer to the higher tetragonal ($D_{4h}$) symmetry, preserving a strong quasi-degeneracy among the relevant $4d$ orbitals. Since the local $xy$ quadrupolar order responsible for altermagnetic spin splitting in independent-particle models relies on this symmetry reduction, its suppression by orbital quasi-degeneracy offers a natural explanation for why altermagnetism is not observed in bulk $\mathrm{RuO_2}$ experiments, in contrast to the robust altermagnetic signatures reported in strained thin films.

</details>


### [215] [Lyapunov-controlled thermalization: an exact real-time example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20597)
*Jonas Loy,Jan C. Louw*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 满足KMS关系不足以证明平衡态；在大N大q的SYK淬火方案中，淬火后费米子关联函数虽完全热化，但二次淬火揭示了初始态的隐藏记忆，其衰减率为Lyapunov指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"淬火后满足KMS关系即证明系统达到平衡"这一常见判据是否充分，探索量子多体系统热化过程中的非平衡特征与隐藏记忆。

Method: 采用大N、大q极限下的SYK模型淬火方案：先进行一次淬火使系统看似热化，再二次淬火回到初始哈密顿量，通过连接首次淬火前时刻的关联函数来探测隐藏记忆，并利用该极限下的可解性获得完整非平衡实时演化。

Result: 所有淬火后的费米子关联函数均精确呈现热性，满足KMS关系；但二次淬火显示初始态仍存在隐藏记忆，表现为连接首次淬火前时刻的关联函数，该记忆是广延的非平衡判据，其衰减率恰为Lyapunov指数λ_L。

Conclusion: KMS关系的满足并非平衡的充分证据，λ_L决定了量子态变得与Gibbs态实际不可区分的时间尺度；该可解方案为解析研究热化提供了理想平台。

Abstract: A verified Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) relation, after a drive has ceased, is not sufficient evidence to prove equilibrium. We demonstrate this in a large-$N$ large-$q$ Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) quench protocol. Although all post-quench fermion correlators are exactly thermal, a second quench back to the initial Hamiltonian reveals hidden memory of the initial state. It is encoded in correlators connecting to times before the first quench. This memory is an extensive nonequilibrium (NEQ) witness with its decay rate being the Lyapunov exponent $λ_L$. Thus $λ_L$ sets the rate at which the state becomes effectively indistinguishable from a Gibbs state. Despite being a unitary interacting many-body system, the complete NEQ real-time evolution is obtained exactly in the large-$N$, large-$q$ limit, making the setup ideal for analytically studying thermalization.

</details>


### [216] [Engineering Weak Universality with Quantum Dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20755)
*Warre Missiaen,Michael Wimmer,Natalia Chepiga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出量子点架构分别利用谐振子介导与直接库仑相互作用实现量子Ashkin-Teller模型和XYZ/八顶点模型，可在短链中分辨连续变化的临界指数，实验参数下预期变化超过测量不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有连续可变临界指数的量子临界理论在实验上难以实现，需要可控且可测量的平台来验证非局域对偶与连续变化的临界行为。

Method: 设计量子点架构：采用谐振子介导相互作用实现量子Ashkin-Teller模型，采用直接库仑相互作用实现XYZ/八顶点模型；聚焦由非局域对偶关联的Ashkin-Teller与八顶点临界线，实现对控制临界指数的参数进行微观调控。

Result: 短链即可分辨连续变化的临界行为；在实验现实参数下，Ashkin-Teller模型的临界指数变化约为10%，八顶点模型变化显著更大；两者的预测变化均明显高于估计的测量不确定度。

Conclusion: 该量子点平台可实验可达地实现并探测连续可变临界指数，为研究量子临界理论提供可行方案，且库仑实现可达到更强相互作用区域。

Abstract: Quantum critical theories with continuously varying critical exponents remain challenging to access experimentally. Here, we propose quantum-dot architectures for realizing the quantum Ashkin-Teller and XYZ/eight-vertex models using resonator-mediated and direct Coulomb interactions, respectively. We focus on the Ashkin-Teller and eight-vertex critical lines, which are connected by a non-local duality relating local order parameters to topological string operators. The resulting platforms provide microscopic control over the parameters explicitly controlling critical exponents, with the Coulomb-based implementation enabling stronger interaction regimes. We demonstrate that continuously varying critical behavior can already be resolved in short chains. For experimentally realistic parameters, the accessible interaction range is expected to produce changes in the critical exponents of order $10\%$ for the Ashkin-Teller model and substantially larger changes for the eight-vertex model. In both cases, the predicted variations lie well above the estimated measurement uncertainties.

</details>


### [217] [Spacetime Dynamics of Altermagnetic Magnons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20767)
*Ali Emami Kopaei,Karthik Subramaniam Eswaran,Krzysztof Wohlfeld*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出利用局域自旋激发的实空间传播作为交变磁性（altermagnetism）的动力学指纹，在棋盘格晶格上用线性自旋波理论发现J_2诱导方向依赖的磁振子群速度和两支分裂，形成近似圆形的外波前与内部十字形结构，改变J_2符号可使各向异性图案旋转π/2，从而为实空间探测非常规磁序提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分交变磁性、常规铁磁与反铁磁通常依赖动量空间探测手段，缺乏实空间、动力学层面的互补判据，因此需要寻找能够直接反映交变磁序特征的新探针。

Method: 在二维棋盘格晶格上采用线性自旋波理论，考虑最近邻与次近邻耦合J_1和J_2，计算局域自旋激发的实空间传播行为，分析磁振子群速度的方向依赖性、两支磁振子色散的分裂以及J_2符号变化（J_2<0非阻挫区）对传播图案的影响。

Result: 有限J_2产生方向依赖的磁振子群速度并分裂两支磁振子；传播整体更接近反铁磁而非铁磁，保持近似圆形的外波前，同时在波前内部形成明显的十字形空间结构。将J_2变为负值进入非阻挫区后，特征传播速度增大，传播增强的对角方向互换，即各向异性空间图案旋转π/2。

Conclusion: 时空动力学可作为交变磁磁振子的互补探针，通过实空间传播图案识别非常规磁序，为固态与合成量子系统中的交变磁性检测提供可行路径。

Abstract: Distinguishing altermagnetism from conventional ferromagnetism and antiferromagnetism typically relies on momentum-space probes. Here, we show that the real-space spreading of a localized spin excitation provides a distinctive dynamical fingerprint of altermagnetic order. Using linear spin-wave theory on a two-dimensional checkerboard lattice with nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor couplings J_1 and J_2, we demonstrate that finite J_2 produces direction-dependent magnon group velocities and splits the two magnon branches. The resulting propagation remains closer to that of an antiferromagnet than a ferromagnet, retaining an approximately circular outer wavefront, while the direction-dependent magnon velocities produce a pronounced cross-like spatial structure inside this front. We further show that changing the sign of J_2 to enter the unfrustrated regime (J_2<0) increases the characteristic propagation velocities and interchanges the diagonal directions of enhanced propagation, corresponding to a pi/2 rotation of the anisotropic spatial pattern. These results establish spacetime dynamics as a complementary probe of altermagnetic magnons, providing a route to identifying unconventional magnetic order through real-space propagation patterns in solid-state and synthetic quantum systems.

</details>


### [218] [Statistics, 't Hooft Anomaly, and the Else-Nayak Index: a careful comparison of concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20813)
*Hanyu Xue*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文指出"对称变换的格点截断"这一常见几何图像在关联广义对称、拓扑激发统计与对称反常时存在陷阱，提出以"拓扑激发跃迁算符与对称变换对易"作为更自然稳健的判据，并在适当假设下给出统计与反常的一一对应，最后通过高一维 DW 规范场耦合从 gauging 视角解释该关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中普遍认为广义对称、拓扑激发、对称反常与拓扑激发统计之间存在关联，但这一关联的建立方式存在陷阱：将对称变换截断到有限格点区域得到的"对称 patch 算符"虽在边界产生对称缺陷，几何上类似在支撑边界产生拓扑激发的跃迁算符，却无法给出良定义的统计，更不保证与对称反常一致。因此需要一个更可靠、更自然的框架来比较对称与拓扑激发。

Method: 1) 批判性分析"对称变换格点截断"图像：对称 patch 算符在边界产生对称缺陷，看似对应跃迁算符在支撑边界产生拓扑激发，但不足以定义统计。2) 提出替代判据：要求拓扑激发的跃迁算符是对称的，即与对称变换对易（symmetric hopping operators）。3) 在适当假设下，证明该条件给出统计与反常之间的一一对应。4) 通过将边界物质与高一维的 DW 规范场耦合，从 gauging 角度解释该关系：跃迁算符本质上是 gauging 后作用在物理自由度上的规范不变算符；规范场背景固定后，全局对称来自保持该背景的规范变换，而跃迁的对称条件正是剩余的规范不变性。

Result: 在适当假设下，"跃迁算符与对称变换对易"的条件建立了拓扑激发统计与对称反常之间的一一对应；通过高一维 DW 规范场耦合的 gauging 解释，说明跃迁算符的对称性等价于固定规范背景后的剩余规范不变性，从而澄清了文献中的潜在误解。

Conclusion: 区分"对称 patch 算符"与"对称跃迁算符"有助于澄清文献中关于对称与拓扑激发关系的潜在误解，并提供比较对称与拓扑激发的更可靠框架。

Abstract: Generalized symmetries and topological excitations, as well as symmetry anomalies and the statistics of topological excitations, are widely believed to be related. There are, however, pitfalls in how this relation is established. A lattice truncation of a symmetry transformation to a finite patch gives a symmetry patch operator that creates symmetry defects at its boundary. This geometric picture resembles a hopping operator creating topological excitations at the boundary of its support, but does not provide well-defined statistics, let alone guarantee agreement with the symmetry anomaly. A more natural and robust relation is that the hopping operators of topological excitations are symmetric: they commute with symmetry transformations. Under suitable assumptions, this condition yields a one-to-one correspondence between statistics and anomalies. We further couple boundary matter to a DW gauge field in one higher dimension to explain this relation from the perspective of gauging. A hopping operator is, in essence, a gauge-invariant operator acting on the physical degrees of freedom after gauging. Once the gauge-field background is fixed, global symmetry comes from gauge transformations that preserve that background, while the symmetric condition on hopping is precisely the remaining gauge invariance. This distinction clarifies potential misconceptions in the literature and provides a more reliable framework for comparing symmetries and topological excitations.

</details>

### [219] [Recent benchmarks in the Analysis Grand Challenge and integration with Combine (and HS3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19753)
*Peter Fackeldey,Massimiliano Galli,Hannah Havel,Alexander Held,Iason Krommydas,Oksana Shadura,Nick Smith*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍在AGC（分析大挑战）中，将CMS核心统计工具Combine集成进PyHEP生态的工作，涵盖性能基准、在coffea-casa基础设施中运行Combine的示例，以及HS3标准的集成计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGC作为HEP分析示例，其参考实现基于现代Python包完成从数据访问到统计建模与拟合的流程；同时，Combine是CMS分析（近年发表分析90%以上）的核心工具却不在PyHEP生态内。因此有必要以AGC为例，将Combine集成进PyHEP生态，并长期支持语言无关的HS3标准以实现似然表示与框架互换。

Method: 结合使用现代Python包（coffea、uproot、awkward-array）进行数据处理，并针对其近期的性能优化开展性能基准测试；示例展示Combine如何在专用基础设施（coffea-casa）中集成与运行；同时开展将HS3集成进Combine的示例与规划工作。

Result: 给出了涵盖数据处理包近期优化收益的性能基准结果；提供了Combine在coffea-casa中集成运行的示例；并展示了将HS3集成到Combine的示例与计划。

Conclusion: 以AGC为例，Combine能够被集成进PyHEP生态并运行于专用基础设施，且HS3的集成为似然的语言无关表示与跨框架互换提供了路径。

Abstract: The Analysis Grand Challenge (AGC) showcases an example of HEP analysis. Its reference implementation uses modern Python packages to realize the main steps, from data access to statistical model building and fitting. The packages used for data handling and processing (coffea, uproot, awkward-array) have recently undergone a series of performance optimizations. While not being part of the HEP Python (PyHEP) ecosystem, the Combine tool is a pillar of CMS analyses, covering more than 90% of the analyses published in the last few years. As such, it is necessary to have Combine integrated in the PyHEP ecosystem, using the AGC as example. This project also includes, in the long-term, providing support and integration for the High Energy Physics Statistics Serialization Standard (HS3), as a way to have a language-independent way of representing the likelihood and use different frameworks interchangeably. In these proceedings we cover part of the recent work performed on the AGC and Combine, including: performance benchmarks, covering benefits introduced by the recent improvements in the data processing packages; examples of how Combine can be integrated and run in a dedicated infrastructure (coffea-casa); and examples and plans to integrate HS3 in Combine.

</details>


### [220] [Status and Prospects of the HEP Statistical Inference Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19761)
*Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 高能物理统计推断工具正面临模型复杂度、可扩展性与互操作性挑战；本文综述CMS、ATLAS所用统计工具的现状与未来规划，重点介绍RooFit自动微分、与JAX互操作及HS3框架通信等改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理（HEP）分析的统计推断传统上依赖RooFit与RooStats，但当前面临三大挑战：统计模型复杂度急剧上升（涉及数百个感兴趣参数与数千个 nuisance 参数）；大规模似然最小化需要可扩展性能；以及日益多样化的工具、计算硬件、框架与库（尤其是机器学习领域）之间的互操作性需求。

Method: 本文为综述性贡献，梳理主要LHC实验（CMS、ATLAS）所用统计工具的现状与未来规划，聚焦三个方向：ROOT生态的改进（RooFit自动微分）、与现代库的互操作（JAX）、以及跨框架通信（HS3）。

Result: 摘要未报告具体定量结果，主要给出统计工具现状总结与未来规划，并指出改进来源包括RooFit自动微分、JAX互操作与HS3通信。

Conclusion: 面对模型复杂度、性能可扩展性与生态互操作性的挑战，LHC实验的统计工具需借助自动微分、现代机器学习库集成（如JAX）与标准化跨框架通信（HS3）等途径持续演进。

Abstract: Statistical inference is a crucial part of HEP analyses. Historically based on RooFit and RooStats, the statistical tools used by the experiments are now facing unprecedented challenges, such as the rapidly growing complexity of statistical models (involving hundreds of parameters of interest and thousands of nuisance parameters), the need for scalable performance in large likelihood minimizations, and the demand for interoperability across an increasingly diverse ecosystem of tools, computational hardware, and frameworks and libraries, especially the ones developed and used within the machine learning world. This contribution summarizes status and future plans for the statistical tools used by some of the main LHC experiments (CMS, ATLAS), with a focus on improvements coming from the ROOT world (RooFit automatic differentiation), interoperability with modern libraries (JAX) and communication across frameworks (HS3).

</details>


### [221] [Constraints on the Higgs boson total width from on-shell signal-background interference in the $H\toγγ$ decay channel with $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19948)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用ATLAS探测器140 fb$^{-1}$的13 TeV质子-质子碰撞数据，通过共振$H\to\gamma\gamma$振幅与连续双光子本底的干涉效应，对希格斯玻色子总衰变宽度$\Gamma_H$进行直接壳上测量，在95%置信度下给出观测（预期）上限$\Gamma_H<307(258)$ MeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯玻色子总衰变宽度$\Gamma_H$包含所有衰变模式的信息，任何超出标准模型的衰变都会使其大于SM预言值4.1 MeV。已有的间接离壳约束依赖对希格斯耦合的假设，因此需要一种不依赖耦合假设的直接测量方法来互补探测潜在的新粒子衰变。

Method: 利用共振$H\to\gamma\gamma$振幅与连续双光子本底之间的干涉效应，该干涉会以致依赖于$\Gamma_H$的方式扭曲双光子不变质量分布，而无需对希格斯玻色子耦合作任何假设。使用ATLAS探测器在LHC Run 2期间记录的$\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV、140 fb$^{-1}$质子-质子碰撞数据进行直接壳上测量。

Result: 在95%置信度下得到观测（预期）上限$\Gamma_H<307(258)$ MeV，约为SM预言值（4.1 MeV）的75（63）倍。

Conclusion: 与间接离壳约束不同，该测量不依赖关于希格斯玻色子耦合的假设，提供了一种对希格斯玻色子潜在衰变为新粒子敏感的、新颖且互补的探针。

Abstract: The total decay width of the Higgs boson, $Γ_H$, encodes all of its decay modes; any decay into particles beyond the Standard Model (SM) would increase $Γ_H$ above the SM prediction of 4.1 MeV. In this Letter, a direct, on-shell measurement of $Γ_H$ is reported, exploiting the interference between the resonant $H \to γγ$ amplitude and the continuum diphoton background, which distorts the diphoton invariant mass distribution in a way that depends on $Γ_H$ without requiring assumptions on the Higgs boson couplings. Using 140 fb$^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV recorded by the ATLAS detector during Run 2 of the LHC, an observed (expected) upper limit of $Γ_H < 307$ (258) MeV is set at 95% confidence level, approximately 75 (63) times the SM prediction. Unlike indirect off-shell constraints on $Γ_H$, this measurement does not rely on assumptions about the Higgs boson couplings, providing a novel complementary probe sensitive to potential Higgs boson decays into new particles.

</details>


### [222] [IRIS-HEP 2026 Statistical Ecosystem Blueprint White Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19992)
*Matthew Feickert,Massimiliano Galli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该白皮书梳理了高能物理（HEP）统计工具生态系统的现状，并汇总了2026年2月IRIS-HEP统计生态蓝图活动中收集的未来研发方向意见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理的统计分析依赖大量统计工具，其生态系统现状与未来研发方向需要系统性梳理和社区共识，以指导后续投入与开发。

Method: 通过举办IRIS-HEP统计生态蓝图活动（2026年2月），汇集社区观点，对现有统计工具生态进行调研与总结。

Result: 形成了对HEP统计工具生态系统现状的综述，以及关于未来研发方向的社区意见汇总。

Conclusion: 白皮书为HEP统计工具的后续研发提供了现状评估与方向性建议，可作为社区规划与决策的参考依据。

Abstract: This white paper presents the current status of the ecosystem of statistical tools used in High Energy Physics (HEP), and attempts to summarize the views on the future R&D directions. These views have been collected during the latest IRIS-HEP statistical ecosystem blueprint, which took place in February 2026.

</details>


### [223] [Coverage Is Not Ordering: Ancillary Leakage and Representation Dependence in Covariance-Based Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20007)
*Tommaso Dorigo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 协方差矩阵替代真实统计模型会改变似然比检验的排序，即使两种程序校准完全一致，在10%相对不确定度下仍有约7.6%实验改变置信决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵常被用作测量统计模型的紧凑替代品，但此前研究多关注其对拟合值或不确定度的影响，未考察其是否改变实验本身的似然比参数推断排序。

Method: 构建可解析的相关测量模型，将数据分解为信息成分与辅助残差分歧；用等校准接受域对称差的概率质量量化排序差异，并在小不确定度区间做阶数展开分析。

Result: 排序差异通常一阶依赖于总相对不确定度，而常规与辅助条件覆盖缺陷从二阶开始；存在"覆盖盲点"处覆盖差消失而排序差异仍非零；10%相对不确定度下约7.6%实验改变置信决策。该机制将Peelle难题与辅助性及相关子集文献联系起来。

Conclusion: 协方差近似会为真实模型中具辅助性的拟合优度统计量制造推断相关性，从而改变同一信息内容下的置信区间；结论可推广至不等统计不确定度及多于两测量的情形。

Abstract: A covariance matrix is often used as a compact surrogate for the statistical model of a measurement. We show that this replacement can change not only a fitted value or its uncertainty, but also the likelihood-ratio ordering of the experiment itself. In a tractable correlated-measurement model, the data separate into an informative component and an ancillary residual disagreement. Exact likelihood inference therefore does not use that disagreement, whereas data-dependent covariance matrices and Gaussian reconstructions after nonlinear transformations can reintroduce it into parameter inference.
  We quantify the resulting change by the probability mass of the symmetric difference of equally calibrated acceptance regions - the fraction of repeated experiments for which the confidence decision about a tested parameter value changes. In the small-uncertainty regime the ordering discrepancy is generically first order in the total relative uncertainty, whereas conventional and ancillary-conditioned coverage defects begin at second order. At 'coverage-blind' points the leading coverage difference vanishes while the ordering discrepancy remains nonzero. In a representative blind case with 10% total relative uncertainty, about 7.6% of experiments change their confidence decision despite exact calibration of both procedures.
  The same mechanism connects Peelle's Pertinent Puzzle to the classical literature on ancillarity and relevant subsets: a covariance approximation can manufacture inferential relevance for a goodness-of-fit statistic that is ancillary in the true model. We show directly how this changes the confidence interval reported for the same informative content, and extend the analysis beyond equal statistical uncertainties and beyond the two-measurement case.

</details>


### [224] [A heterogeneous and vectorized sequence for the HL-LHC full tracking reconstruction of the CMS experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20135)
*Emmanouil Vourliotis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CMS实验Phase-2在线事件重建及离线跟踪主迭代的新基线策略，通过融合GPU优化的Patatrack、LST算法与CPU向量化的mkFit算法，应对HL-LHC高堆叠带来的计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC预期每束团交叉存在大量同时碰撞，给CMS实验的在线事件重建与离线跟踪带来前所未有的计算挑战，需要新的追踪策略在降低计算资源需求的同时提升物理覆盖范围。

Method: 提出一种组合式追踪序列：结合面向GPU并行优化的追踪算法（Patatrack与LST）以及面向CPU高效性能向量化的mkFit算法，并引入位移追踪（displaced tracking）与机器学习技术。

Result: 该组合方案不仅降低了计算资源需求，还通过纳入位移追踪和更多机器学习技术增强了物理探测能力。

Conclusion: 该组合追踪策略被确立为CMS Phase-2在线事件重建与离线跟踪主迭代的新基线，可有效应对HL-LHC的极端计算挑战。

Abstract: This contribution presents the new baseline strategy for the Phase-2 tracking of the CMS experiment for online event reconstruction, and for the main iteration of offline tracking. This tracking sequence takes advantage of the combination of cutting-edge tracking algorithms that are either optimized for parallel execution on GPUs (Patatrack and LST), or vectorized for efficient CPU performance (mkFit). Such a combined approach offers an effective solution to deal with the unprecedented computational challenges caused by the large number of simultaneous collisions per bunch crossing expected at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC). The proposed combination not only reduces the computational resource requirements but also enhances the physics reach by incorporating displaced tracking and increasingly leveraging machine learning techniques.

</details>

Zhou,S. Zhou,X. Zhou,X. K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII在√s=3.08 GeV的e⁺e⁻数据中观测到显著的double-s s̄产生，比值σ(e⁺e⁻→φs s̄+anything)/σ(e⁺e⁻→φ+anything)=(40.4±1.7±1.5)%，与Belle和BaBar报道的double-c c̄产生水平一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle和BaBar报道的double-c c̄产生率高于理论预期，需要类似的多夸克对产生实验来检验夸克强子化与QCD理解。此前对double-s s̄产生缺乏实验测量。

Method: 利用BESIII在√s=3.08 GeV采集的e⁺e⁻对撞数据，测量与含s夸克或s s̄对的强子伴随产生的prompt φ介子，通过探测e⁺e⁻→φ+X(s s̄)末态（X为η、η′或K⁺K⁻、K⁺K*⁻、K⁻K*⁺、K⁰K̄⁰、K⁰K̄*⁰+c.c.等奇异介子对）确定截面比值。

Result: σ(e⁺e⁻→φs s̄+anything)/σ(e⁺e⁻→φ+anything)=(40.4±1.7_stat.±1.5_syst.)%，显示出显著的double-s s̄产生。

Conclusion: double-s s̄产生水平与Belle、BaBar报道的double-c c̄产生一致，而后者理论计算预言更低。该测量有助于理解夸克强子化和QCD。

Abstract: We report the observation of significant double-$s\bar{s}$ production in the $e^+e^-$ continuum, based on the measurement of prompt $φ$ mesons produced in association with hadrons containing an $s$ quark or an $s\bar{s}$ pair. In an analysis of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected by the BESIII experiment at $\sqrt{s}=3.08~\textrm{GeV}$, the ratio $σ(e^+e^- \to φ s\bar{s}+\textrm{anything}) / σ(e^+e^-\rightarrowφ+\textrm{anything})$ is determined to be $(40.4\pm1.7_{\rm stat.}\pm1.5_{\rm syst.})\%$ by detecting and measuring $e^+e^-\toφ+ X(s\bar{s})$, where $X(s\bar{s})$ denotes an $η$ meson, an $η^{\prime}$ meson, or one of the strange-meson pairs $K^+K^-$, $K^+K^{*-}$, $K^-K^{*+}$, $K^0\bar{K}^{0}$, and $K^0\bar{K}^{*0}+\textrm{c.c.}$. The level of double-$s\bar{s}$ production is in line with the double-$c\bar{c}$ production reported by the Belle and \babar\ collaborations, for which theoretical calculations predict lower rates. The experimental measurement of double $s\bar{s}$ production at BESIII can shed light on the understanding of quark hadronization and QCD.

</details>
