# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [Identifying Damage Pathways Linking Sequence Composition to Storage Failure in DNA Data Storage via High-Dimensional Mediation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19822)
*Jingyi Li,Huaming Wu,Haixiang Zhang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出高维半参数中介分析框架（深度神经网络逼近非线性效应 + 三步中介筛选与推断），应用于电化学合成DNA老化实验，识别出14个显著中介变量，均为单碱基缺失型损伤，且中介效应集中在以C结尾的三核苷酸上下文，揭示缺失型损伤是序列组成影响存储可靠性的主要通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNA数据存储具有极高信息密度和长期耐久性，但其可靠性受合成阶段引入、存储阶段累积的序列依赖性错误限制。现有研究尚不清楚序列组成如何通过具体分子损伤成分与存储失败相关联，缺乏机制导向的统计推断工具。本文旨在建立从序列组成（GC含量）到存储质量失败之间、以高维损伤谱为中介的因果机制分析框架。

Method: 提出面向生存结局的高维半参数中介分析框架：以GC含量为暴露变量，以高维基线损伤谱（按三核苷酸上下文与错误类型分层的每条读段损伤计数向量）为中介变量，以存储质量失败为结局。中介模型与生存模型中的非线性协变量效应均用深度神经网络逼近。采用三步流程进行中介变量选择与推断：系数乘积筛选、SCAD（平滑裁剪绝对偏差）惩罚估计、联合显著性检验。

Result: 将该方法应用于电化学合成DNA的老化实验，识别出14个显著中介变量，全部对应单碱基缺失；估计的中介效应集中于以C结尾的三核苷酸上下文。

Conclusion: 结果揭示缺失型损伤是连接序列组成与档案存储可靠性下降的主要通路，并为未来序列优化与纠错控制策略提示候选序列特征。所提框架为理解并提升DNA数据存储可靠性提供了机制导向的统计方法。

Abstract: DNA data storage offers extraordinary information density and long-term durability, but its reliability is limited by sequence-dependent errors introduced during synthesis and accumulated during storage. It remains unclear how sequence composition is associated with storage failure through specific molecular damage components. We develop a high-dimensional semiparametric mediation framework for survival outcomes. GC content is treated as the exposure, a high-dimensional baseline damage spectrum (a vector of per-read damage counts stratified by trinucleotide context and error type) as the mediator, and storage-quality failure as the outcome. Nonlinear covariate effects in both the mediator and survival models are approximated using deep neural networks. A three-step procedure combining product-of-coefficients screening, Smoothly Clipped Absolute Deviation (SCAD) penalized estimation, and joint significance testing is developed for mediator selection and inference. Applied to an aging experiment on electrochemically synthesized DNA, the method identifies 14 significant mediators, all corresponding to single-base deletions, with estimated mediated effects concentrated in trinucleotide contexts ending in C. These results reveal deletion-type damage as a major pathway linking sequence composition to reduced archival reliability and suggest candidate sequence features for future optimization and error-control strategies. The proposed framework thus offers a mechanism-oriented statistical approach for understanding and improving the reliability of DNA data storage.

</details>


### [227] [Comparing statistical learning models in wastewater-based epidemiology: An application to norovirus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20038)
*Caelan McNamara,Fion Tan,Ella White,Elizaveta Semenova,Marta Blangiardo*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文以英格兰诺如病毒为案例，对比6种建模方法在污水流行病学（WBE）中时空浓度预测的表现。Random Forest综合最优（区间评分最佳、覆盖率95.3%），INLA-SPDE贝叶斯时空模型在不确定性量化上表现稳健。研究揭示预测精度、不确定性量化与计算效率之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 污水流行病学（WBE）已成为传染病监测的重要工具，但针对病原体浓度时空预测的不同建模方法缺乏直接比较，实践者难以判断何种方法在预测精度、不确定性量化和计算成本之间更优。

Method: 以英格兰诺如病毒为案例，使用2021年5月至2022年3月采集自152座污水处理厂的3,232份污水样本。比较6种方法：基准模型为采用INLA与SPDE的贝叶斯时空模型，对照Lasso回归、广义可加模型（GAM）、贝叶斯GAM、XGBoost与Random Forest。采用10折空间分块交叉验证，评价指标包括均方误差、偏差、相关性、95%预测区间经验覆盖率、区间评分和计算成本。

Result: Random Forest综合表现最佳，区间评分最优，经验覆盖率最接近名义值（95.3%），点预测精度与XGBoost相当。INLA-SPDE模型同样表现良好，覆盖率接近名义水平，区间评分位列第三，在所有评价指标上偏差持续较低。两模型预测的时空趋势空间格局相似，但不确定性估计存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了预测精度、不确定性量化与计算效率之间的权衡。集成机器学习方法适合快速预测，而贝叶斯地统计学模型在需要概率化决策支持的公共卫生监测中仍具重要价值。

Abstract: Wastewater-based epidemiology (WBE) is an increasingly important tool for infectious disease surveillance, but there has been limited direct comparison of modelling approaches for predicting pathogen concentrations across space and time. We compare the predictive performance of six modelling approaches using norovirus in England as a case study, considering 3,232 wastewater samples from 152 sewage treatment works collected between May 2021 and March 2022 as part of the UK Environmental Monitoring for Health Protection programme. The benchmark was a Bayesian spatio-temporal model using Integrated Nested Laplace Approximation (INLA) with the Stochastic Partial Differential Equation approach (SPDE), compared with Lasso regression, Generalised Additive Models (GAM), Bayesian GAM, Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), and Random Forest. Models were evaluated using 10-fold spatial-block cross-validation with metrics including mean squared error, bias, correlation, empirical coverage of 95% prediction intervals, interval score, and computational cost. Random Forest was the best overall performing model, achieving the best interval score and nearest to nominal empirical coverage (95.3%), while maintaining point prediction accuracy comparable to XGBoost. The INLA-SPDE model also performed well, with near-nominal empirical coverage, the third best interval score, and consistently low bias across all evaluated metrics. Comparing predicted spatio-temporal trends, both models yielded similar spatial patterns but notable differences in uncertainty estimation. Our findings highlight a trade-off between predictive accuracy, uncertainty quantification, and computational efficiency. Ensemble machine learning methods are well suited to rapid prediction, whereas Bayesian geostatistical models remain valuable when probabilistic decision support is a priority for public health surveillance

</details>


### [228] [Risk-Set Transported Synthetic Control with Difference-in-Differences Adjustment under Staggered Treatment Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20264)
*Mojtaba Eslami*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出RT-SC-DiD估计量，在交错处理采纳设计中随事件时间收缩的捐赠池上，通过将权重向"传输参考"收缩并做DiD基线校正，缓解逐期重优化导致的反事实不稳定，仿真显示中等传输正则化可降低平均RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错采纳设计中，后处理单位仅在其自身处理开始前可作为先处理队列的有效对照，因此可用捐赠集合随事件时间不断收缩。固定最长horizon的捐赠池会丢弃暂时合格的捐赠者；而在每个horizon独立重估合成控制权重，又会因捐赠者构成变化使反事实不稳定。现有交错合成控制与合成DiD方法未显式地对队列内权重序列向"传输参考"进行正则化。

Method: 提出风险集传输合成控制结合双重差分调整（RT-SC-DiD）。对每个队列与事件时间horizon，在当前未处理捐赠者上拟合权重，并将其向一个"传输参考"收缩——该参考把退出捐赠者的权重再分配给相似的存续捐赠者；同时用DiD基线校正剔除持续性的水平差异。理论上刻画逐horizon重优化带来的扭曲，推导朴素删除与重归一化引起的载荷变化，并在传输映射的显式正则条件下给出误差传播的条件递归界。此外引入捐赠支持诊断与仅用捐赠者的安慰剂程序来选择传输惩罚参数。

Result: 在80次重复的初步对比和另一项每取值40次重复的敏感性分析中，中等强度的传输正则化相比逐horizon独立估计与强锚定均降低了平均RMSE。该证据支持方法的偏差-方差动机，但并非已被证明的保证。

Conclusion: RT-SC-DiD适用于后处理单位能提供有用短期信息、且捐赠支持随时间显著收缩的情境。其贡献在于首次（据作者所知）在风险集收缩的设定下，显式地对队列内权重序列向传输参考进行正则化，为交错合成控制类方法提供了新的稳定化思路。

Abstract: In staggered treatment-adoption designs, later-treated units are valid controls for an earlier-treated cohort only until their own treatment begins, so the admissible donor set contracts with event time. Fixing the donor pool at the longest horizon discards temporarily eligible donors, whereas re-estimating synthetic-control weights independently at each horizon can make the counterfactual unstable as donor composition changes. We propose Risk-Set Transported Synthetic Control with Difference-in-Differences Adjustment (RT-SC-DiD). For each cohort and event-time horizon, the estimator fits weights on the currently untreated donors while shrinking them toward a transported reference that reallocates the weight of exiting donors to similar surviving donors. A DiD baseline correction removes persistent level differences. We characterize distortion from horizon-by-horizon reoptimization, derive the loading change induced by naive deletion and renormalization, and give a conditional recursive bound for error propagation under an explicitly assumed regularity condition on the transport map. We also introduce donor-support diagnostics and a donor-only placebo procedure for selecting the transport penalty. In an 80-replication pilot comparison and a separate 40-replication-per-value sensitivity analysis, intermediate transport regularization reduces average RMSE relative to independent horizon-specific estimation and strong anchoring. This evidence supports the method's bias-variance motivation but is not a proved guarantee. RT-SC-DiD is intended for settings where later-treated units provide useful short-horizon information and donor support contracts materially over time. Existing staggered synthetic-control and synthetic difference-in-differences methods do not, to our knowledge, explicitly regularize within-cohort weight sequences toward transported references as risk sets contract.

</details>


### [229] [A Hierarchical Bayesian Model for Selecting Relevant Schema Subgraphs,with an Application to Grounding Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20294)
*Robert Richardson*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出层次贝叶斯模型，将关系数据库的schema linking建模为小属性图上的结构化子集选择，通过外键边上的成对自逻辑斯蒂耦合实现图耦合推断，并在BIRD和Spider上验证其校准性与预测覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有schema linking方法通常独立地为表或列打分，但真正的目标对象往往是外键图中一个连通的子图，独立打分无法利用表之间的连接结构，容易漏选信号弱但起连接作用的表。

Method: 将schema linking视为小属性图上的结构化子集选择，构建层次贝叶斯模型：以query-table与query-column特征提供一元证据，在外键边上加入成对自逻辑斯蒂耦合，并用数据库级随机效应刻画基线纳入率与耦合强度的变异；由于schema图规模小，可精确计算条件似然与纳入概率，并对参数后验采用拉普拉斯近似做平均。

Result: 在BIRD和Spider上，正图耦合能找回被独立打分漏掉的表，但会整体抬高边际纳入概率；联合估计截距与耦合可恢复校准，数据库级部分池化使结构效应跨schema自适应；图耦合后验对精确相关子图赋予更高概率，产生接近名义覆盖率的预测集，而独立模型预测集覆盖不足。

Conclusion: 图耦合有助于恢复相关子图，但需截距补偿其导致的概率整体上移；模型可给出每个数据库中图权重的后验。因此该方法应主要被评估为表子集上的后验预测分布，而非追求精度的选择器。

Abstract: Grounding a large language model on a relational database requires selecting the tables and joins relevant to a query. Existing schema-linking methods usually score tables or columns independently, although the target object is typically a connected subgraph of the foreign-key graph. We propose a hierarchical Bayesian model for schema linking as structured subset selection on small attributed graphs. Unary evidence from query-table and query-column features is combined with a pairwise autologistic coupling on foreign-key edges, allowing weakly-signalled tables to be selected when they connect other relevant tables. Database-level random effects model variation in the baseline inclusion rate and coupling strength, and the small size of the schema graphs permits exact evaluation of the conditional likelihood and inclusion probabilities, which we average over a Laplace approximation to the parameter posterior. On the benchmark datasets BIRD and Spider, positive graph coupling recovers tables missed by independent scoring but shifts the marginal inclusion probabilities upward. Jointly estimating the intercept and coupling restores calibration, and database-level partial pooling adapts the structural effect across schemas. The graph-coupled posterior assigns more probability to the exact relevant subgraph and produces informative predictive sets with near-nominal coverage, whereas predictive sets from the corresponding independent model undercover. The fitted model also reports a posterior over how strongly to weight the graph in each database. Stability and mean-shift results explain when coupling aids recovery and why the intercept must compensate for it. We therefore evaluate the method primarily as a posterior predictive distribution over table subsets rather than as a precision-maximizing selector.

</details>


### [230] [Emulation strategies for Bayesian inference of regional left ventricle material parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20372)
*Hongjin Ren,Vinny Davies,Hao Gao,Mu Niu,Benn Macdonald*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯代理建模框架，用多输出变分高斯过程推断左心室五个生理分区的区域Holzapfel-Ogden材料参数，实现可行且具不确定性感知的参数反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 左心室患者特异性生物力学模型可将心脏磁共振成像与区域心肌材料属性关联，但现有基于仿真器的研究通常将心肌视为力学均质，难以表征局部功能障碍。因此需要一种能推断区域非均质材料参数、并量化不确定性的方法。

Method: 将左心室按美国心脏协会17节段模型划分为五个生理分区，构建贝叶斯代理（emulator）建模框架以推断区域Holzapfel-Ogden材料参数。筛选了八种代理策略，涵盖单输出与多输出、局部与全局、高斯过程与神经网络架构，先以参数点估计精度初筛，再以经验边缘可信区间覆盖率和后验收缩对保留的三种模型进行评估。最终保留多输出变分高斯过程用于后续分析，并在合成局部／全局硬化场景和健康志愿者数据（含不完整观测向量与联合推断的应变噪声尺度）中进行验证。

Result: 点估计精度相近的模型在不确定性表现上差异显著；多输出变分高斯过程在各准则间取得最佳平衡。在合成局部与全局硬化场景中，最大后验估计总体上能区分硬化区与基线区，但非线性硬化参数比刚度幅值参数更难以从舒张末期观测中辨识。健康志愿者分析证明了在不完整观测向量和联合推断应变噪声尺度下的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为左心室区域参数推断提供了一种计算可行且具不确定性感知的方法，表明仅凭点估计精度筛选代理模型不足，需结合不确定性评估，且不同材料参数的可辨识性存在差异。

Abstract: Patient-specific biomechanical models of the left ventricle can relate cardiac magnetic resonance imaging to regional myocardial material properties, but existing emulator-based studies typically treat the myocardium as mechanically homogeneous, limiting representation of localised dysfunction. We propose a Bayesian surrogate-modelling framework for inferring regional Holzapfel-Ogden material parameters in a left ventricle partitioned into five physiological zones derived from the American Heart Association 17-segment model. Eight emulator strategies spanning single- versus multi-output, local versus global, and Gaussian-process- versus neural-network-based architectures were screened using parameter point-estimation accuracy; the three retained models were evaluated using empirical marginal credible-interval coverage and posterior contraction. We found that models with comparable point accuracy nevertheless differed markedly in uncertainty. A multi-output variational Gaussian process provided the most favourable balance across these criteria and was retained for the subsequent analyses. In synthetic local and global stiffening scenarios, maximum a posteriori estimates generally distinguished stiffened from baseline zones, but the nonlinear-stiffening parameters were more difficult to identify from end-diastolic observations than the stiffness-magnitude parameters. A healthy-volunteer analysis demonstrates feasibility with an incomplete observation vector and jointly inferred strain-noise scales. These results suggest that the proposed framework provides a computationally feasible, uncertainty-aware approach to regional left ventricle parameter inference.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Asymptotic counting of integers with prime factors $p_{r^a s^b}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19434)
*Mehdi Golafshan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文给出了一个计数渐近公式：统计所有素因子为形如 $p_{r^a s^b}$ 的整数（$r,s\ge2$ 乘法独立），相对误差为 $o(1)$ 且低至常数项显式；对 $(r,s)=(2,3)$，这解决了相应的序数编码计数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由特定下标集合 $\{r^a s^b\}$ 索引的素数所生成的整数的计数问题。$(r,s)=(2,3)$ 情形对应 $ω^{ω^2}$ 以下序数的素码计数，是 Vernaeve、Vindas 和 Weiermann 提出的计数问题中的未决情形。

Method: 通过对由乘法独立整数 $r,s$ 生成的素数下标集合进行计数分析，推导出相对误差为 $o(1)$ 的渐近公式，并使误差估计显式到乘法常数；随后将 $(r,s)=(2,3)$ 的结果应用于序数素码计数。

Result: 得到一个相对误差为 $o(1)$、显式到乘法常数的渐近计数公式；当 $(r,s)=(2,3)$ 时，该公式确定了 $ω^{ω^2}$ 以下序数素码的计数渐近。

Conclusion: 论文给出了一类由乘法独立指数生成的素数下标所构造整数的显式渐近计数公式，并解决了 Vernaeve–Vindas–Weiermann 计数问题中对应 $(r,s)=(2,3)$ 的情形。

Abstract: Let $p_n$ be the $n$th prime, and let $r,s\ge2$ be fixed multiplicatively independent integers. We count the integers up to $x$ whose prime factors all have the form $p_{r^a s^b}$ with integers $a,b\ge0$. Our asymptotic formula for this count has relative error $o(1)$ and is explicit down to the multiplicative constant. For $(r,s)=(2,3)$ these integers are the prime codes of the ordinals below $ω^{ω^2}$, so the formula settles that case of the counting problem of Vernaeve, Vindas and Weiermann.

</details>


### [232] [Endoscopic description of the local Langlands correspondence for $\mathrm{G}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19439)
*Yugo Takanashi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明Gan-Savin构造的p进G₂的L-丛满足内窥字符恒等式，并给出相关整体重数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Gan与Savin为p进G₂构造的L-丛是否满足内窥字符恒等式，以保证其自守表示分类与Langlands猜想相容。

Method: 通过证明从G₂到带triality的PGSO₈的函子性提升的扭内窥字符恒等式，进而推出G₂的L-丛的内窥字符恒等式。

Result: G₂的L-丛满足内窥字符恒等式；同时得到从G₂到PGSO₈的扭内窥字符恒等式。

Conclusion: 作为副产品，证明了对应于PGL₃尖点自守谱的G₂离散自守谱的整体重数公式。

Abstract: We prove that the $L$-packets for $p$-adic $\mathrm{G}_2$ constructed by Gan and Savin satisfy the endoscopic character identities. In the course of the proof, we also prove twisted endoscopic character identities for functorial lifts from $\mathrm{G}_2$ to $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ with triality. As a byproduct, we also prove a global multiplicity formula for the discrete automorphic spectrum of $\mathrm{G}_2$ coming from the cuspidal automorphic spectrum of $\mathrm{PGL}_3$.

</details>


### [233] [A uniform proof approach for congruences modulo 3 for partitions with $k$-colored odd parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19440)
*Marie Jameson,James A. Sellers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为分拆函数 \(a_k(n)\)（偶部单色、奇部 \(k\) 色可染）的模 3 同余研究提供了统一框架，并证明了无穷多族非嵌套的模 3 同余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于 \(a_k(n)\) 的同余多采用零散（ad hoc）方法在算术级数上逐一发现，缺乏系统性处理手段，作者希望建立一个统一框架来研究其模 3 同余。

Method: 建立研究 \(a_k(n)\) 模 3 同余的统一框架，在此框架下系统推导算术级数中的同余关系。

Result: 在该框架下证明了无穷多个"无穷族"的非嵌套模 3 同余。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效刻画了 \(a_k(n)\) 的模 3 同余结构，将以往零散结果系统化，并产生了大量新的非嵌套同余族。

Abstract: In recent work, Hirschhorn and the second author defined $a_k(n)$ to be the number of partitions of $n$ wherein the even parts come in only one color, while the odd parts may be ``colored'' with one of $k$ colors for fixed $k\geq 1$. This function generalizes the classical partition function and has been of significant interest because it satisfies a number of congruences. Although prior work studying $a_k(n)$ has resulted in congruences in arithmetic progressions in a somewhat ad hoc manner, this work gives a uniform framework for studying congruences modulo 3. This allows us to prove an infinite family of infinite families of non-nested congruences modulo 3.

</details>


### [234] [A new family of maximal curves not covered by the Hermitian curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19546)
*Liming Ma,Yipeng Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 构造了新的𝔽_{q^{2n}}-极大曲线，给出了Beelen–Montanucci曲线的Kummer模型，并证明其在奇n时不被Hermitian曲线覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在构造新的极大曲线并研究其与Hermitian曲线的覆盖关系，同时探讨Beelen–Montanucci曲线的结构。新曲线不被Hermitian曲线覆盖，说明其不属于已知的极大曲线家族；Kummer模型为这些曲线提供了统一的描述方式。

Method: 对每个素数幂q>2和偶数n≥4，通过显式定义方程构造𝔽_{q^{2n}}-极大曲线；用计数有理点的方法给出极大性的统一证明，并分别计算奇、偶n情形的亏格；对奇n≥5证明Beelen–Montanucci曲线不被Hermitian曲线覆盖；同时给出阶为(q^n+1)q(q^2-1)的自同构子群。

Result: 构造出亏格为(q^2-1)q^n/2的𝔽_{q^{2n}}-极大曲线，且该曲线不被𝔽_{q^{2n}}上的Hermitian曲线覆盖。定义方程在n≥3为奇数时给出Beelen–Montanucci曲线的Kummer模型。对q>2、奇n≥5，Beelen–Montanucci曲线不被Hermitian曲线覆盖。对任意素数幂q和n≥4，存在阶为(q^n+1)q(q^2-1)的显式自同构子群。

Conclusion: 本文构造了一类不被Hermitian曲线覆盖的新型𝔽_{q^{2n}}-极大曲线，给出了Beelen–Montanucci曲线的Kummer模型，并在奇n情形下证明了其不被Hermitian曲线覆盖，扩展了Galois覆盖情形下的已知结果，同时明确了曲线自同构子群的结构。

Abstract: For every prime power $q>2$ and every even integer $n\ge4$, we construct an $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$-maximal curve of genus $(q^2-1)q^n/2$ that is not covered by the Hermitian curve over $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$. The defining equation also gives a Kummer model for the Beelen--Montanucci curves when $n\ge3$ is odd. We compute the genus for both odd and even $n$ and give a uniform proof of maximality by counting rational places. For $q>2$ and odd $n\ge5$, we also prove that the Beelen--Montanucci curves are not covered by the Hermitian curve, extending the known result for Galois coverings. For every prime power $q$ and $n\ge4$, we also give an explicit automorphism subgroup of order $(q^n+1)q(q^2-1)$.

</details>


### [235] [Simultaneous nonvanishing of quadratic twists via Rankin-Cohen brackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19649)
*Ramin Takloo-Bighash*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了迹对角Rankin--Cohen括号的线性无关性，进而得到权为2ℓ的一阶尖形式中L函数中心值非零的Hecke特征形式个数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究权为2ℓ的level-one尖形式（SL₂(ℤ)）中，其与二次特征χ_D扭乘后的L函数在中心点ℓ处非零的Hecke特征形式数量，给出一致下界。

Method: 设D为奇基本判别式（允许D=1），r≥1固定。对充分大且满足(-1)^ℓ D>0的整数ℓ，证明前r个迹对角Rankin--Cohen括号Tr_1^{|D|}[G_{ℓ-2e,D},G_{ℓ-2e,D}]_{2e}（1≤e≤r）在S_{2ℓ}(SL₂(ℤ))中线性无关；其中G_{k,D}为权k、级|D|、nebentypus为χ_D的Eisenstein级数。

Result: 结合Kayath、Lane、Neifeld、Ni与Xue的Petersson公式，推出至少r个标准化Hecke特征形式f∈S_{2ℓ}(SL₂(ℤ))满足L(f⊗χ_D,ℓ)≠0。当D=1时，对每个固定的r及所有充分大且K≡0 (mod 4)的K，至少有r个权为K的level-one特征形式具有非零中心值。

Conclusion: 通过迹对角Rankin--Cohen括号的线性无关性，建立了L函数中心值非零的Hecke特征形式数量的下界，并给出level-one情形下权K≡0 (mod 4)时非零中心值的特征形式个数至少为r的结论。

Abstract: Let $D$ be an odd fundamental discriminant, with $D=1$ permitted, and let $r\geq 1$ be fixed. We prove that, for every sufficiently large integer $\ell$ satisfying $(-1)^\ell D>0$, the first $r$ traced diagonal Rankin--Cohen brackets \[
  \mathrm{Tr}_1^{|D|}[G_{\ell-2e,D},G_{\ell-2e,D}]_{2e},
  \qquad 1\leq e\leq r, \] are linearly independent in $S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$. Here $G_{k,D}$ is the Eisenstein series of weight $k$, level $|D|$, and nebentypus $χ_D$. The Petersson formula of Kayath, Lane, Neifeld, Ni, and Xue then implies that at least $r$ normalized Hecke eigenforms $f\in S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$ satisfy $L(f\otimesχ_D,\ell)\neq 0$. For $D=1$, this gives, for every fixed $r$ and every sufficiently large $K\equiv 0\pmod 4$, at least $r$ level-one eigenforms of weight $K$ with nonzero central value.

</details>


### [236] [Finite subgroups of outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19696)
*Yu Nishio*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从单远阿贝尔几何视角研究混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群外自同构群的有限子群，证明存在非拟几何的有限子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合特征局部域的自同构群到其绝对伽罗瓦群的外自同构群的自然单射给出拟几何子群的典型例子。作者关注的问题是：外自同构群的所有有限子群是否都是拟几何的，即拟几何子群集合能否等同于全部有限子群集合。

Method: 作者采用单远阿贝尔几何（mono-anabelian geometry）的方法。首先定义外自同构群的有限子群为拟几何的：若其在相应绝对伽罗瓦群自同构群中的逆像同构于某个混合特征局部域的绝对伽罗瓦群。然后据此构造并分析外自同构群的有限子群。

Result: 主要结果表明：混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群的外自同构群存在非拟几何的有限子群。

Conclusion: 该结果说明，一般情况下，混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群外自同构群的拟几何子群集合不能刻画为外自同构群的全部有限子群集合，即拟几何性是对有限子群的严格限制。

Abstract: In the present paper, we study finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields from the point of view of mono-anabelian geometry. We shall refer to a finite subgroup of the outer automorphism group of the absolute Galois group of a mixed-characteristic local field as quasi-geometric if its inverse image in the automorphism group of the corresponding absolute Galois group is isomorphic to the absolute Galois group of some mixed-characteristic local field. It is well-known that the image of the natural injective homomorphism from the automorphism group of a mixed-characteristic local field to the outer automorphism group of the associated absolute Galois group yields a typical example of a quasi-geometric subgroup. The main result of the present paper asserts the existence of non-quasi-geometric finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields. This result shows that the set of quasi-geometric subgroups of the outer automorphism group of the absolute Galois group of a mixed-characteristic local field cannot be characterized, in general, as the set of all finite subgroups of the outer automorphism group.

</details>


### [237] [Norms of Schneider--Teitelbaum Polynomials in $p$-adic Fourier Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20002)
*Yu Katagiri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文显式计算了Schneider–Teitelbaum p进Fourier理论中多项式 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 在固定阶局部F-解析函数Banach空间上的范数，并据此给出Bannai–Kobayashi基的显式归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schneider–Teitelbaum的p进Fourier理论中多项式 $P_n(T)$ 是核心对象，但其在局部解析函数Banach空间上的范数此前缺乏显式结果；Bannai–Kobayashi构造的基虽有范数1性质，但缺少显式归一化。

Method: 令 $F$ 为 $\mathbb{Q}_p$ 的非分歧二次扩域，$\Omega$ 为Lubin–Tate周期。基于Ardakov–Berger关于 $P_n(\Omega)$ 取值的最新赋值公式，计算 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 在固定阶局部F-解析函数Banach空间上的范数。

Result: 显式确定了 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 在固定阶局部F-解析函数Banach空间上的范数；作为应用，得到Bannai–Kobayashi基的显式归一化，使其元素范数均为1。

Conclusion: 该工作将Ardakov–Berger的赋值公式推广到范数计算，并完善了Bannai–Kobayashi基的归一化理论。

Abstract: Let $F$ be the unramified quadratic extension of $\mathbb{Q}_p$, and let $P_n(T)$ denote the polynomials arising in the $p$-adic Fourier theory of Schneider and Teitelbaum. We determine explicitly the norms of the functions $P_n(xΩ)$ on the Banach spaces of locally $F$-analytic functions of fixed order, where $Ω$ is a Lubin--Tate period. Our computation is based on a recent valuation formula of Ardakov and Berger for the values $P_n(Ω)$. As an application, we obtain an explicit normalization of the basis constructed by Bannai and Kobayashi, whose elements all have norm one.

</details>


### [238] [Sampling and Moments of Reciprocal Quadratic Mellin Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20201)
*K. Srinivasa Raghava*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究互反二次有理函数相关的对数Mellin积分，证明整函数变形在正整数采样下的生成函数除以π后对0<|a|<1的有理数a均为代数函数，并给出主半权例子的极小四次式、分支轨迹、微分方程、多项式递推与系数渐近，在鞍点区域c>1、-2√c<b<0中建立实a的正双曲公式、Hausdorff矩序列确定性、严格全正性与单调商极限，且给出统一渐近与固定临界轨迹上的两项对角渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注对数Mellin积分及其采样生成函数的代数性与渐近行为。核心动机是：对于分母为互反二次有理函数的核，希望理解其整函数变形在正整数采样点处的生成函数结构，特别是除以π后的代数性质、极小多项式与微分方程，并在鞍点区域刻画矩序列的正性、全正性与渐近控制，以及固定临界轨迹上的对角渐近修正。

Method: 研究对象为互反二次有理函数相关的对数Mellin积分及其整函数变形；对核具有代数系数的情况，考察正整数采样生成函数除以π后的代数性。针对主半权例子确定极小四次式、完整有限分支轨迹、极小微分方程、多项式递推与系数渐近。在鞍点区域c>1、-2√c<b<0中推导实0<a<1的双曲公式，分析Hausdorff矩序列的确定性、严格全正性与单调商极限。对实|a|<1给出该区域的一般系数渐近，说明单一最大值同时控制采样半径与高阶对数矩。在固定临界轨迹上计算两项对角渐近，并研究除以修正因子1+γ/M后的O(M^{-2})逼近。

Result: 当有理核具有代数系数时，正整数采样生成函数除以π后对任意a∈Q且0<|a|<1均为代数函数。对主半权例子给出极小四次式、完整有限分支轨迹、极小微分方程、多项式递推和系数渐近。在鞍点区域c>1、-2√c<b<0中，实0<a<1的正双曲公式给出确定的Hausdorff矩序列、严格全正性与单调商极限。对实|a|<1，该区域的一般系数渐近表明一个最大值同时控制采样半径与高阶对数矩。固定临界轨迹上的两项对角渐近给出趋于π的归一化乘积，除以1+γ/M后达到O(M^{-2})逼近。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了互反二次有理核对数Mellin积分采样生成函数的代数性、极小微分结构与渐近性质：主半权情形具有极小四次式与有限分支轨迹；鞍点区域内矩序列具有确定性、严格全正性与单调商极限，并由单一最大值统一控制采样半径与高阶矩；固定临界轨迹上的对角渐近可通过1+γ/M修正达到二阶精度，归一化乘积趋于π。

Abstract: We study logarithmic Mellin integrals attached to a reciprocal quadratic rational function. When the rational kernel has algebraic coefficients, positive-integer samples of an entire deformation have a generating function which, after division by $π$, is algebraic for every $a\in\mathbb Q$ with $0<\vert{}a\vert{}<1$. For the principal half-weight example we determine the minimal quartic, complete finite branch locus, minimal differential equation, polynomial recurrence, and coefficient asymptotics. Throughout the saddle region $c>1$, $-2\sqrt c<b<0$, positive hyperbolic formulas for real $0<a<1$ give determinate Hausdorff moment sequences, strict total positivity, and monotone quotient limits. For real $\vert{}a\vert{}<1$, a general coefficient asymptotic in the same region shows that one maximum controls both the sampling radius and the high logarithmic moments. On the fixed-critical locus, two-term diagonal asymptotics give a normalized product tending to $π$; division by the displayed correction $1+γ/M$ gives an $O(M^{-2})$ approximation.

</details>


### [239] [On a question of Ruzsa about densities of sumsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20206)
*Paolo Leonetti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文否定回答了Ruzsa的一个问题：不存在常数0<ν<1和c>0，使得对每个具有渐近密度的集合A，都有d_*(A+A)≥c(d^*(A+A))^{1-ν}d(A)^ν。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruzsa提出了关于和集A+A的下渐近密度、上渐近密度与A的渐近密度之间是否存在某种幂次插值不等式的问题。本文旨在检验该不等式是否成立。

Method: 在自然数集N上定义渐近密度d、上渐近密度d^*和下渐近密度d_*，针对具有渐近密度的集合A，构造反例并分析d_*(A+A)、d^*(A+A)与d(A)之间的关系，从而排除所提幂次不等式的存在性。

Result: 证明了不存在常数0<ν<1和c>0满足上述不等式对一切具有渐近密度的A⊆N成立。因此，Ruzsa的问题得到否定回答。

Conclusion: Ruzsa所问的关于和集密度下界的不等式一般不成立，下渐近密度不能由A+A的上渐近密度与A的渐近密度按该幂次形式控制。

Abstract: Let $\mathsf{d}$, $\mathsf{d}^{\ast}$, and $\mathsf{d}_{\ast}$ denote, respectively, the asymptotic density, the upper asymptotic density, and the lower asymptotic density on $\mathbb N$. We show that there are no constants $0<ν<1$ and $c>0$ such that $$ \mathsf{d}_{\ast}(A+A)\ge c\bigl(\mathsf{d}^{\ast}(A+A)\bigr)^{1-ν}\mathsf{d}(A)^ν $$ for every set $A\subseteq \mathbb{N}$ which admits asymptotic density. This answers in the negative a question of I.Z. Ruzsa.

</details>


### [240] [Rogers--Ramanujan identities from the geometry of $X^a=Y^b$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20567)
*Yifeng Huang,Kenny Lau,Ken Ono*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文证明了Huang–Jiang–Oblomkov (HJO) 猜想，建立了复平面曲线奇点 $X^a=Y^b$ 相关的三重重等式：点计数、HJO $q$-级数与显式无穷乘积三者相等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Rogers–Ramanujan与Andrews–Gordon恒等式推广到环面纽结奇点 $X^a=Y^b$（$\gcd(a,b)=1$）的几何情形，验证HJO猜想。

Method: 证明更强的有限恒等式：秩 $N$ 的HJO和等于 $(q;q)_N$ 乘以有界柱面分拆的生成函数。技巧上结合Bergeron–Garsia–Leven–Xin与Mellit的组合有理洗牌定理、斜率算子的乘性定理，以及有界柱面分拆的行列式模型，并用共同的 $q$-差分方程串联。$N\to\infty$ 即得HJO猜想。形式化方面使用Lean由AxiomProver完成。

Result: 建立三重重等式：$\prod_{m\ge1}(1-q^{-m})\sum_{n\ge0}\frac{|\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)|}{|\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb F_q)|}=Z_{a,b}(q^{-1})=P_{a,b}(q^{-1})$。有限恒等式与HJO猜想已在Lean中形式化，依赖两个已陈述的文献输入。

Conclusion: HJO猜想得证；有限秩恒等式是其强化形式，配合组合与行列式工具统一了三类对象，并完成机器形式化验证。

Abstract: We prove the conjecture of Huang, Jiang, and Oblomkov (HJO) giving a geometric extension of the Rogers--Ramanujan and Andrews--Gordon identities for every torus-knot singularity $X^a=Y^b$ with coprime $1<a<b.$ For a prime power $q$, let $\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)$ denote the set of pairs of commuting nilpotent $n\times n$ matrices $(A,B)$ over $\mathbb F_q$ satisfying $A^a=B^b$. We establish the threefold equality between their normalized counts, the HJO $q$-series $Z_{a,b}$, and the explicit infinite product $P_{a,b}$: \[ \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|} \prod_{m\geq1}(1-q^{-m}) \Biggl(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty} \frac{\lvert\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)\rvert} {\lvert\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb F_q)\rvert}\Biggr) }_{\text{point count}} = \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|}Z_{a,b}(q^{-1}) }_{\text{\(q\)-series}} = \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|}P_{a,b}(q^{-1}) }_{\text{infinite product}}. \] Our main result is a stronger finite identity: the rank $N$ HJO sum equals $(q;q)_N$ times the generating function for balanced cylindric partitions with entries bounded by $N$. Taking $N\to\infty$ yields the HJO conjecture. The proof combines the compositional rational shuffle theorem of Bergeron--Garsia--Leven--Xin and Mellit with a multiplicativity theorem for slope operators and a determinantal model for bounded cylindric partitions, linked by a common $q$-difference equation. The finite identity and the HJO conjecture have been formalized in Lean by AxiomProver, conditional on two stated literature inputs.

</details>


### [241] [A characterization of Sophie Germain primes - II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20208)
*Paolo Leonetti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对于偶数 n≥4，存在置换 σ 使得 {1^σ(1), 2^σ(2), …, n^σ(n)} 构成模 n 的完全剩余系，当且仅当 n/2 为素数且 p-1 为无平方因子数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有文献 [Int. J. Number Theory 14 (2018), 653–660] 对奇数情形给出了类似刻画，本文旨在补充偶数情形的对应结论，完善这一问题在奇偶两种情形下的完整分类。

Method: 作者研究偶数 n，考察是否存在置换 σ 使 {1^σ(1), 2^σ(2), …, n^σ(n)} 构成模 n 的完全剩余系，并通过数论条件（p = n/2 为素数且 p-1 无平方因子）刻画这一性质，证明其必要性与充分性。

Result: 对于偶数 n≥4，{1^σ(1), 2^σ(2), …, n^σ(n)} 对某个置换 σ 构成模 n 完全剩余系，当且仅当 p := n/2 是素数且 p-1 是平方自由（squarefree）的。

Conclusion: 该结果与文献中奇数情形的刻画形成互补，给出了偶数情形下此类完全剩余系存在性的充要条件。

Abstract: Let $n\ge 4$ be an even integer. We show that $\{1^{σ(1)},2^{σ(2)},\ldots,n^{σ(n)}\}$ is a complete residue system modulo $n$ for some permutation $σ$ of $\{1,2,\ldots,n\}$ if and only if $p:=n/2$ is prime and $p-1$ is squarefree. This complements the results in [Int. J. Number Theory \textbf{14} (2018), 653--660], where an analogue characterization has been proved for odd integers.

</details>


### [242] [The Three Gates: A Rooted-Operator Approach to Weil Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20367)
*Marco Desogus*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文通过三阶段（Gate I/II/III）的局部化根算子论证，在实奇对数信道中保留极项，结合区间算术、Mellin单元分解、Schur几何与限制奇Weil判据，证明Weil正性并推出Riemann假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实奇对数信道中建立Weil正性，需要保留极秩一项并处理继承父响应、算术失配与折叠标量债务的统一形式，从而为Weil判据到Riemann假设的路径提供严格基础。

Method: 采用局部化根算子论证：Gate I用区间算术建立严格胞腔/壳层符号定理；Gate II组合Mellin单元分解、除子部分等距平方、仿射平移、全形式Cauchy–Carleman传输、相干多源短路、增广标量根及真度量下Schur几何，并将继承父响应置于同一切割原始形式；Gate III使用压缩、闭包与限制奇Weil判据。

Result: 若继承枢轴为P^{ex}_{k,m}、Schur极小元继承坐标为a_m，则P^{ex}_{k,m}a_m=ω^{ex}_{k,m}，继承源耦合贡献为该响应的负度量能量；向外舍入有限计算加解析尾部给出k≥7时g^{M+}_k=1/2+log k−(439/(250 log2))∑_m V_{k,m}−(5/2)W_k>0。共同切割定理分离父基与横向预算，折叠恒等式留下非负余项。由已严格认证端点Y=7出发，Schur归纳给出整数端点正性，零延拓给出任意有限支撑半径非负性，闭包与限制奇Weil判据推出Riemann假设。

Conclusion: 在保留极项与统一原始形式下，三阶段局部化根算子论证建立了Weil正性，并经Schur归纳、零延拓与限制奇Weil判据严格推出Riemann假设；文中的幻想插曲仅为说明性内容，可省略而不影响任何定义、引理、定理或证明。

Abstract: We present a localized rooted-operator argument for Weil positivity in the real odd logarithmic channel, retaining the polar rank-one term throughout. Gate I establishes a strict cellular/shell sign theorem with rigorous interval-arithmetic bounds. Gate II combines the Mellin unit-cell decomposition, divisor partial-isometry squares, affine translations, full-form Cauchy--Carleman transport, coherent multi-source shorting, the augmented scalar root, and Schur geometry in the true metric. Gate III uses compression, closure, and the restricted odd Weil criterion. A central point in Gate II is to place the inherited parent response in the same post-old-core/common-cut primal form in which the arithmetic mismatch and folded scalar debit are charged. If $P^{\rm ex}_{k,m}$ is the surviving inherited pivot and $a_m$ the inherited coordinate of the Schur minimizer, then $P^{\rm ex}_{k,m}a_m=ω^{\rm ex}_{k,m}$, so the inherited source-coupling contribution is the negative metric energy of that response. The aligned forcing is retained explicitly, and an outward-rounded finite computation together with the analytic tail gives $g^{M+}_k=\frac12+\log k-\frac{439}{250\log 2}\sum_m V_{k,m}-\frac52 W_k>0$ for $k\ge7$. The simultaneous common-cut theorem keeps the parent-ground and transverse budgets separate before the common infimum, while the fold identity leaves a non-negative remainder. Starting from the rigorously certified endpoint $Y=7$, Schur induction yields positivity at the integer endpoints, zero-extension gives non-negativity at every finite support radius, and closure together with the restricted odd Weil criterion yields the Riemann hypothesis. Short fantasy interludes provide only an expository map and may be omitted without changing any definition, lemma, theorem, or proof.

</details>


### [243] [A problem of Yang and Chen on weighted representation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20385)
*Shuang-Shuang Li,Ya-Ting Xu,Xiao-Hui Yan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究集合 A 在 n=a1+k a2 表示函数下的互补等价性质计数 f_k(t)，证明其渐近阶为 2^t/t^{k/2}，从而否定 Yang 与 Chen 关于不同 k 的 f_k(t) 最终相等的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yang 与 Chen 定义 f_k(t) 为满足 R_{1,k}(A,n)=R_{1,k}(N\A,n)（对所有 n≥t）的集合 A 的个数，并提出问题：对任意整数 k,l>1，f_k(t) 与 f_l(t) 是否最终相等。本文旨在确定 f_k(t) 的渐近增长阶，以判断该问题。

Method: 通过分析 R_{1,k}(A,n) 的生成函数/幂级数结构，建立 f_k(t) 与满足互补等式的集合 A 的计数之间的对应关系，并使用组合渐近分析（可能涉及生成函数奇点分析或递推估计）来估计 f_k(t) 的增长阶。

Result: 证明了 f_k(t) ≍_k 2^t/t^{k/2}，其中隐含常数依赖于 k。由此，对不同的 k，其增长阶相差 t 的幂次，因此 f_k(t) 与 f_l(t) 不最终相等。

Conclusion: f_k(t) 的渐近阶为 2^t/t^{k/2}，这给出了 Yang 与 Chen 问题的否定答案：存在 k≠l，使 f_k(t) 与 f_l(t) 最终不等。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb N$ denote the set of nonnegative integers. For an integer $k>1$ and a set $A\subseteq\mathbb N$, let $R_{1,k}(A,n)$ denote the number of solutions of $n=a_1+ka_2$ with $a_1,a_2\in A$. For integers $k>1$ and $t\ge1$, Yang and Chen defined $f_k(t)$ to be the number of sets $A\subseteq\mathbb N$ for which \[ R_{1,k}(A,n)=R_{1,k}(\mathbb N\setminus A,n) \] for all integers $n\ge t$, and asked whether $f_k(t)$ and $f_l(t)$ are eventually equal for any integers $k,l>1$. We prove that \[ f_k(t)\asymp_k \frac{2^t}{t^{k/2}}, \] which gives a negative answer to their problem.

</details>


### [244] [Low moments of automorphic random multiplicative function sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20460)
*Sun-Kai Leung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了与有界欧拉乘积相关的随机乘性函数部分和低阶矩的阶，推广了Harper的经典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机乘性函数理论中，部分和低阶矩的阶是一个核心问题。Harper此前的工作解决了特定情形，但对更一般的欧拉乘积（有界次数、满足素平方消去条件的酉局部参数）尚无系统结论，尤其是高秩Sato--Tate等模型。

Method: 在酉局部参数满足素平方消去条件、欧拉乘积次数有界的设定下，分析相应随机乘性函数部分和的低阶矩阶数。方法可应用于酉型或辛型不可约紧群表示，包括高秩Sato--Tate模型 SU(d) 及奇对称幂 Sato--Tate 模型 Sym^m SU(2)。

Result: 确定了该类随机乘性函数部分和低阶矩的精确阶，结果覆盖酉型与辛型不可约紧群表示，以及 SU(d) 和奇对称幂 Sym^m SU(2) 等模型。

Conclusion: 该工作将Harper的结论推广至更一般的欧拉乘积框架，为高秩及对称幂Sato--Tate模型下的随机乘性函数矩估计提供了统一结果。

Abstract: We determine the order of the low moments of partial sums of random multiplicative functions associated with Euler products of bounded degree whose unitary local parameters satisfy a prime-square cancellation condition, thereby generalizing Harper's seminal work. The result applies to irreducible compact-group representations of unitary or symplectic type, including the higher-rank Sato--Tate model $SU(d)$ and the odd symmetric-power Sato--Tate model $\operatorname{Sym}^{m}SU(2)$.

</details>


### [245] [Finding New Limit Points of Mahler Measure by Methods of Missing Data Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20468)
*Jean-Marc Sac-Épée,Souad El Otmani,Armand Maul,Georges Rhin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文借助“缺失数据恢复”方法，进一步扩展了单变量多项式Mahler测度的已知极限点列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单变量多项式Mahler测度的极限点列表最初由D. Boyd和M. Mossinghoff给出，后续通过遗传算法得到扩展，作者希望在此基础上继续补充更多已知极限点。

Method: 采用一种缺失数据恢复（missing data restoration）的方法来扩展已知极限点列表。

Result: 扩展了单变量多项式Mahler测度的已知极限点列表。

Conclusion: 缺失数据恢复方法可有效用于扩充Mahler测度极限点的已知清单，为该列表的进一步完善提供了新途径。

Abstract: It is well known that the set of Mahler measures of single variable polynomial has limit points of which a list established by D. Boyd and M. Mossinghoff has been extended through approaches based on genetic algorithms. In this paper, we wish to further extend the list of known limit points by adapting a method of missing data restoration.

</details>


### [246] [Computational results on sums of a prime with squares or cubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20505)
*Kenny Applegate,Kyle Pratt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文验证了"大于14的整数均可表为一个奇素数加两个正平方数之和"这一猜想在 14<n<10^26 范围内成立，并证明 308 到 10^16 之间所有整数可表为奇素数加两个正立方数之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围绕"整数表为奇素数加两个正平方数之和"的猜想展开，针对该猜想的有限范围验证需求，同时探索类似形式的"素数加两个正立方数"问题。

Method: 先研究素数与单个平方数之和的表示，再通过"bootstrap"方法推广到素数与两个平方数之和；对于立方数情形采用类似思路。过程中需要确定若干椭圆曲线上的整点。

Result: 证明了所有满足 14<n<10^26 的整数都可表为一个奇素数加两个正平方数之和；并证明 308 到 10^16 之间所有整数可表为奇素数加两个正立方数之和。

Conclusion: 在给定的大范围内证实了素数加两个正平方数之和的猜想，并将方法推广至素数加两个正立方数的情形，依赖于椭圆曲线整点的计算。

Abstract: One conjectures that all integers greater than 14 are the sum of an odd prime and two positive squares. We prove that all integers $n$ with $14<n<10^{26}$ can be represented in this manner. Along the way, we investigate sums of a prime and one square and then ``bootstrap'' to get our results on sums of a prime and two squares. We also show that all integers between $308$ and $10^{16}$ can be represented as the sum of an odd prime and two positive cubes. Our methods require the determination of the integral points on various elliptic curves.

</details>


### [247] [An ergodic approach to equations of the form $x+y=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20727)
*Vitaly Bergelson,Hao Pan,Saúl Rodríguez Martín*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入遍历论方法研究形如 x+y=⌊α(n)⌋ 的加性方程解的存在性，其中 α 为多项式增长的正则函数，A 为正上密度集或有限分划的胞元；完整刻画了使分划问题与密度问题可解的多项式增长 Hardy 域函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Erdős、Sárközy 与 Sós (1989) 以及 Khalfalah 与 Szemerédi (2006) 的问题与结果启发，作者希望系统处理 x+y=⌊α(n)⌋ 型加性方程在密度集与分划胞元中的可解性，并给出 α 的完整刻画。

Method: 引入遍历论（ergodic）方法，处理 α 为足够正则函数（如多项式或多项式增长的 logarithmico-exponential 函数，例如 α(t)=t^{5/2}+t²log t）的情形；方法可推广至 x∈A₁, y∈A₂ 的双密度集问题，进而处理 kx+ly=⌊α(n)⌋。

Result: 完整刻画了多项式增长的 Hardy 域函数 α，使得相应的分划问题与密度问题始终可解；同时将结论推广到两个正密度集的交叉情形以及 kx+ly=⌊α(n)⌋ 型方程；部分结果在 A 为具有正相对上密度的素数子集时也成立。

Conclusion: 遍历论方法为 Erdős–Sárközy–Sós 与 Khalfalah–Szemerédi 类型的加性方程问题提供了统一框架，并给出多项式增长 Hardy 域函数可解性的完整分类，兼具向双集合、线性组合及素数子集的推广能力。

Abstract: Motivated by questions and results of Erdős, Sárközy and Sós (1989), and Khalfalah and Szemerédi (2006), we introduce an ergodic approach to additive problems concerning solutions of equations of the form \[ x+y=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor,\quad x,y\in A,n\in\mathbb{N} \] where $α(t)$ is a sufficiently regular function, such as a polynomial or a logarithmico-exponential function of polynomial growth (e.g. $α(t)=t^{5/2}+t^2\log t$), and $A$ is either a given set of positive upper density, or a cell of a prescribed finite partition of $\mathbb{N}$. We give a complete description of the Hardy field functions of polynomial growth for which these partition and density problems are always solvable.
  The ergodic approach also applies to the more general problem of finding $x\in A_1,y\in A_2$, where $A_1,A_2$ are two given sets of positive density. This in turn allows us to treat equations of the form $kx+ly=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor$, where $k,l\in\mathbb{Z}$.
  Some of our results also hold true when $A$ is a subset of the primes with positive relative upper density.

</details>
